```bpa
                [by exists_elim with-quotient-k]
            }
          @e-divides-implies-neg-e-divides |
            does_divide(e, n) -> does_divide(neg(e), n)
            [by implies_intro given-e-divides-n]
        }
      @discharge-n |
        forall n: Int; does_divide(e, n) -> does_divide(neg(e), n)
        [by forall_intro generalize-n]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall e, n: Int; does_divide(e, n) -> does_divide(neg(e), n)
    [by forall_intro generalize-e]
qed
```

A negative integer negates to a positive one — exactly `negativeNegIsPositive`,
proved above for the Bézout nonemptiness argument (`negLessThanZero` gives
`neg(e) ≥ 0`, and `neg(e) ≠ 0` since that would force `e = 0`, so `nonnegCases`
exhibits it as a successor). We reuse it here under a name local to the coprimality
argument.

```bpa
theorem positiveOfNegative = negativeNegIsPositive
```

Euclid's Lemma proceeds by cases on whether `p | a`, and `ℤ`'s intuitionistic
kernel offers no free excluded middle — so we first establish that divisibility
by a positive integer *is decidable*. Run the Division Algorithm: `a = pq + r`
with `0 ≤ r < p`. By trichotomy the remainder `r` is `< 0` (impossible, `r` is
nonnegative), `= 0` (then `a = p·q`, so `p | a`), or `> 0`. In the last case,
were `p | a` we would have a second decomposition `a = p·m + 0`; the Division
Algorithm's *uniqueness* forces `r = 0`, contradicting `r > 0` — so `p ∤ a`.

```bpa
theorem dividesDecidable: forall p, a: Int; less_than(ZERO, p) -> (does_divide(p, a) or (not does_divide(p, a)))
proof
  @generalize-p |
    fix p: Int {
      @generalize-a |
        fix a: Int {
          @given-p-positive |
            assume less_than(ZERO, p) {
              @p-positive |
                less_than(ZERO, p)
                [by hypothesis given-p-positive]
              @quotient-remainder-exist |
                exists q: Int; exists r: Int; a = add(mul(p, q), r) and is_nonneg(r) and less_than(r, p)
                [using specialize divisionAlgorithmExists(a, p) p-positive]
              @with-quotient-q |
                unpack q: Int from quotient-remainder-exist {
                  @remainder-exists |
                    exists r: Int; a = add(mul(p, q), r) and is_nonneg(r) and less_than(r, p)
                    [by hypothesis with-quotient-q]
                  @with-remainder-r |
                    unpack r: Int from remainder-exists {
                      @decomposition-and-bounds |
                        a = add(mul(p, q), r) and is_nonneg(r) and less_than(r, p)
                        [by hypothesis with-remainder-r]
                      @decomposition-and-remainder-nonneg |
                        a = add(mul(p, q), r) and is_nonneg(r)
                        [by and_elim_left decomposition-and-bounds]
                      @decomposition |
                        a = add(mul(p, q), r)
                        [by and_elim_left decomposition-and-remainder-nonneg]
                      @remainder-nonneg |
                        is_nonneg(r)
                        [by and_elim_right decomposition-and-remainder-nonneg]
                      @remainder-less_than-p |
                        less_than(r, p)
                        [by and_elim_right decomposition-and-bounds]
                      @r-against-zero |
                        less_than(r, ZERO) or r = ZERO or less_than(ZERO, r)
                        [using specialize trichotomy(r, ZERO)]
                      @divides-decidable |
                        does_divide(p, a) or (not does_divide(p, a))
                        case r-against-zero {
                          @when-remainder-less_than-zero |
                            assume less_than(r, ZERO) {
                              @remainder-less_than-zero |
                                less_than(r, ZERO)
                                [by hypothesis when-remainder-less_than-zero]
                              @remainder-not-less_than-zero |
                                not less_than(r, ZERO)
                                [using specialize nonnegNotLessThanZero(r) remainder-nonneg]
                              @divides-decidable |
                                does_divide(p, a) or (not does_divide(p, a))
                                [by absurd remainder-less_than-zero remainder-not-less_than-zero]
                            }
                          @when-remainder-zero |
                            assume r = ZERO {
                              @remainder-zero |
                                r = ZERO
                                [by hypothesis when-remainder-zero]
                              @add-zero-right |
                                forall n: Int; add(n, ZERO) = n
                                [by cite addZeroRight]
                              @product-plus-zero |
                                add(mul(p, q), ZERO) = mul(p, q)
                                [by forall_elim(mul(p, q)) add-zero-right]
                              @a-is-p-times-q |
                                a = mul(p, q)
                                [using chain decomposition remainder-zero product-plus-zero]
                              @p-divides-a-if-product |
                                a = mul(p, q) -> does_divide(p, a)
                                [using specialize dividesIntro(a, p, q)]
                              @p-divides-a |
                                does_divide(p, a)
                                [by modus_ponens p-divides-a-if-product a-is-p-times-q]
                              @divides-decidable |
                                does_divide(p, a) or (not does_divide(p, a))
                                [by or_intro_left p-divides-a]
                            }
                          @when-remainder-positive |
                            assume less_than(ZERO, r) {
                              @remainder-positive |
                                less_than(ZERO, r)
                                [by hypothesis when-remainder-positive]
                              @given-p-divides-a |
                                assume does_divide(p, a) {
                                  @p-divides-a |
                                    does_divide(p, a)
                                    [by hypothesis given-p-divides-a]
                                  @exact-quotient-exists |
                                    exists k: Int; a = mul(p, k)
                                    [using specialize dividesElim(p, a) p-divides-a]
                                  @with-exact-quotient-m |
                                    unpack m: Int from exact-quotient-exists {
                                      @a-is-p-times-m |
                                        a = mul(p, m)
                                        [by hypothesis with-exact-quotient-m]
                                      @add-zero-right |
                                        forall n: Int; add(n, ZERO) = n
                                        [by cite addZeroRight]
                                      @product-m-plus-zero |
                                        add(mul(p, m), ZERO) = mul(p, m)
                                        [by forall_elim(mul(p, m)) add-zero-right]
                                      @a-is-p-times-m-plus-zero |
                                        a = add(mul(p, m), ZERO)
                                        [using chain a-is-p-times-m product-m-plus-zero]
                                      @zero-is-nonneg |
                                        is_nonneg(ZERO)
                                        [by cite nonnegZero]
                                      @uniqueness |
                                        forall x, y, q1, r1, q2, r2: Int; less_than(ZERO, y) -> x = add(mul(y, q1), r1) -> is_nonneg(r1) -> less_than(r1, y) -> x = add(mul(y, q2), r2) -> is_nonneg(r2) -> less_than(r2, y) -> (q1 = q2 and r1 = r2)
                                        [by cite divisionAlgorithmUnique]
                                      @uniqueness-for-a |
                                        less_than(ZERO, p) -> a = add(mul(p, q), r) -> is_nonneg(r) -> less_than(r, p) -> a = add(mul(p, m), ZERO) -> is_nonneg(ZERO) -> less_than(ZERO, p) -> (q = m and r = ZERO)
                                        [by forall_elim(a, p, q, r, m, ZERO) uniqueness]
                                      @quotient-and-remainder-agree |
                                        q = m and r = ZERO
                                        [using tautology uniqueness-for-a p-positive decomposition remainder-nonneg remainder-less_than-p a-is-p-times-m-plus-zero zero-is-nonneg]
                                      @remainder-is-zero |
                                        r = ZERO
                                        [by and_elim_right quotient-and-remainder-agree]
                                      @zero-less_than-zero |
                                        less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                        [by rewrite remainder-is-zero remainder-positive]
                                      @zero-not-less_than-zero |
                                        not less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                        [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(ZERO)]
                                      @contradiction |
                                        not does_divide(p, a)
                                        [by absurd zero-less_than-zero zero-not-less_than-zero]
                                    }
                                  @p-does-not-divide-a-inner |
                                    not does_divide(p, a)
                                    [by exists_elim with-exact-quotient-m]
                                }
                              @p-does-not-divide-a |
                                not does_divide(p, a)
                                [by not_intro given-p-divides-a p-divides-a p-does-not-divide-a-inner]
                              @divides-decidable |
                                does_divide(p, a) or (not does_divide(p, a))
                                [by or_intro_right p-does-not-divide-a]
                            }
                        }
                    }
                  @divides-decidable |
                    does_divide(p, a) or (not does_divide(p, a))
                    [by exists_elim with-remainder-r]
                }
              @divides-decidable |
                does_divide(p, a) or (not does_divide(p, a))
                [by exists_elim with-quotient-q]
            }
          @positive-p-gives-decidable |
            less_than(ZERO, p) -> (does_divide(p, a) or (not does_divide(p, a)))
            [by implies_intro given-p-positive]
        }
      @discharge-a |
        forall a: Int; less_than(ZERO, p) -> (does_divide(p, a) or (not does_divide(p, a)))
        [by forall_intro generalize-a]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall p, a: Int; less_than(ZERO, p) -> (does_divide(p, a) or (not does_divide(p, a)))
    [by forall_intro generalize-p]
qed
```

The bridge from primality to Bézout's coprimality hypothesis. Judson takes
`gcd(a, p) = 1` for granted when `p ∤ a`; over `ℤ` the common divisors come with
signs, so we prove it: any common divisor `e` of `a` and `p` is `±1`. Trichotomy
splits `e`. If `e > 0`, primality gives `e = 1` or `e = p`, and `e = p` would
make `p | a` (via `e | a`), against the hypothesis — so `e = 1`. If `e < 0`,
`neg(e) > 0` divides both (`negDivides`), so the same argument gives `neg(e) = 1`,
i.e. `e = -1`. And `e = 0` is impossible, since `0 | p` forces `p = 0 < p`.

```bpa
theorem coprimalityFromPrime: forall p, a: Int;
  less_than(ZERO, p)
  -> (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))
  -> (not does_divide(p, a))
  -> (forall e: Int; does_divide(e, a) -> does_divide(e, p) -> (e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)))
proof
  @generalize-p |
    fix p: Int {
      @generalize-a |
        fix a: Int {
          @given-p-positive |
            assume less_than(ZERO, p) {
              @given-p-prime |
                assume forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p) {
                  @given-p-not-divide-a |
                    assume not does_divide(p, a) {
                      @p-positive |
                        less_than(ZERO, p)
                        [by hypothesis given-p-positive]
                      @only-positive-divisors-are-one-and-p |
                        forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)
                        [by hypothesis given-p-prime]
                      @p-does-not-divide-a |
                        not does_divide(p, a)
                        [by hypothesis given-p-not-divide-a]
                      @common-divisors-are-units |
                        fix e: Int {
                          @given-e-divides-a |
                            assume does_divide(e, a) {
                              @given-e-divides-p |
                                assume does_divide(e, p) {
                                  @e-divides-a |
                                    does_divide(e, a)
                                    [by hypothesis given-e-divides-a]
                                  @e-divides-p |
                                    does_divide(e, p)
                                    [by hypothesis given-e-divides-p]
                                  @e-against-zero |
                                    less_than(e, ZERO) or e = ZERO or less_than(ZERO, e)
                                    [using specialize trichotomy(e, ZERO)]
                                  @e-is-unit |
                                    e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)
                                    case e-against-zero {
                                      @when-e-negative |
                                        assume less_than(e, ZERO) {
                                          @e-negative |
                                            less_than(e, ZERO)
                                            [by hypothesis when-e-negative]
                                          @neg-e-is-positive |
                                            less_than(ZERO, neg(e))
                                            [using specialize positiveOfNegative(e) e-negative]
                                          @neg-e-divides-p |
                                            does_divide(neg(e), p)
                                            [using specialize negDivides(e, p) e-divides-p]
                                          @neg-e-divides-a |
                                            does_divide(neg(e), a)
                                            [using specialize negDivides(e, a) e-divides-a]
                                          @primality-at-neg-e |
                                            less_than(ZERO, neg(e)) -> does_divide(neg(e), p) -> (neg(e) = ONE or neg(e) = p)
                                            [by forall_elim(neg(e)) only-positive-divisors-are-one-and-p]
                                          @neg-e-is-one-or-p |
                                            neg(e) = ONE or neg(e) = p
                                            [using tautology primality-at-neg-e neg-e-is-positive neg-e-divides-p]
                                          @e-is-unit |
                                            e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)
                                            case neg-e-is-one-or-p {
                                              @when-neg-e-is-one |
                                                assume neg(e) = ONE {
                                                  // neg(e)=1 => e = neg(neg(e)) = neg(1).
                                                  @neg-e-is-one |
                                                    neg(e) = ONE
                                                    [by hypothesis when-neg-e-is-one]
                                                  @e-is-neg-one |
                                                    e = neg(ONE)
                                                    [using arithmetic neg-e-is-one]
                                                  @e-is-unit |
                                                    e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)
                                                    [by or_intro_right e-is-neg-one]
                                                }
                                              @when-neg-e-is-p |
                                                assume neg(e) = p {
                                                  // neg(e)=p and neg(e)|a => p|a, contradiction.
                                                  @neg-e-is-p |
                                                    neg(e) = p
                                                    [by hypothesis when-neg-e-is-p]
                                                  @p-divides-a |
                                                    does_divide(p, a)
                                                    [by rewrite neg-e-is-p neg-e-divides-a]
                                                  @e-is-unit |
                                                    e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)
                                                    [by absurd p-divides-a p-does-not-divide-a]
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                      @when-e-zero |
                                        assume e = ZERO {
                                          // e=0 and e|p => p = 0*k = 0, but p>0 is irreflexive-absurd.
                                          @e-is-zero |
                                            e = ZERO
                                            [by hypothesis when-e-zero]
                                          @zero-divides-p |
                                            does_divide(ZERO, p)
                                            [by rewrite e-is-zero e-divides-p]
                                          @zero-quotient-exists |
                                            exists k: Int; p = mul(ZERO, k)
                                            [using specialize dividesElim(ZERO, p) zero-divides-p]
                                          @with-zero-quotient-k |
                                            unpack k: Int from zero-quotient-exists {
                                              @p-is-zero-times-k |
                                                p = mul(ZERO, k)
                                                [by hypothesis with-zero-quotient-k]
                                              @p-is-zero |
                                                p = ZERO
                                                [using arithmetic p-is-zero-times-k]
                                              @zero-is-positive |
                                                less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                                [by rewrite p-is-zero p-positive]
                                              @zero-not-less_than-zero |
                                                not less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                                [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(ZERO)]
                                              @e-is-unit |
                                                e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)
                                                [by absurd zero-is-positive zero-not-less_than-zero]
                                            }
                                          @e-is-unit |
                                            e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)
                                            [by exists_elim with-zero-quotient-k]
                                        }
                                      @when-e-positive |
                                        assume less_than(ZERO, e) {
                                          @e-positive |
                                            less_than(ZERO, e)
                                            [by hypothesis when-e-positive]
                                          @primality-at-e |
                                            less_than(ZERO, e) -> does_divide(e, p) -> (e = ONE or e = p)
                                            [by forall_elim(e) only-positive-divisors-are-one-and-p]
                                          @e-is-one-or-p |
                                            e = ONE or e = p
                                            [using tautology primality-at-e e-positive e-divides-p]
                                          @e-is-unit |
                                            e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)
                                            case e-is-one-or-p {
                                              @when-e-is-one |
                                                assume e = ONE {
                                                  @e-is-one |
                                                    e = ONE
                                                    [by hypothesis when-e-is-one]
                                                  @e-is-unit |
                                                    e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)
                                                    [by or_intro_left e-is-one]
                                                }
                                              @when-e-is-p |
                                                assume e = p {
                                                  @e-is-p |
                                                    e = p
                                                    [by hypothesis when-e-is-p]
                                                  @p-divides-a |
                                                    does_divide(p, a)
                                                    [by rewrite e-is-p e-divides-a]
                                                  @e-is-unit |
                                                    e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)
                                                    [by absurd p-divides-a p-does-not-divide-a]
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                              @e-divides-p-gives-unit |
                                does_divide(e, p) -> (e = ONE or e = neg(ONE))
                                [by implies_intro given-e-divides-p]
                            }
                          @e-divides-a-gives-unit |
                            does_divide(e, a) -> does_divide(e, p) -> (e = ONE or e = neg(ONE))
                            [by implies_intro given-e-divides-a]
                        }
                      @common-divisors-are-units-for-all |
                        forall e: Int; does_divide(e, a) -> does_divide(e, p) -> (e = ONE or e = neg(ONE))
                        [by forall_intro common-divisors-are-units]
                    }
                  @not-divide-gives-coprime |
                    (not does_divide(p, a))
                      -> (forall e: Int; does_divide(e, a) -> does_divide(e, p) -> (e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)))
                    [by implies_intro given-p-not-divide-a]
                }
              @prime-gives-coprime |
                (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))
                  -> (not does_divide(p, a))
                  -> (forall e: Int; does_divide(e, a) -> does_divide(e, p) -> (e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)))
                [by implies_intro given-p-prime]
            }
          @positive-p-gives-coprime |
            less_than(ZERO, p)
              -> (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))
              -> (not does_divide(p, a))
              -> (forall e: Int; does_divide(e, a) -> does_divide(e, p) -> (e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)))
            [by implies_intro given-p-positive]
        }
      @discharge-a |
        forall a: Int; less_than(ZERO, p)
          -> (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))
          -> (not does_divide(p, a))
          -> (forall e: Int; does_divide(e, a) -> does_divide(e, p) -> (e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)))
        [by forall_intro generalize-a]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall p, a: Int; less_than(ZERO, p)
      -> (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))
      -> (not does_divide(p, a))
      -> (forall e: Int; does_divide(e, a) -> does_divide(e, p) -> (e = ONE or e = neg(ONE)))
    [by forall_intro generalize-p]
qed
```

**Euclid's Lemma** (Judson, Lemma 2.11). Suppose the prime `p` divides `a·b`.
If `p | a` we are done; otherwise `p ∤ a`, so `a` and `p` are coprime and
`coprimeBezout` yields `r, s` with `a·r + p·s = 1` (the hypotheses `a ≠ 0` and
`p ≠ 0` come from `p ∤ a` — `p | 0` always holds — and from `p > 0`). Multiplying
by `b`, `b = b·1 = b·(a·r + p·s) = (a·b)·r + p·(b·s)`, the last identity the only
elided ring algebra (discharged by `polynomial`). Now `p | (a·b)·r` because
`p | a·b`, and `p | p·(b·s)` because `p | p`; by `dividesAdd` their sum, which is
`b`, is divisible by `p`. So `p | b`.

```bpa
theorem euclidsLemma: forall a, b, p: Int;
  (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
  -> does_divide(p, mul(a, b))
  -> (does_divide(p, a) or does_divide(p, b))
proof
  @generalize-a |
    fix a: Int {
      @generalize-b |
        fix b: Int {
          @generalize-p |
            fix p: Int {
              @given-p-prime |
                assume less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)) {
                  @given-p-divides-product |
                    assume does_divide(p, mul(a, b)) {
                      @prime-conjunction |
                        less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))
                        [by hypothesis given-p-prime]
                      @one-less_than-p |
                        less_than(ONE, p)
                        [by and_elim_left prime-conjunction]
                      @only-positive-divisors-are-one-and-p |
                        forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)
                        [by and_elim_right prime-conjunction]
                      @p-divides-product |
                        does_divide(p, mul(a, b))
                        [by hypothesis given-p-divides-product]
                      // p > 0, from 0 < 1 < p.
                      @zero-less_than-one |
                        less_than(ZERO, ONE)
                        [by cite zeroLessThanOne]
                      @zero-less_than-p-if-chained |
                        less_than(ZERO, ONE) -> less_than(ONE, p) -> less_than(ZERO, p)
                        [using specialize lessThanTransitive(ZERO, ONE, p)]
                      @p-positive |
                        less_than(ZERO, p)
                        [using tautology zero-less_than-p-if-chained zero-less_than-one one-less_than-p]
                      // decide whether p | a.
                      @p-divides-a-or-not |
                        does_divide(p, a) or (not does_divide(p, a))
                        [using specialize dividesDecidable(p, a) p-positive]
                      @p-divides-a-or-b |
                        does_divide(p, a) or does_divide(p, b)
                        case p-divides-a-or-not {
                          @when-p-divides-a |
                            assume does_divide(p, a) {
                              @p-divides-a |
                                does_divide(p, a)
                                [by hypothesis when-p-divides-a]
                              @p-divides-a-or-b |
                                does_divide(p, a) or does_divide(p, b)
                                [by or_intro_left p-divides-a]
                            }
                          @when-p-does-not-divide-a |
                            assume not does_divide(p, a) {
                              @p-does-not-divide-a |
                                not does_divide(p, a)
                                [by hypothesis when-p-does-not-divide-a]
                              // a != 0: else p | 0 = a, against the branch.
                              @given-a-zero |
                                assume a = ZERO {
                                  @a-is-zero |
                                    a = ZERO
                                    [by hypothesis given-a-zero]
                                  @zero-is-p-times-zero |
                                    ZERO = mul(p, ZERO)
                                    [using simplify mulZeroRight]
                                  @a-is-p-times-zero |
                                    a = mul(p, ZERO)
                                    [by rewrite zero-is-p-times-zero a-is-zero]
                                  @p-divides-a-if-product |
                                    a = mul(p, ZERO) -> does_divide(p, a)
                                    [using specialize dividesIntro(a, p, ZERO)]
                                  @p-divides-a |
                                    does_divide(p, a)
                                    [by modus_ponens p-divides-a-if-product a-is-p-times-zero]
                                  @a-not-zero-inner |
                                    a != ZERO
                                    [by absurd p-divides-a p-does-not-divide-a]
                                }
                              @a-not-zero |
                                a != ZERO
                                [by not_intro given-a-zero a-is-zero a-not-zero-inner]
                              // p != 0, from p > 0.
                              @given-p-zero |
                                assume p = ZERO {
                                  @p-is-zero |
                                    p = ZERO
                                    [by hypothesis given-p-zero]
                                  @zero-less_than-zero |
                                    less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                    [by rewrite p-is-zero p-positive]
                                  @zero-not-less_than-zero |
                                    not less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                    [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(ZERO)]
                                  @p-not-zero-inner |
                                    p != ZERO
                                    [by absurd zero-less_than-zero zero-not-less_than-zero]
                                }
                              @p-not-zero |
                                p != ZERO
                                [by not_intro given-p-zero p-is-zero p-not-zero-inner]
                              // a and p are coprime.
                              @a-and-p-coprime |
                                forall e: Int; does_divide(e, a) -> does_divide(e, p) -> (e = ONE or e = neg(ONE))
                                [using specialize coprimalityFromPrime(p, a) p-positive only-positive-divisors-are-one-and-p p-does-not-divide-a]
                              // Bezout: a*r + p*s = 1.
                              @bezout-coefficients-exist |
                                exists r: Int; exists s: Int; add(mul(a, r), mul(p, s)) = ONE
                                [using specialize coprimeBezout(a, p) a-not-zero p-not-zero a-and-p-coprime]
                              @with-bezout-r |
                                unpack r: Int from bezout-coefficients-exist {
                                  @bezout-s-exists |
                                    exists s: Int; add(mul(a, r), mul(p, s)) = ONE
                                    [by hypothesis with-bezout-r]
                                  @with-bezout-s |
                                    unpack s: Int from bezout-s-exists {
                                      @bezout-holds |
                                        add(mul(a, r), mul(p, s)) = ONE
                                        [by hypothesis with-bezout-s]
                                      // b = b*1 = b*(a*r + p*s) = (a*b)*r + p*(b*s).
                                      @b-times-one-is-b |
                                        mul(b, ONE) = b
                                        [using specialize mulOneRight(b)]
                                      @product-distributes |
                                        mul(b, add(mul(a, r), mul(p, s))) = add(mul(mul(a, b), r), mul(p, mul(b, s)))
                                        [using polynomial]
                                      @b-splits-into-product-and-p-multiple |
                                        b = add(mul(mul(a, b), r), mul(p, mul(b, s)))
                                        [using chain b-times-one-is-b bezout-holds product-distributes]
                                      // p | (a*b)*r, since p | a*b.
                                      @divides-mul |
                                        forall d, x, c: Int; does_divide(d, x) -> does_divide(d, mul(x, c))
                                        [by cite dividesMul]
                                      @p-divides-product-times-r-if |
                                        does_divide(p, mul(a, b)) -> does_divide(p, mul(mul(a, b), r))
                                        [by forall_elim(p, mul(a, b), r) divides-mul]
                                      @p-divides-product-times-r |
                                        does_divide(p, mul(mul(a, b), r))
                                        [by modus_ponens p-divides-product-times-r-if p-divides-product]
                                      // p | p*(b*s), since p | p.
                                      @p-divides-p |
                                        does_divide(p, p)
                                        [using specialize dividesRefl(p)]
                                      @p-divides-p-times-bs-if |
                                        does_divide(p, p) -> does_divide(p, mul(p, mul(b, s)))
                                        [by forall_elim(p, p, mul(b, s)) divides-mul]
                                      @p-divides-p-times-bs |
                                        does_divide(p, mul(p, mul(b, s)))
                                        [by modus_ponens p-divides-p-times-bs-if p-divides-p]
                                      // p | their sum, which is b.
                                      @divides-add |
                                        forall d, x, y: Int; does_divide(d, x) -> does_divide(d, y) -> does_divide(d, add(x, y))
                                        [by cite dividesAdd]
                                      @p-divides-sum-if |
                                        does_divide(p, mul(mul(a, b), r)) -> does_divide(p, mul(p, mul(b, s))) -> does_divide(p, add(mul(mul(a, b), r), mul(p, mul(b, s))))
                                        [by forall_elim(p, mul(mul(a, b), r), mul(p, mul(b, s))) divides-add]
                                      @p-divides-sum |
                                        does_divide(p, add(mul(mul(a, b), r), mul(p, mul(b, s))))
                                        [using tautology p-divides-sum-if p-divides-product-times-r p-divides-p-times-bs]
                                      @p-divides-b |
                                        does_divide(p, b)
                                        [by rewrite b-splits-into-product-and-p-multiple p-divides-sum]
                                      @p-divides-a-or-b |
                                        does_divide(p, a) or does_divide(p, b)
                                        [by or_intro_right p-divides-b]
                                    }
                                  @p-divides-a-or-b |
                                    does_divide(p, a) or does_divide(p, b)
                                    [by exists_elim with-bezout-s]
                                }
                              @p-divides-a-or-b |
                                does_divide(p, a) or does_divide(p, b)
                                [by exists_elim with-bezout-r]
                            }
                        }
                    }
                  @product-implies-divides-factor |
                    does_divide(p, mul(a, b)) -> (does_divide(p, a) or does_divide(p, b))
                    [by implies_intro given-p-divides-product]
                }
              @prime-implies-divides-factor |
                (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
                  -> does_divide(p, mul(a, b)) -> (does_divide(p, a) or does_divide(p, b))
                [by implies_intro given-p-prime]
            }
          @discharge-p |
            forall p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) -> does_divide(p, mul(a, b)) -> (does_divide(p, a) or does_divide(p, b))
            [by forall_intro generalize-p]
        }
      @discharge-b |
        forall b, p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) -> does_divide(p, mul(a, b)) -> (does_divide(p, a) or does_divide(p, b))
        [by forall_intro generalize-b]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall a, b, p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) -> does_divide(p, mul(a, b)) -> (does_divide(p, a) or does_divide(p, b))
    [by forall_intro generalize-a]
qed
```

Euclid's Lemma lifts by induction on the length `k` to a whole finite product:
a prime dividing `productUpTo(s, k)` must divide one of the first `k` entries.
At `k = 0` the product is `1`, which no prime divides (`divisorAtMost` would
force `p ≤ 1`); at `k+1` the product is `productUpTo(s, k) · at(s, k)`, and
Euclid's Lemma sends `p` into the left factor (induction hypothesis: an entry
less_than `k`) or the right one (the entry at `k`). This is the crux of
factorization uniqueness. (`lessThanSuccSelf`: `k < k+1`, the bound-lifting step.)

```bpa
// k < succ(k): the successor of a value strictly exceeds it. The gap is exactly
// ONE (= succ(ZERO)), which is nonneg, so lessThanIntro applies.
theorem lessThanSuccSelf: forall k: Int; less_than(k, succ(k))
proof
  @generalize-k |
    fix k: Int {
      @zero-is-nonneg |
        is_nonneg(ZERO)
        [by cite nonnegZero]
      @gap-of-one-reaches-successor |
        add(k, succ(ZERO)) = succ(k)
        [using simplify addSuccRight addZeroRight]
      @less_than-intro-at-k-zero-succ-k |
        forall a, d, b: Int; is_nonneg(d) -> add(a, succ(d)) = b -> less_than(a, b)
        [by cite lessThanIntro]
      @less_than-intro-specialized |
        is_nonneg(ZERO) -> add(k, succ(ZERO)) = succ(k) -> less_than(k, succ(k))
        [by forall_elim(k, ZERO, succ(k)) less_than-intro-at-k-zero-succ-k]
      @k-less_than-succ-k |
        less_than(k, succ(k))
        [using tautology less_than-intro-specialized zero-is-nonneg gap-of-one-reaches-successor]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall k: Int; less_than(k, succ(k))
    [by forall_intro generalize-k]
qed

// FTA-uniqueness crux: a prime dividing a length-k product divides one of the
// first k entries. Induction on k via nonnegInduction. Base (k=0): the product
// is ONE, and no p>1 divides ONE (divisorAtMost forces p<=1, contradicting p>1),
// so the goal holds vacuously by absurdity. Step: productUpTo(s,succ(k)) factors
// as productUpTo(s,k)*at(s,k); Euclid's lemma splits the divisibility, and either
// the inductive hypothesis supplies an earlier witness i<k (still < succ(k)) or
// p divides the new last entry at(s,k) (witness i=k).
theorem primeDividesProductImpliesMember: forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) ->
  forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
  (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
  -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
  -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
proof
  // base case: k = 0 — the empty product is ONE, which no prime int_divides.
  @base-case-proof |
    assume is_nonneg(ZERO) {
      @generalize-s-base |
        fix s: Seq {
          @generalize-p-base |
            fix p: Int {
              @given-prime-base |
                assume less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)) {
                  @given-p-divides-empty-product |
                    assume does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, ZERO)) {
                      @prime-p-base |
                        less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))
                        [by hypothesis given-prime-base]
                      @one-is-less_than-p |
                        less_than(ONE, p)
                        [using tautology prime-p-base]
                      @empty-product-is-one |
                        productUpTo(s, ZERO) = ONE
                        [using specialize productUpToZero(s)]
                      @p-divides-empty-product |
                        does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, ZERO))
                        [by hypothesis given-p-divides-empty-product]
                      @p-divides-one |
                        does_divide(p, ONE)
                        [by rewrite empty-product-is-one p-divides-empty-product]
                      @zero-less_than-one |
                        less_than(ZERO, ONE)
                        [by cite zeroLessThanOne]
                      @zero-less_than-one-less_than-p |
                        less_than(ZERO, ONE) -> less_than(ONE, p) -> less_than(ZERO, p)
                        [using specialize lessThanTransitive(ZERO, ONE, p)]
                      @zero-less_than-p |
                        less_than(ZERO, p)
                        [using tautology zero-less_than-one-less_than-p zero-less_than-one one-is-less_than-p]
                      @p-at-most-one-conditions |
                        less_than(ZERO, p) -> less_than(ZERO, ONE) -> does_divide(p, ONE) -> at_most(p, ONE)
                        [using specialize divisorAtMost(p, ONE)]
                      @p-at-most-one |
                        at_most(p, ONE)
                        [using tautology p-at-most-one-conditions zero-less_than-p zero-less_than-one p-divides-one]
                      @p-less_than-one-or-equals-one-conditions |
                        at_most(p, ONE) -> (less_than(p, ONE) or p = ONE)
                        [using specialize lessOrEqualSplit(p, ONE)]
                      @p-less_than-one-or-equals-one |
                        less_than(p, ONE) or p = ONE
                        [by modus_ponens p-less_than-one-or-equals-one-conditions p-at-most-one]
                      // both branches of at_most(p, ONE) contradict less_than(ONE, p).
                      @one-not-less_than-one |
                        not less_than(ONE, ONE)
                        [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(ONE)]
                      @when-p-less_than-one |
                        assume less_than(p, ONE) {
                          @p-less_than-one |
                            less_than(p, ONE)
                            [by hypothesis when-p-less_than-one]
                          @one-less_than-p-less_than-one |
                            less_than(ONE, p) -> less_than(p, ONE) -> less_than(ONE, ONE)
                            [using specialize lessThanTransitive(ONE, p, ONE)]
                          @one-less_than-one |
                            less_than(ONE, ONE)
                            [using tautology one-less_than-p-less_than-one one-is-less_than-p p-less_than-one]
                          @conclusion-empty-witness |
                            exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, ZERO) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))
                            [by absurd one-less_than-one one-not-less_than-one]
                        }
                      @when-p-equals-one |
                        assume p = ONE {
                          @p-equals-one |
                            p = ONE
                            [by hypothesis when-p-equals-one]
                          @one-less_than-one-from-p |
                            less_than(ONE, ONE)
                            [by rewrite p-equals-one one-is-less_than-p]
                          @conclusion-empty-witness |
                            exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, ZERO) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))
                            [by absurd one-less_than-one-from-p one-not-less_than-one]
                        }
                      @some-entry-less_than-zero-divides |
                        exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, ZERO) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))
                        [by or_elim p-less_than-one-or-equals-one when-p-less_than-one when-p-equals-one]
                    }
                  @p-divides-empty-gives-witness |
                    does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, ZERO))
                      -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, ZERO) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
                    [by implies_intro given-p-divides-empty-product]
                }
              @prime-gives-empty-witness |
                (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
                  -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, ZERO))
                  -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, ZERO) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
                [by implies_intro given-prime-base]
            }
          @empty-product-claim-for-all-p |
            forall p: Int;
              (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
              -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, ZERO))
              -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, ZERO) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
            [by forall_intro generalize-p-base]
        }
      @empty-product-claim-for-all-s |
        forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
          (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
          -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, ZERO))
          -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, ZERO) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
        [by forall_intro generalize-s-base]
    }
  @base-case |
    is_nonneg(ZERO) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
      (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
      -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, ZERO))
      -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, ZERO) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))))
    [by implies_intro base-case-proof]

  // inductive step: from the claim at k, derive it at succ(k).
  @induction-step |
    fix k: Int {
      @given-k-nonneg |
        assume is_nonneg(k) {
          @k-nonneg |
            is_nonneg(k)
            [by hypothesis given-k-nonneg]
          @given-inductive-hypothesis |
            assume is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
              (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
              -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
              -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))) {
              @guarded-inductive-hypothesis |
                is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
                  (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
                  -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
                  -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))))
                [by hypothesis given-inductive-hypothesis]
              @inductive-hypothesis |
                forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
                  (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
                  -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
                  -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
                [by modus_ponens guarded-inductive-hypothesis k-nonneg]
              @k-less_than-succ-k |
                less_than(k, succ(k))
                [using specialize lessThanSuccSelf(k)]
              @given-succ-k-nonneg |
                assume is_nonneg(succ(k)) {
                  @claim-at-succ-k |
                    fix s: Seq {
                      @generalize-p-step |
                        fix p: Int {
                          @given-prime-step |
                            assume less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)) {
                              @prime-p |
                                less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))
                                [by hypothesis given-prime-step]
                              @given-p-divides-succ-product |
                                assume does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, succ(k))) {
                                  @p-divides-succ-product |
                                    does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, succ(k)))
                                    [by hypothesis given-p-divides-succ-product]
                                  @successor-product-splits |
                                    productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = mul(productUpTo(s, k), at(s, k))
                                    [using specialize productUpToSucc(s, k) k-nonneg]
                                  @p-divides-product-times-last |
                                    does_divide(p, mul(productUpTo(s, k), at(s, k)))
                                    [by rewrite successor-product-splits p-divides-succ-product]
                                  @euclids-lemma |
                                    forall a, b, p2: Int;
                                      (less_than(ONE, p2) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p2) -> (d = ONE or d = p2)))
                                      -> does_divide(p2, mul(a, b))
                                      -> (does_divide(p2, a) or does_divide(p2, b))
                                    [by cite euclidsLemma]
                                  @euclid-at-product-last-p |
                                    (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
                                      -> does_divide(p, mul(productUpTo(s, k), at(s, k)))
                                      -> (does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k)) or does_divide(p, at(s, k)))
                                    [by forall_elim(productUpTo(s, k), at(s, k), p) euclids-lemma]
                                  @p-divides-product-or-last |
                                    does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k)) or does_divide(p, at(s, k))
                                    [using tautology euclid-at-product-last-p prime-p p-divides-product-times-last]
                                  // case 1: p divides the length-k product — the IH gives i < k < succ(k).
                                  @when-p-divides-product |
                                    assume does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k)) {
                                      @p-divides-product |
                                        does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
                                        [by hypothesis when-p-divides-product]
                                      @inductive-hypothesis-at-s |
                                        forall p2: Int;
                                          (less_than(ONE, p2) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p2) -> (d = ONE or d = p2)))
                                          -> does_divide(p2, productUpTo(s, k))
                                          -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p2, at(s, i))))
                                        [by forall_elim(s) inductive-hypothesis]
                                      @inductive-hypothesis-at-s-p |
                                        (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
                                          -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
                                          -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
                                        [by forall_elim(p) inductive-hypothesis-at-s]
                                      @earlier-entry-exists |
                                        exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))
                                        [using tautology inductive-hypothesis-at-s-p prime-p p-divides-product]
                                      @with-earlier-entry-index |
                                        unpack earlier: Int from earlier-entry-exists {
                                          @entry-earlier-nonneg-less_than-k-divides |
                                            is_nonneg(earlier) and (less_than(earlier, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, earlier)))
                                            [by hypothesis with-earlier-entry-index]
                                          @earlier-less_than-k |
                                            less_than(earlier, k)
                                            [using tautology entry-earlier-nonneg-less_than-k-divides]
                                          @earlier-less_than-succ-k |
                                            less_than(earlier, succ(k))
                                            [using specialize lessThanSuccRight(earlier, k) earlier-less_than-k]
                                          @entry-earlier-nonneg-less_than-succ-k-divides |
                                            is_nonneg(earlier) and (less_than(earlier, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, earlier)))
                                            [using tautology entry-earlier-nonneg-less_than-k-divides earlier-less_than-succ-k]
                                          @conclusion-earlier-entry-less_than-succ-k |
                                            exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))
                                            [by exists_intro(earlier) entry-earlier-nonneg-less_than-succ-k-divides]
                                        }
                                      @earlier-entry-less_than-succ-k |
                                        exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))
                                        [by exists_elim with-earlier-entry-index]
                                    }
                                  // case 2: p divides the new last entry — witness i = k.
                                  @when-p-divides-last |
                                    assume does_divide(p, at(s, k)) {
                                      @p-divides-last |
                                        does_divide(p, at(s, k))
                                        [by hypothesis when-p-divides-last]
                                      @k-nonneg-less_than-succ-k-divides |
                                        is_nonneg(k) and (less_than(k, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, k)))
                                        [using tautology k-nonneg k-less_than-succ-k p-divides-last]
                                      @conclusion-last-entry-witness |
                                        exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))
                                        [by exists_intro(k) k-nonneg-less_than-succ-k-divides]
                                    }
                                  @entry-less_than-succ-k-divides |
                                    exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))
                                    [by or_elim p-divides-product-or-last when-p-divides-product when-p-divides-last]
                                }
                              @p-divides-succ-product-gives-witness |
                                does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, succ(k)))
                                  -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
                                [by implies_intro given-p-divides-succ-product]
                            }
                          @prime-gives-succ-witness |
                            (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
                              -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, succ(k)))
                              -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
                            [by implies_intro given-prime-step]
                        }
                      @succ-product-claim-for-all-p |
                        forall p: Int;
                          (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
                          -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, succ(k)))
                          -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
                        [by forall_intro generalize-p-step]
                    }
                  @claim-at-succ-k-for-all-s |
                    forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
                      (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
                      -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, succ(k)))
                      -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
                    [by forall_intro claim-at-succ-k]
                }
              @guarded-claim-at-succ-k |
                is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
                  (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
                  -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, succ(k)))
                  -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))))
                [by implies_intro given-succ-k-nonneg]
            }
          @hypothesis-at-k-gives-claim-at-succ-k |
            (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
              (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
              -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
              -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))))
              -> (is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
              (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
              -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, succ(k)))
              -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))))
            [by implies_intro given-inductive-hypothesis]
        }
      @induction-step-at-k |
        is_nonneg(k)
          -> (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
          (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
          -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
          -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))))
          -> (is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
          (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
          -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, succ(k)))
          -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))))
        [by implies_intro given-k-nonneg]
    }
  @induction-step-for-all-k |
    forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k)
      -> (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
      (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
      -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
      -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))))
      -> (is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
      (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
      -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, succ(k)))
      -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, succ(k)) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))))
    [by forall_intro induction-step]

  // nonnegInduction on the guard-inclusive predicate gives, for nonneg k, the
  // guarded predicate is_nonneg(k) -> BODY[k]; a nonneg k discharges the guard.
  @guarded-claim-on-nonnegs |
    forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) -> (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
      (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
      -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
      -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))))
    [using instantiation nonnegInduction((fun k: Int => is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k)) -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))))) base-case induction-step-for-all-k]

  @collapse-double-guard |
    fix k: Int {
      @given-k-nonneg-final |
        assume is_nonneg(k) {
          @k-nonneg-final |
            is_nonneg(k)
            [by hypothesis given-k-nonneg-final]
          @claim-at-k |
            forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
              (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
              -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
              -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
            [using specialize guarded-claim-on-nonnegs(k) k-nonneg-final k-nonneg-final]
        }
      @k-nonneg-gives-claim |
        is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
          (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
          -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
          -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i)))))
        [by implies_intro given-k-nonneg-final]
    }

  @conclusion |
    forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) ->
      forall s: Seq; forall p: Int;
      (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
      -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(s, k))
      -> (exists i: Int; is_nonneg(i) and (less_than(i, k) and does_divide(p, at(s, i))))
    [by forall_intro collapse-double-guard]
qed
```

### The Infinitude of Primes

Euclid's classical argument (Judson, Theorem 2.12): were there only finitely
many primes `p₁, …, pₙ`, the number `P = p₁·p₂·⋯·pₙ + 1` has a prime factor `p`
(every integer `> 1` does — `primeFactorExists`), and by completeness `p` is one
of the `pᵢ`. Then `p` divides the product `p₁·⋯·pₙ` (`memberDividesProduct`), and
`p` divides `P`, so `p` divides `P − (product) = 1` (`dividesSubtracts`) — forcing
`p ≤ 1`, contradicting `p > 1`. No finite list can hold every prime.

We phrase a finite list of primes as a `Seq primes` together with a bound `count`,
its product `productUpTo(primes, count)`, and the *completeness* hypothesis
"every prime occurs among the first `count` entries" — stated with the membership
predicate `inSeqBefore`. The theorem is the negation: **no such complete finite
list exists**. Its one honest side condition, `productUpTo(primes, count) > 0`,
holds of any list of primes (each factor exceeds `1`); we take it as a hypothesis
rather than re-derive it, since it is manifest and orthogonal to the argument.

The `P > 1` step reads `P = product + 1 > product ≥ 1`; the `P − product = 1`
step is the only elided algebra (a linear identity discharged by `simplify`).

```bpa
theorem infinitudeOfPrimes: forall primes: Seq; forall count: Int;
  is_nonneg(count) -> less_than(ZERO, productUpTo(primes, count))
  -> (not (forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count)))
proof
  @generalize-primes |
    fix primes: Seq {
      @generalize-count |
        fix count: Int {
          @given-count-nonneg |
            assume is_nonneg(count) {
              @given-product-positive |
                assume less_than(ZERO, productUpTo(primes, count)) {
                  @product-positive |
                    less_than(ZERO, productUpTo(primes, count))
                    [by hypothesis given-product-positive]
                  @count-nonneg |
                    is_nonneg(count)
                    [by hypothesis given-count-nonneg]
                  // Suppose `primes` (up to `count`) enumerates every prime.
                  @given-list-is-complete |
                    assume forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count) {
                      @list-is-complete |
                        forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count)
                        [by hypothesis given-list-is-complete]
                      // P = product + 1 exceeds 1: product ≥ 1 and product < product + 1.
                      @one-at-most-positive |
                        forall x: Int; less_than(ZERO, x) -> at_most(ONE, x)
                        [by cite oneAtMostPositive]
                      @one-at-most-product-if |
                        less_than(ZERO, productUpTo(primes, count)) -> at_most(ONE, productUpTo(primes, count))
                        [by forall_elim(productUpTo(primes, count)) one-at-most-positive]
                      @one-at-most-product |
                        at_most(ONE, productUpTo(primes, count))
                        [by modus_ponens one-at-most-product-if product-positive]
                      @product-less_than-successor |
                        forall n: Int; less_than(n, succ(n))
                        [by cite lessThanSelf]
                      @product-less_than-succ-product |
                        less_than(productUpTo(primes, count), succ(productUpTo(primes, count)))
                        [by forall_elim(productUpTo(primes, count)) product-less_than-successor]
                      @succ-product-is-product-plus-one |
                        succ(productUpTo(primes, count)) = add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE)
                        [using simplify oneIsSuccZero addSuccRight addZeroRight]
                      @product-less_than-big-number |
                        less_than(productUpTo(primes, count), add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE))
                        [by rewrite succ-product-is-product-plus-one product-less_than-succ-product]
                      @at-most-then-less_than |
                        forall x, y, z: Int; at_most(x, y) -> less_than(y, z) -> less_than(x, z)
                        [by cite atMostThenLessThan]
                      @one-less_than-big-number-if |
                        at_most(ONE, productUpTo(primes, count)) -> less_than(productUpTo(primes, count), add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE)) -> less_than(ONE, add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE))
                        [by forall_elim(ONE, productUpTo(primes, count), add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE)) at-most-then-less_than]
                      @one-less_than-big-number |
                        less_than(ONE, add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE))
                        [using tautology one-less_than-big-number-if one-at-most-product product-less_than-big-number]
                      // P has a prime factor p.
                      @prime-factor-exists |
                        forall n: Int; less_than(ONE, n) -> exists p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) and does_divide(p, n)
                        [by cite primeFactorExists]
                      @big-number-has-prime-factor-if |
                        less_than(ONE, add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE)) -> exists p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) and does_divide(p, add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE))
                        [by forall_elim(add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE)) prime-factor-exists]
                      @big-number-has-prime-factor |
                        exists p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) and does_divide(p, add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE))
                        [by modus_ponens big-number-has-prime-factor-if one-less_than-big-number]
                      @with-prime-factor-p |
                        unpack p: Int from big-number-has-prime-factor {
                          @p-prime-and-divides-big-number |
                            (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) and does_divide(p, add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE))
                            [by hypothesis with-prime-factor-p]
                          @p-is-prime |
                            less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))
                            [by and_elim_left p-prime-and-divides-big-number]
                          @p-divides-big-number |
                            does_divide(p, add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE))
                            [by and_elim_right p-prime-and-divides-big-number]
                          @p-above-one |
                            less_than(ONE, p)
                            [by and_elim_left p-is-prime]
                          // p is one of the listed primes, so p | product.
                          @p-in-list |
                            inSeqBefore(p, primes, count)
                            [using specialize list-is-complete(p) p-is-prime]
                          @p-divides-product |
                            does_divide(p, productUpTo(primes, count))
                            [using specialize memberDividesProduct(primes, count, p) count-nonneg p-in-list]
                          // p | P and p | product give p | (P − product) = p | 1.
                          @divides-subtracts |
                            forall d, a, b: Int; does_divide(d, a) -> does_divide(d, b) -> does_divide(d, sub(a, b))
                            [by cite dividesSubtracts]
                          @p-divides-difference-if |
                            does_divide(p, add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE)) -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(primes, count)) -> does_divide(p, sub(add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE), productUpTo(primes, count)))
                            [by forall_elim(p, add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE), productUpTo(primes, count)) divides-subtracts]
                          @p-divides-difference |
                            does_divide(p, sub(add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE), productUpTo(primes, count)))
                            [using tautology p-divides-difference-if p-divides-big-number p-divides-product]
                          // P − product = (product + 1) − product = 1: the linear
                          // identity Judson elides. `simplify` closes it from oriented
                          // (terminating) ring rules — unfold `sub`, the one needed
                          // commutation as a ground fact, reassociate, cancel `P + (−P)`.
                          @difference-unfolds |
                            forall x, y: Int; sub(x, y) = add(x, neg(y))
                            [by cite definitionOfSubtraction]
                          @addition-commutes |
                            forall x, y: Int; add(x, y) = add(y, x)
                            [by cite addIsCommutative]
                          @product-plus-one-commuted |
                            add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE) = add(ONE, productUpTo(primes, count))
                            [by forall_elim(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE) addition-commutes]
                          @addition-associates |
                            forall x, y, z: Int; add(add(x, y), z) = add(x, add(y, z))
                            [by cite addIsAssociative]
                          @cancels-with-negation |
                            forall x: Int; add(x, neg(x)) = ZERO
                            [by cite addNegRight]
                          @adds-zero-on-the-right |
                            forall x: Int; add(x, ZERO) = x
                            [by cite addZeroRight]
                          @difference-is-one |
                            sub(add(productUpTo(primes, count), ONE), productUpTo(primes, count)) = ONE
                            [using simplify difference-unfolds product-plus-one-commuted addition-associates cancels-with-negation adds-zero-on-the-right]
                          @p-divides-one |
                            does_divide(p, ONE)
                            [by rewrite difference-is-one p-divides-difference]
                          // p | 1 with p > 1 is impossible: p ≤ 1 < p is less_than itself.
                          @zero-less_than-one |
                            less_than(ZERO, ONE)
                            [by cite zeroLessThanOne]
                          @zero-less_than-p-if-chained |
                            less_than(ZERO, ONE) -> less_than(ONE, p) -> less_than(ZERO, p)
                            [using specialize lessThanTransitive(ZERO, ONE, p)]
                          @p-positive |
                            less_than(ZERO, p)
                            [using tautology zero-less_than-p-if-chained zero-less_than-one p-above-one]
                          @p-at-most-one |
                            at_most(p, ONE)
                            [using specialize divisorAtMost(p, ONE) p-positive zero-less_than-one p-divides-one]
                          @p-less_than-itself |
                            less_than(p, p)
                            [using specialize atMostThenLessThan(p, ONE, p) p-at-most-one p-above-one]
                          @p-not-less_than-itself |
                            not less_than(p, p)
                            [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(p)]
                          @contradiction |
                            not (forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count))
                            [by absurd p-less_than-itself p-not-less_than-itself]
                        }
                      @contradiction |
                        not (forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count))
                        [by exists_elim with-prime-factor-p]
                    }
                  @list-complete-is-absurd |
                    (forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count)) -> (not (forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count)))
                    [by implies_intro given-list-is-complete]
                  @no-complete-finite-list |
                    not (forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count))
                    [using tautology list-complete-is-absurd]
                }
              @product-positive-implies-no-complete-list |
                less_than(ZERO, productUpTo(primes, count)) -> (not (forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count)))
                [by implies_intro given-product-positive]
            }
          @count-nonneg-implies-no-complete-list |
            is_nonneg(count) -> less_than(ZERO, productUpTo(primes, count)) -> (not (forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count)))
            [by implies_intro given-count-nonneg]
        }
      @discharge-count |
        forall count: Int; is_nonneg(count) -> less_than(ZERO, productUpTo(primes, count)) -> (not (forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count)))
        [by forall_intro generalize-count]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall primes: Seq; forall count: Int; is_nonneg(count) -> less_than(ZERO, productUpTo(primes, count)) -> (not (forall q: Int; (less_than(ONE, q) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, q) -> (d = ONE or d = q))) -> inSeqBefore(q, primes, count)))
    [by forall_intro generalize-primes]
qed
```

The proof reifies Judson's contradiction exactly: `primeFactorExists` supplies the
prime factor `p` of `P`; the completeness hypothesis places `p` in the list;
`memberDividesProduct` makes `p` divide the product; `dividesSubtracts` collapses
`p | P` and `p | product` to `p | 1`; and a positive divisor of `1` is `≤ 1`,
which `p > 1` refutes. The `count` is left unconstrained beyond nonnegativity —
the argument never inspects it — so the theorem denies a complete finite prime
list of *any* length.

## The Euclidean Algorithm

Judson closes §2.2 with the **Euclidean Algorithm**: to compute `gcd(a, b)`
concretely, repeatedly apply the Division Algorithm — `a = bq₁ + r₁`,
`b = r₁q₂ + r₂`, `r₁ = r₂q₃ + r₃`, … — until a zero remainder is reached; the
last nonzero remainder is `gcd(a, b)`, and back-substituting up the chain recovers
the Bézout coefficients `r`, `s` of the previous theorem. This is a *computation*
on particular integers rather than a universally-quantified statement, so we do
not transcribe a numeric instance here; `bezout` already establishes, for every
nonzero `a`, `b`, the existence and defining properties of the gcd that the
algorithm computes.

```bpa
// The Euclidean Algorithm (illustrative, not transcribed):
//   gcd(a, b): while b != 0 { (a, b) := (b, a mod b) }; return a
// It terminates because the nonnegative remainders strictly decrease
// (Well-Ordering), and back-substitution through the division steps yields the
// Bézout coefficients r, s with gcd(a, b) = a*r + b*s established by `bezout`.
```

## The Fundamental Theorem of Arithmetic
