> **Theorem 2.15 (Fundamental Theorem of Arithmetic).** Let $n$ be an integer such
> that $n > 1$. Then $n = p_1 p_2 \cdots p_k$, where $p_1, \ldots, p_k$ are primes
> (not necessarily distinct). Furthermore, this factorization is unique; that is,
> if $n = q_1 q_2 \cdots q_l$ is a second factorization into primes, then $k = l$
> and the $q_i$ are, after reordering, the $p_i$.

The book's §2.2 capstone. We prove it in the form the ℤ sequence library makes
expressible: a **prime factorization of `n`** is a finite sequence `s` of length
`k` whose every entry is prime and whose finite product `productUpTo(s, k)` is
`n`. As throughout, "`x` is prime" is spelled inline as
`less_than(ONE, x) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, x) -> (d = ONE or d = x))`.

### Existence

Judson: *every integer `n > 1` is a product of primes.* The argument is strong
induction on `n`. A prime factor `p` splits off (`primeFactorExists` gives a
prime `p` dividing `n`, hence a cofactor `c` with `n = p·c`); the cofactor is
positive, so either `c = 1` — and then `n = p` is itself prime, factored by the
singleton `[p]` — or `c > 1`, and then `c < n`, so the induction hypothesis
factors `c` as a sequence `t`, which we prepend `p` to. We first isolate three
order/arithmetic facts the induction leans on.

A nonnegative index strictly less_than `ONE` must be `ZERO`: `ONE = succ ZERO`, so
`m < ONE` splits (`lessThanSuccSplit`) into `m < ZERO` — impossible for a
nonnegative `m` — or `m = ZERO`.

```bpa
theorem nonnegLessThanOneIsZero: forall m: Int; is_nonneg(m) -> less_than(m, ONE) -> m = ZERO
proof
  @generalize-m |
    fix m: Int {
      @given-m-nonneg |
        assume is_nonneg(m) {
          @given-m-less_than-one |
            assume less_than(m, ONE) {
              @m-nonneg |
                is_nonneg(m)
                [by hypothesis given-m-nonneg]
              @m-less_than-one |
                less_than(m, ONE)
                [by hypothesis given-m-less_than-one]
              @one-is-succ-zero |
                ONE = succ(ZERO)
                [by cite oneIsSuccZero]
              @m-less_than-succ-zero |
                less_than(m, succ(ZERO))
                [by rewrite one-is-succ-zero m-less_than-one]
              @zero-is-nonneg |
                is_nonneg(ZERO)
                [by cite nonnegZero]
              @m-less_than-zero-or-equal-zero |
                less_than(m, ZERO) or m = ZERO
                [using specialize lessThanSuccSplit(m, ZERO) m-nonneg zero-is-nonneg m-less_than-succ-zero]
              @m-is-zero |
                m = ZERO
                case m-less_than-zero-or-equal-zero {
                  @when-m-less_than-zero |
                    assume less_than(m, ZERO) {
                      @m-less_than-zero |
                        less_than(m, ZERO)
                        [by hypothesis when-m-less_than-zero]
                      @m-not-less_than-zero |
                        not less_than(m, ZERO)
                        [using specialize nonnegNotLessThanZero(m) m-nonneg]
                      @conclusion-m-is-zero |
                        m = ZERO
                        [by absurd m-less_than-zero m-not-less_than-zero]
                    }
                  @when-m-equals-zero |
                    assume m = ZERO {
                      @conclusion-m-is-zero |
                        m = ZERO
                        [by hypothesis when-m-equals-zero]
                    }
                }
            }
          @less_than-one-implies-zero |
            less_than(m, ONE) -> m = ZERO
            [by implies_intro given-m-less_than-one]
        }
      @nonneg-implies-less_than-one-implies-zero |
        is_nonneg(m) -> less_than(m, ONE) -> m = ZERO
        [by implies_intro given-m-nonneg]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall m: Int; is_nonneg(m) -> less_than(m, ONE) -> m = ZERO
    [by forall_intro generalize-m]
qed
```

`p > 1` and `c > 0` force `c < p·c`: write `p = 1 + q` with `q = p − 1 > 0`, so
`p·c = c·p = c·1 + c·q = c + c·q` with `c·q > 0`, whence `c < c + c·q = p·c`. This
is what makes the cofactor `c` strictly smaller than `n = p·c`, licensing the
induction hypothesis.

```bpa
theorem lessThanMulGreaterThanOne: forall p, c: Int;
  less_than(ONE, p) -> less_than(ZERO, c) -> less_than(c, mul(p, c))
proof
  @generalize-p |
    fix p: Int {
      @generalize-c |
        fix c: Int {
          @given-p-above-one |
            assume less_than(ONE, p) {
              @given-c-positive |
                assume less_than(ZERO, c) {
                  @p-above-one |
                    less_than(ONE, p)
                    [by hypothesis given-p-above-one]
                  @c-positive |
                    less_than(ZERO, c)
                    [by hypothesis given-c-positive]
                  // q := p − 1 is positive, and 1 + q = p.
                  @one-plus-gap-is-p |
                    add(ONE, sub(p, ONE)) = p
                    [using arithmetic]
                  @add-preserves-order |
                    forall a, b, x: Int; less_than(a, b) -> less_than(add(x, a), add(x, b))
                    [by cite additionPreservesOrder]
                  @one-is-one-plus-zero |
                    ONE = add(ONE, ZERO)
                    [using arithmetic]
                  @p-is-one-plus-gap |
                    p = add(ONE, sub(p, ONE))
                    [using arithmetic one-plus-gap-is-p]
                  // recast 1 < p as (1+0) < (1+q), then cancel the leading 1.
                  @one-recast-less_than-p |
                    less_than(add(ONE, ZERO), p)
                    [by rewrite one-is-one-plus-zero p-above-one]
                  @one-plus-zero-less_than-one-plus-gap |
                    less_than(add(ONE, ZERO), add(ONE, sub(p, ONE)))
                    [by rewrite p-is-one-plus-gap one-recast-less_than-p]
                  @cancel-leading-one |
                    forall x, a, b: Int; less_than(add(x, a), add(x, b)) -> less_than(a, b)
                    [by cite lessThanAddCancelLeft]
                  @cancel-one-for-gap |
                    less_than(add(ONE, ZERO), add(ONE, sub(p, ONE))) -> less_than(ZERO, sub(p, ONE))
                    [by forall_elim(ONE, ZERO, sub(p, ONE)) cancel-leading-one]
                  @gap-positive |
                    less_than(ZERO, sub(p, ONE))
                    [by modus_ponens cancel-one-for-gap one-plus-zero-less_than-one-plus-gap]
                  // c·q > 0 and c·1 + c·q = c·p.
                  @product-positive |
                    forall a, b: Int; less_than(ZERO, a) -> less_than(ZERO, b) -> less_than(ZERO, mul(a, b))
                    [by cite productWithPositiveIsPositive]
                  @c-gap-positive-if-both |
                    less_than(ZERO, c) -> less_than(ZERO, sub(p, ONE)) -> less_than(ZERO, mul(c, sub(p, ONE)))
                    [by forall_elim(c, sub(p, ONE)) product-positive]
                  @c-times-gap-positive |
                    less_than(ZERO, mul(c, sub(p, ONE)))
                    [using tautology c-gap-positive-if-both c-positive gap-positive]
                  @distribute |
                    forall a, b, x: Int; mul(a, add(b, x)) = add(mul(a, b), mul(a, x))
                    [by cite mulAddDistribLeft]
                  @c-times-p-distributes |
                    mul(c, add(ONE, sub(p, ONE))) = add(mul(c, ONE), mul(c, sub(p, ONE)))
                    [by forall_elim(c, ONE, sub(p, ONE)) distribute]
                  @c-times-one-is-c |
                    mul(c, ONE) = c
                    [using specialize mulOneRight(c)]
                  @c-times-p-is-c-plus-c-gap |
                    mul(c, p) = add(c, mul(c, sub(p, ONE)))
                    [using chain one-plus-gap-is-p c-times-p-distributes c-times-one-is-c]
                  // c < c + c·q via additionPreservesOrder, then rewrite to c·p, p·c.
                  @c-less_than-c-plus-c-gap-raw |
                    less_than(ZERO, mul(c, sub(p, ONE))) -> less_than(add(c, ZERO), add(c, mul(c, sub(p, ONE))))
                    [by forall_elim(ZERO, mul(c, sub(p, ONE)), c) add-preserves-order]
                  @c-plus-zero-less_than-c-plus-gap |
                    less_than(add(c, ZERO), add(c, mul(c, sub(p, ONE))))
                    [by modus_ponens c-less_than-c-plus-c-gap-raw c-times-gap-positive]
                  @c-plus-zero-is-c |
                    add(c, ZERO) = c
                    [using specialize addZeroRight(c)]
                  @c-less_than-c-plus-c-gap |
                    less_than(c, add(c, mul(c, sub(p, ONE))))
                    [by rewrite c-plus-zero-is-c c-plus-zero-less_than-c-plus-gap]
                  @c-less_than-c-times-p |
                    less_than(c, mul(c, p))
                    [by rewrite c-times-p-is-c-plus-c-gap c-less_than-c-plus-c-gap]
                  @c-times-p-is-p-times-c |
                    mul(c, p) = mul(p, c)
                    [using specialize mulIsCommutative(c, p)]
                  @c-less_than-p-times-c |
                    less_than(c, mul(p, c))
                    [by rewrite c-times-p-is-p-times-c c-less_than-c-times-p]
                }
              @c-positive-implies-less_than |
                less_than(ZERO, c) -> less_than(c, mul(p, c))
                [by implies_intro given-c-positive]
            }
          @p-above-one-implies-less_than |
            less_than(ONE, p) -> less_than(ZERO, c) -> less_than(c, mul(p, c))
            [by implies_intro given-p-above-one]
        }
      @discharge-c |
        forall c: Int; less_than(ONE, p) -> less_than(ZERO, c) -> less_than(c, mul(p, c))
        [by forall_intro generalize-c]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall p, c: Int; less_than(ONE, p) -> less_than(ZERO, c) -> less_than(c, mul(p, c))
    [by forall_intro generalize-p]
qed
```

The cofactor of a positive number by a positive factor is itself positive: from
`n = p·c` with `n, p > 0`, the sign of `c` (by `signTrichotomy`) cannot be zero
(that would make `n = 0`) nor negative (that would make `n = −(p·(−c)) ≤ 0`),
both against `n > 0`.

```bpa
theorem positiveCofactor: forall n, p, c: Int;
  less_than(ZERO, n) -> less_than(ZERO, p) -> n = mul(p, c) -> less_than(ZERO, c)
proof
  @generalize-n |
    fix n: Int {
      @generalize-p |
        fix p: Int {
          @generalize-c |
            fix c: Int {
              @given-n-positive |
                assume less_than(ZERO, n) {
                  @given-p-positive |
                    assume less_than(ZERO, p) {
                      @given-n-is-p-times-c |
                        assume n = mul(p, c) {
                          @n-positive |
                            less_than(ZERO, n)
                            [by hypothesis given-n-positive]
                          @p-positive |
                            less_than(ZERO, p)
                            [by hypothesis given-p-positive]
                          @n-is-p-times-c |
                            n = mul(p, c)
                            [by hypothesis given-n-is-p-times-c]
                          @p-nonneg |
                            is_nonneg(p)
                            [using specialize positiveIsNonneg(p) p-positive]
                          @c-sign |
                            less_than(ZERO, c) or c = ZERO or less_than(c, ZERO)
                            [using specialize signTrichotomy(c)]
                          @c-positive |
                            less_than(ZERO, c)
                            case c-sign {
                              @when-c-positive |
                                assume less_than(ZERO, c) {
                                  @conclusion-c-positive |
                                    less_than(ZERO, c)
                                    [by hypothesis when-c-positive]
                                }
                              @when-c-zero |
                                assume c = ZERO {
                                  @c-is-zero |
                                    c = ZERO
                                    [by hypothesis when-c-zero]
                                  @p-times-zero-is-zero |
                                    mul(p, ZERO) = ZERO
                                    [using specialize mulZeroRight(p)]
                                  @n-is-zero |
                                    n = ZERO
                                    [using chain n-is-p-times-c c-is-zero p-times-zero-is-zero]
                                  @zero-less_than-zero |
                                    less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                    [by rewrite n-is-zero n-positive]
                                  @zero-not-less_than-zero |
                                    not less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                    [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(ZERO)]
                                  @conclusion-c-positive |
                                    less_than(ZERO, c)
                                    [by absurd zero-less_than-zero zero-not-less_than-zero]
                                }
                              @when-c-negative |
                                assume less_than(c, ZERO) {
                                  @c-negative |
                                    less_than(c, ZERO)
                                    [by hypothesis when-c-negative]
                                  @neg-c-nonneg |
                                    is_nonneg(neg(c))
                                    [using specialize negLessThanZero(c) c-negative]
                                  @product-of-nonnegs-nonneg |
                                    forall a, b: Int; is_nonneg(a) -> is_nonneg(b) -> is_nonneg(mul(a, b))
                                    [by cite nonnegMul]
                                  @p-times-neg-c-nonneg-if |
                                    is_nonneg(p) -> is_nonneg(neg(c)) -> is_nonneg(mul(p, neg(c)))
                                    [by forall_elim(p, neg(c)) product-of-nonnegs-nonneg]
                                  @p-times-neg-c-nonneg |
                                    is_nonneg(mul(p, neg(c)))
                                    [using tautology p-times-neg-c-nonneg-if p-nonneg neg-c-nonneg]
                                  @p-times-neg-c-is-neg-product |
                                    mul(p, neg(c)) = neg(mul(p, c))
                                    [using specialize mulNegRight(p, c)]
                                  // p·(−c) = −(p·c) = −n.
                                  @p-times-neg-c-is-neg-n |
                                    mul(p, neg(c)) = neg(n)
                                    [using chain p-times-neg-c-is-neg-product n-is-p-times-c]
                                  @neg-n-nonneg |
                                    is_nonneg(neg(n))
                                    [by rewrite p-times-neg-c-is-neg-n p-times-neg-c-nonneg]
                                  @add-n-neg-n-is-zero |
                                    add(n, neg(n)) = ZERO
                                    [using specialize addNegRight(n)]
                                  @at-most-intro |
                                    forall a, g, b: Int; is_nonneg(g) -> add(a, g) = b -> at_most(a, b)
                                    [by cite lessOrEqualIntro]
                                  @n-at-most-zero-if |
                                    is_nonneg(neg(n)) -> add(n, neg(n)) = ZERO -> at_most(n, ZERO)
                                    [by forall_elim(n, neg(n), ZERO) at-most-intro]
                                  @n-at-most-zero |
                                    at_most(n, ZERO)
                                    [using tautology n-at-most-zero-if neg-n-nonneg add-n-neg-n-is-zero]
                                  @zero-at-most-n |
                                    at_most(ZERO, n)
                                    [using specialize lessThanToLessOrEqual(ZERO, n) n-positive]
                                  @n-is-zero |
                                    n = ZERO
                                    [using specialize lessOrEqualAntisymmetric(n, ZERO) n-at-most-zero zero-at-most-n]
                                  @zero-less_than-zero |
                                    less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                    [by rewrite n-is-zero n-positive]
                                  @zero-not-less_than-zero |
                                    not less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                    [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(ZERO)]
                                  @conclusion-c-positive |
                                    less_than(ZERO, c)
                                    [by absurd zero-less_than-zero zero-not-less_than-zero]
                                }
                            }
                        }
                      @is-p-times-c-implies-c-positive |
                        n = mul(p, c) -> less_than(ZERO, c)
                        [by implies_intro given-n-is-p-times-c]
                    }
                  @p-positive-implies |
                    less_than(ZERO, p) -> n = mul(p, c) -> less_than(ZERO, c)
                    [by implies_intro given-p-positive]
                }
              @n-positive-implies |
                less_than(ZERO, n) -> less_than(ZERO, p) -> n = mul(p, c) -> less_than(ZERO, c)
                [by implies_intro given-n-positive]
            }
          @discharge-c |
            forall c: Int; less_than(ZERO, n) -> less_than(ZERO, p) -> n = mul(p, c) -> less_than(ZERO, c)
            [by forall_intro generalize-c]
        }
      @discharge-p |
        forall p, c: Int; less_than(ZERO, n) -> less_than(ZERO, p) -> n = mul(p, c) -> less_than(ZERO, c)
        [by forall_intro generalize-p]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall n, p, c: Int; less_than(ZERO, n) -> less_than(ZERO, p) -> n = mul(p, c) -> less_than(ZERO, c)
    [by forall_intro generalize-n]
qed
```

Now the existence half itself. Strong induction on `n` (`nonnegStrongInduction`):
take a prime factor `p | n` and the cofactor `c` with `n = p·c`, positive by
`positiveCofactor`. Split on `c`: when `c = 1`, `n = p` and the singleton `[p]`
(from `seqSingletonExists`) is the factorization; when `c > 1`, `c < n` so the
induction hypothesis gives a prime factorization `t` of length `j` for `c`, and
`seqConcatExists` prepends `[p]` — the resulting sequence has product
`p · productUpTo(t, j) = p·c = n`, and each of its entries is prime (index `0` is
`p`, the shifted indices are `t`'s prime entries). The `c < 1` branch is vacuous
(`c > 0` contradicts `c < 1`).

```bpa
theorem ftaExistence: forall n: Int; less_than(ONE, n) ->
  exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
  is_nonneg(k)
  and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
  (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
  and n = productUpTo(s, k))
proof
  @zero-less_than-one |
    less_than(ZERO, ONE)
    [by cite zeroLessThanOne]
  @one-nonneg |
    is_nonneg(ONE)
    [using specialize positiveIsNonneg(ONE) zero-less_than-one]
  @strong-step |
    fix n: Int {
      @given-n-nonneg |
        assume is_nonneg(n) {
          @given-inductive-hypothesis |
            assume forall m: Int; is_nonneg(m) -> less_than(m, n) ->
              (less_than(ONE, m) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
              is_nonneg(k)
              and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
              (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
              and m = productUpTo(s, k))) {
              @inductive-hypothesis |
                forall m: Int; is_nonneg(m) -> less_than(m, n) ->
                  (less_than(ONE, m) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                  is_nonneg(k)
                  and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                  (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                  and m = productUpTo(s, k)))
                [by hypothesis given-inductive-hypothesis]
              @given-n-above-one |
                assume less_than(ONE, n) {
                  @n-above-one |
                    less_than(ONE, n)
                    [by hypothesis given-n-above-one]
                  @one-less_than-n-transitive |
                    less_than(ZERO, ONE) -> less_than(ONE, n) -> less_than(ZERO, n)
                    [using specialize lessThanTransitive(ZERO, ONE, n)]
                  @n-positive |
                    less_than(ZERO, n)
                    [using tautology one-less_than-n-transitive zero-less_than-one n-above-one]

                  // ---- get a prime factor p and cofactor c with n = p·c -----
                  @n-has-a-prime-factor |
                    exists p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) and does_divide(p, n)
                    [using specialize primeFactorExists(n) n-above-one]
                  @with-prime-factor-p |
                    unpack p: Int from n-has-a-prime-factor {
                      @prime-and-divides |
                        (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) and does_divide(p, n)
                        [by hypothesis with-prime-factor-p]
                      @p-is-prime |
                        less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))
                        [by and_elim_left prime-and-divides]
                      @p-above-one |
                        less_than(ONE, p)
                        [by and_elim_left p-is-prime]
                      @p-divides-n |
                        does_divide(p, n)
                        [by and_elim_right prime-and-divides]
                      @p-positive |
                        less_than(ZERO, p)
                        [using specialize lessThanTransitive(ZERO, ONE, p) zero-less_than-one p-above-one]
                      @n-is-p-multiple |
                        exists k: Int; n = mul(p, k)
                        [using specialize dividesElim(p, n) p-divides-n]
                      @with-cofactor-c |
                        unpack c: Int from n-is-p-multiple {
                          @n-is-p-times-c |
                            n = mul(p, c)
                            [by hypothesis with-cofactor-c]
                          @c-positive |
                            less_than(ZERO, c)
                            [using specialize positiveCofactor(n, p, c) n-positive p-positive n-is-p-times-c]

                          // ---- split the cofactor: c = 1 or c > 1 ----------
                          @one-versus-c |
                            less_than(ONE, c) or ONE = c or less_than(c, ONE)
                            [using specialize trichotomy(ONE, c)]
                          @factorization |
                            exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                              is_nonneg(k)
                              and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                              (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                              and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                            case one-versus-c {
                              // c > 1: factor c by the IH, then prepend p via concat.
                              @when-c-above-one |
                                assume less_than(ONE, c) {
                                  @c-above-one |
                                    less_than(ONE, c)
                                    [by hypothesis when-c-above-one]
                                  @c-nonneg-if-positive |
                                    less_than(ZERO, c) -> is_nonneg(c)
                                    [using specialize positiveIsNonneg(c)]
                                  @c-nonneg |
                                    is_nonneg(c)
                                    [by modus_ponens c-nonneg-if-positive c-positive]
                                  // c < n = p·c, so the IH applies to c.
                                  @c-less_than-p-times-c |
                                    less_than(c, mul(p, c))
                                    [using specialize lessThanMulGreaterThanOne(p, c) p-above-one c-positive]
                                  @p-times-c-is-n |
                                    mul(p, c) = n
                                    [by symmetry n-is-p-times-c]
                                  @c-less_than-n |
                                    less_than(c, n)
                                    [by rewrite p-times-c-is-n c-less_than-p-times-c]
                                  // apply the IH: c has a prime factorization (t, j).
                                  @ih-at-c |
                                    is_nonneg(c) -> less_than(c, n) ->
                                      (less_than(ONE, c) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                                      is_nonneg(k)
                                      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                      (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                                      and c = productUpTo(s, k)))
                                    [by forall_elim(c) inductive-hypothesis]
                                  @c-has-factorization |
                                    exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                                      is_nonneg(k)
                                      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                      (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                                      and c = productUpTo(s, k))
                                    [using tautology ih-at-c c-nonneg c-less_than-n c-above-one]
                                  @with-factor-sequence |
                                    unpack t: Seq from c-has-factorization {
                                      @factorization-of-c-with-length |
                                        exists k: Int;
                                          is_nonneg(k)
                                          and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                          (less_than(ONE, at(t, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, i)))))
                                          and c = productUpTo(t, k))
                                        [by hypothesis with-factor-sequence]
                                      @with-factor-length |
                                        unpack j: Int from factorization-of-c-with-length {
                                          @factorization-of-c |
                                            is_nonneg(j)
                                              and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, j) ->
                                              (less_than(ONE, at(t, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, i)))))
                                              and c = productUpTo(t, j))
                                            [by hypothesis with-factor-length]
                                          @j-nonneg |
                                            is_nonneg(j)
                                            [by and_elim_left factorization-of-c]
                                          @primes-and-product-of-c |
                                            (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, j) ->
                                              (less_than(ONE, at(t, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, i)))))
                                              and c = productUpTo(t, j)
                                            [by and_elim_right factorization-of-c]
                                          @t-entries-prime |
                                            forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, j) ->
                                              (less_than(ONE, at(t, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, i))))
                                            [by and_elim_left primes-and-product-of-c]
                                          @c-is-t-product |
                                            c = productUpTo(t, j)
                                            [by and_elim_right primes-and-product-of-c]
                                          // singleton [p] to prepend (its index ZERO is a valid bound).
                                          @zero-nonneg-for-singleton |
                                            is_nonneg(ZERO)
                                            [by cite nonnegZero]
                                          @singleton-p-exists |
                                            exists s: Seq; at(s, ZERO) = p and productUpTo(s, ONE) = p
                                            [using specialize seqSingletonExists(p)]
                                          @with-singleton |
                                            unpack sp: Seq from singleton-p-exists {
                                              @singleton-entry-and-product |
                                                at(sp, ZERO) = p and productUpTo(sp, ONE) = p
                                                [by hypothesis with-singleton]
                                              @singleton-entry-is-p |
                                                at(sp, ZERO) = p
                                                [by and_elim_left singleton-entry-and-product]
                                              @singleton-product-is-p |
                                                productUpTo(sp, ONE) = p
                                                [by and_elim_right singleton-entry-and-product]
                                              // concatenate [p] (length 1) with t (length j).
                                              @one-plus-j-nonneg |
                                                is_nonneg(add(ONE, j))
                                                [using specialize nonnegAdd(ONE, j) one-nonneg j-nonneg]
                                              @concat-sp-t |
                                                exists u: Seq;
                                                  (forall i: sequence.NonNegZ; less_than(i, ONE) -> at(u, i) = at(sp, i))
                                                  and (forall i: sequence.NonNegZ; is_nonneg(add(ONE, i)) -> less_than(i, j) -> at(u, add(ONE, i)) = at(t, i))
                                                  and productUpTo(u, add(ONE, j)) = mul(productUpTo(sp, ONE), productUpTo(t, j))
                                                [using specialize seqConcatExists(sp, t, ONE, j) one-nonneg j-nonneg one-plus-j-nonneg]
                                              @with-concat |
                                                unpack u: Seq from concat-sp-t {
                                                  @concat-clauses |
                                                    (forall i: sequence.NonNegZ; less_than(i, ONE) -> at(u, i) = at(sp, i))
                                                      and (forall i: sequence.NonNegZ; is_nonneg(add(ONE, i)) -> less_than(i, j) -> at(u, add(ONE, i)) = at(t, i))
                                                      and productUpTo(u, add(ONE, j)) = mul(productUpTo(sp, ONE), productUpTo(t, j))
                                                    [by hypothesis with-concat]
                                                  @concat-prefix-and-shift |
                                                    (forall i: sequence.NonNegZ; less_than(i, ONE) -> at(u, i) = at(sp, i))
                                                      and (forall i: sequence.NonNegZ; is_nonneg(add(ONE, i)) -> less_than(i, j) -> at(u, add(ONE, i)) = at(t, i))
                                                    [by and_elim_left concat-clauses]
                                                  @concat-prefix |
                                                    forall i: sequence.NonNegZ; less_than(i, ONE) -> at(u, i) = at(sp, i)
                                                    [by and_elim_left concat-prefix-and-shift]
                                                  @concat-shift |
                                                    forall i: sequence.NonNegZ; is_nonneg(add(ONE, i)) -> less_than(i, j) -> at(u, add(ONE, i)) = at(t, i)
                                                    [by and_elim_right concat-prefix-and-shift]
                                                  @concat-product |
                                                    productUpTo(u, add(ONE, j)) = mul(productUpTo(sp, ONE), productUpTo(t, j))
                                                    [by and_elim_right concat-clauses]
                                                  // length k = 1 + j is nonneg.
                                                  @length-nonneg |
                                                    is_nonneg(add(ONE, j))
                                                    [using specialize nonnegAdd(ONE, j) one-nonneg j-nonneg]
                                                  // product: productUpTo(u, 1+j) = p·(productUpTo t j) = p·c = n.
                                                  @n-is-concat-product |
                                                    n = productUpTo(u, add(ONE, j))
                                                    [using chain p-times-c-is-n c-is-t-product singleton-product-is-p concat-product]
                                                  // every entry of u less_than 1+j is prime.
                                                  @entries-of-concat-prime |
                                                    fix i: Int {
                                                      @given-i-nonneg |
                                                        assume is_nonneg(i) {
                                                          @given-i-less_than-length |
                                                            assume less_than(i, add(ONE, j)) {
                                                              @i-nonneg |
                                                                is_nonneg(i)
                                                                [by hypothesis given-i-nonneg]
                                                              @i-less_than-length |
                                                                less_than(i, add(ONE, j))
                                                                [by hypothesis given-i-less_than-length]
                                                              @at-most-elim-at-one-i |
                                                                at_most(ONE, i) -> exists d: Int; is_nonneg(d) and add(ONE, d) = i
                                                                [using specialize lessOrEqualElim(ONE, i)]
                                                              // split i < 1 (the p slot) vs 1 ≤ i (a t slot).
                                                              @i-versus-one |
                                                                less_than(i, ONE) or i = ONE or less_than(ONE, i)
                                                                [using specialize trichotomy(i, ONE)]
                                                              @less_than-or-at-most |
                                                                less_than(i, ONE) or at_most(ONE, i)
                                                                case i-versus-one {
                                                                  @when-i-less_than-one |
                                                                    assume less_than(i, ONE) {
                                                                      @i-less_than-one-here |
                                                                        less_than(i, ONE)
                                                                        [by hypothesis when-i-less_than-one]
                                                                      @conclusion-less_than-or-at-most |
                                                                        less_than(i, ONE) or at_most(ONE, i)
                                                                        [by or_intro_left i-less_than-one-here]
                                                                    }
                                                                  @when-i-equals-one |
                                                                    assume i = ONE {
                                                                      @i-equals-one-here |
                                                                        i = ONE
                                                                        [by hypothesis when-i-equals-one]
                                                                      @one-equals-i |
                                                                        ONE = i
                                                                        [using arithmetic i-equals-one-here]
                                                                      @one-at-most-one |
                                                                        at_most(ONE, ONE)
                                                                        [using specialize lessOrEqualRefl(ONE)]
                                                                      @one-at-most-i |
                                                                        at_most(ONE, i)
                                                                        [by rewrite one-equals-i one-at-most-one]
                                                                      @conclusion-less_than-or-at-most |
                                                                        less_than(i, ONE) or at_most(ONE, i)
                                                                        [by or_intro_right one-at-most-i]
                                                                    }
                                                                  @when-one-less_than-i |
                                                                    assume less_than(ONE, i) {
                                                                      @one-less_than-i-here |
                                                                        less_than(ONE, i)
                                                                        [by hypothesis when-one-less_than-i]
                                                                      @one-at-most-i |
                                                                        at_most(ONE, i)
                                                                        [using specialize lessThanToLessOrEqual(ONE, i) one-less_than-i-here]
                                                                      @conclusion-less_than-or-at-most |
                                                                        less_than(i, ONE) or at_most(ONE, i)
                                                                        [by or_intro_right one-at-most-i]
                                                                    }
                                                                }
                                                              @entry-is-prime |
                                                                less_than(ONE, at(u, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i)))
                                                                case less_than-or-at-most {
                                                                  // index 0: at(u,0) = at(sp,0) = p, prime.
                                                                  @when-i-in-prefix |
                                                                    assume less_than(i, ONE) {
                                                                      @i-less_than-one |
                                                                        less_than(i, ONE)
                                                                        [by hypothesis when-i-in-prefix]
                                                                      @i-is-zero-if |
                                                                        is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ONE) -> i = ZERO
                                                                        [using specialize nonnegLessThanOneIsZero(i)]
                                                                      @i-is-zero |
                                                                        i = ZERO
                                                                        [using tautology i-is-zero-if i-nonneg i-less_than-one]
                                                                      @concat-prefix-at-i |
                                                                        is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ONE) -> at(u, i) = at(sp, i)
                                                                        [by forall_elim(i) concat-prefix]
                                                                      @u-at-i-is-sp-at-i |
                                                                        at(u, i) = at(sp, i)
                                                                        [using tautology concat-prefix-at-i i-nonneg i-less_than-one]
                                                                      // at(u, i) = at(sp, i) = at(sp, 0) = p.
                                                                      @u-at-i-is-p |
                                                                        at(u, i) = p
                                                                        [using chain u-at-i-is-sp-at-i i-is-zero singleton-entry-is-p]
                                                                      @entry-above-one |
                                                                        less_than(ONE, at(u, i))
                                                                        [by rewrite u-at-i-is-p p-above-one]
                                                                      @p-divisor-clause |
                                                                        forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)
                                                                        [by and_elim_right p-is-prime]
                                                                      @entry-divisor-clause |
                                                                        forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i))
                                                                        [by rewrite u-at-i-is-p p-divisor-clause]
                                                                      @conclusion-entry-is-prime |
                                                                        less_than(ONE, at(u, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i)))
                                                                        [by and_intro entry-above-one entry-divisor-clause]
                                                                    }
                                                                  // index ≥ 1: at(u,i) = at(t, i-1), prime by t's factorization.
                                                                  @when-i-in-shift |
                                                                    assume at_most(ONE, i) {
                                                                      @one-at-most-i |
                                                                        at_most(ONE, i)
                                                                        [by hypothesis when-i-in-shift]
                                                                      @gap-exists |
                                                                        exists d: Int; is_nonneg(d) and add(ONE, d) = i
                                                                        [by modus_ponens at-most-elim-at-one-i one-at-most-i]
                                                                      @with-gap |
                                                                        unpack g: Int from gap-exists {
                                                                          @gap-nonneg-and-reaches |
                                                                            is_nonneg(g) and add(ONE, g) = i
                                                                            [by hypothesis with-gap]
                                                                          @gap-nonneg |
                                                                            is_nonneg(g)
                                                                            [by and_elim_left gap-nonneg-and-reaches]
                                                                          @one-plus-gap-is-i |
                                                                            add(ONE, g) = i
                                                                            [by and_elim_right gap-nonneg-and-reaches]
                                                                          @i-is-one-plus-gap |
                                                                            i = add(ONE, g)
                                                                            [using arithmetic one-plus-gap-is-i]
                                                                          // g < j from add(1,g) = i < add(1,j).
                                                                          @i-less_than-length-recast |
                                                                            less_than(add(ONE, g), add(ONE, j))
                                                                            [by rewrite i-is-one-plus-gap i-less_than-length]
                                                                          @gap-less_than-j |
                                                                            less_than(g, j)
                                                                            [using specialize lessThanAddCancelLeft(ONE, g, j) i-less_than-length-recast]
                                                                          // at(u, i) = at(u, 1+g) = at(t, g).
                                                                          @one-plus-gap-nonneg |
                                                                            is_nonneg(add(ONE, g))
                                                                            [using specialize nonnegAdd(ONE, g) one-nonneg gap-nonneg]
                                                                          @concat-shift-at-g |
                                                                            is_nonneg(g) -> is_nonneg(add(ONE, g)) -> less_than(g, j) -> at(u, add(ONE, g)) = at(t, g)
                                                                            [by forall_elim(g) concat-shift]
                                                                          @u-at-one-plus-gap-is-t-at-gap |
                                                                            at(u, add(ONE, g)) = at(t, g)
                                                                            [using tautology concat-shift-at-g gap-nonneg one-plus-gap-nonneg gap-less_than-j]
                                                                          @u-at-i-is-t-at-gap |
                                                                            at(u, i) = at(t, g)
                                                                            [by rewrite one-plus-gap-is-i u-at-one-plus-gap-is-t-at-gap]
                                                                          // t's g-th entry is prime.
                                                                          @t-entry-prime-at-g |
                                                                            is_nonneg(g) -> less_than(g, j) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, g)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, g)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, g))))
                                                                            [by forall_elim(g) t-entries-prime]
                                                                          @t-gap-entry-prime |
                                                                            less_than(ONE, at(t, g)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, g)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, g)))
                                                                            [using tautology t-entry-prime-at-g gap-nonneg gap-less_than-j]
                                                                          @t-gap-above-one |
                                                                            less_than(ONE, at(t, g))
                                                                            [by and_elim_left t-gap-entry-prime]
                                                                          @t-gap-divisor-clause |
                                                                            forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, g)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, g))
                                                                            [by and_elim_right t-gap-entry-prime]
                                                                          @entry-above-one |
                                                                            less_than(ONE, at(u, i))
                                                                            [by rewrite u-at-i-is-t-at-gap t-gap-above-one]
                                                                          @entry-divisor-clause |
                                                                            forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i))
                                                                            [by rewrite u-at-i-is-t-at-gap t-gap-divisor-clause]
                                                                          @conclusion-entry-is-prime |
                                                                            less_than(ONE, at(u, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i)))
                                                                            [by and_intro entry-above-one entry-divisor-clause]
                                                                        }
                                                                      @conclusion-entry-is-prime |
                                                                        less_than(ONE, at(u, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i)))
                                                                        [by exists_elim with-gap]
                                                                    }
                                                                }
                                                            }
                                                          @i-less_than-length-implies-prime |
                                                            less_than(i, add(ONE, j)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(u, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i))))
                                                            [by implies_intro given-i-less_than-length]
                                                        }
                                                      @i-nonneg-implies-prime |
                                                        is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, add(ONE, j)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(u, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i))))
                                                        [by implies_intro given-i-nonneg]
                                                    }
                                                  @every-concat-entry-prime |
                                                    forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, add(ONE, j)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(u, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i))))
                                                    [by forall_intro entries-of-concat-prime]
                                                  // assemble the existential.
                                                  @primes-and-product |
                                                    (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, add(ONE, j)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(u, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i)))))
                                                      and n = productUpTo(u, add(ONE, j))
                                                    [by and_intro every-concat-entry-prime n-is-concat-product]
                                                  @factorization-body |
                                                    is_nonneg(add(ONE, j))
                                                      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, add(ONE, j)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(u, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i)))))
                                                      and n = productUpTo(u, add(ONE, j)))
                                                    [by and_intro length-nonneg primes-and-product]
                                                  @factorization-with-length |
                                                    exists k: Int;
                                                      is_nonneg(k)
                                                      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                                      (less_than(ONE, at(u, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(u, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(u, i)))))
                                                      and n = productUpTo(u, k))
                                                    [by exists_intro(add(ONE, j)) factorization-body]
                                                  @conclusion-factorization |
                                                    exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                                                      is_nonneg(k)
                                                      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                                      (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                                                      and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                                                    [by exists_intro(u) factorization-with-length]
                                                }
                                              @conclusion-concat-factorization |
                                                exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                                                  is_nonneg(k)
                                                  and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                                  (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                                                  and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                                                [by exists_elim with-concat]
                                            }
                                          @conclusion-singleton-factorization |
                                            exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                                              is_nonneg(k)
                                              and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                              (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                                              and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                                            [by exists_elim with-singleton]
                                        }
                                      @conclusion-factor-length |
                                        exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                                          is_nonneg(k)
                                          and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                          (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                                          and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                                        [by exists_elim with-factor-length]
                                    }
                                  @conclusion-factorization |
                                    exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                                      is_nonneg(k)
                                      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                      (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                                      and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                                    [by exists_elim with-factor-sequence]
                                }
                              // c = 1: n = p·1 = p is itself prime; use [p].
                              @when-c-equals-one |
                                assume ONE = c {
                                  @one-is-c |
                                    ONE = c
                                    [by hypothesis when-c-equals-one]
                                  @p-times-one-is-p |
                                    mul(p, ONE) = p
                                    [using specialize mulOneRight(p)]
                                  // n = p·c = p·1 = p.
                                  @n-is-p |
                                    n = p
                                    [using chain n-is-p-times-c one-is-c p-times-one-is-p]
                                  // build the singleton [p] (its index ZERO is a valid bound).
                                  @zero-nonneg-for-singleton-p |
                                    is_nonneg(ZERO)
                                    [by cite nonnegZero]
                                  @singleton-p-exists |
                                    exists s: Seq; at(s, ZERO) = p and productUpTo(s, ONE) = p
                                    [using specialize seqSingletonExists(p)]
                                  @with-singleton |
                                    unpack sp: Seq from singleton-p-exists {
                                      @singleton-entry-and-product |
                                        at(sp, ZERO) = p and productUpTo(sp, ONE) = p
                                        [by hypothesis with-singleton]
                                      @singleton-entry-is-p |
                                        at(sp, ZERO) = p
                                        [by and_elim_left singleton-entry-and-product]
                                      @singleton-product-is-p |
                                        productUpTo(sp, ONE) = p
                                        [by and_elim_right singleton-entry-and-product]
                                      // the sole entry (index 0) is p, which is prime.
                                      @entries-less_than-one-are-prime |
                                        fix i: Int {
                                          @given-i-nonneg |
                                            assume is_nonneg(i) {
                                              @given-i-less_than-one |
                                                assume less_than(i, ONE) {
                                                  @i-nonneg |
                                                    is_nonneg(i)
                                                    [by hypothesis given-i-nonneg]
                                                  @i-less_than-one |
                                                    less_than(i, ONE)
                                                    [by hypothesis given-i-less_than-one]
                                                  @i-is-zero-if |
                                                    is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ONE) -> i = ZERO
                                                    [using specialize nonnegLessThanOneIsZero(i)]
                                                  @i-is-zero |
                                                    i = ZERO
                                                    [using tautology i-is-zero-if i-nonneg i-less_than-one]
                                                  // at(sp, i) = at(sp, 0) = p.
                                                  @entry-at-i-is-p |
                                                    at(sp, i) = p
                                                    [using chain i-is-zero singleton-entry-is-p]
                                                  @entry-above-one |
                                                    less_than(ONE, at(sp, i))
                                                    [by rewrite entry-at-i-is-p p-above-one]
                                                  @p-divisor-clause |
                                                    forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)
                                                    [by and_elim_right p-is-prime]
                                                  @entry-divisor-clause |
                                                    forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(sp, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(sp, i))
                                                    [by rewrite entry-at-i-is-p p-divisor-clause]
                                                  @conclusion-entry-is-prime |
                                                    less_than(ONE, at(sp, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(sp, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(sp, i)))
                                                    [by and_intro entry-above-one entry-divisor-clause]
                                                }
                                              @i-less_than-one-implies-prime |
                                                less_than(i, ONE) -> (less_than(ONE, at(sp, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(sp, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(sp, i))))
                                                [by implies_intro given-i-less_than-one]
                                            }
                                          @i-nonneg-implies-prime |
                                            is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ONE) -> (less_than(ONE, at(sp, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(sp, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(sp, i))))
                                            [by implies_intro given-i-nonneg]
                                        }
                                      @every-entry-is-prime |
                                        forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ONE) -> (less_than(ONE, at(sp, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(sp, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(sp, i))))
                                        [by forall_intro entries-less_than-one-are-prime]
                                      // n = p = productUpTo(sp, 1).
                                      @n-is-singleton-product |
                                        n = productUpTo(sp, ONE)
                                        [using chain n-is-p singleton-product-is-p]
                                      @primes-and-product |
                                        (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ONE) -> (less_than(ONE, at(sp, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(sp, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(sp, i)))))
                                          and n = productUpTo(sp, ONE)
                                        [by and_intro every-entry-is-prime n-is-singleton-product]
                                      @factorization-body |
                                        is_nonneg(ONE)
                                          and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ONE) -> (less_than(ONE, at(sp, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(sp, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(sp, i)))))
                                          and n = productUpTo(sp, ONE))
                                        [by and_intro one-nonneg primes-and-product]
                                      @factorization-with-length |
                                        exists k: Int;
                                          is_nonneg(k)
                                          and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                          (less_than(ONE, at(sp, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(sp, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(sp, i)))))
                                          and n = productUpTo(sp, k))
                                        [by exists_intro(ONE) factorization-body]
                                      @conclusion-factorization |
                                        exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                                          is_nonneg(k)
                                          and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                          (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                                          and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                                        [by exists_intro(sp) factorization-with-length]
                                    }
                                  @conclusion-singleton-factorization |
                                    exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                                      is_nonneg(k)
                                      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                      (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                                      and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                                    [by exists_elim with-singleton]
                                }
                              @when-c-less_than-one |
                                assume less_than(c, ONE) {
                                  @c-nonneg-if-positive |
                                    less_than(ZERO, c) -> is_nonneg(c)
                                    [using specialize positiveIsNonneg(c)]
                                  @c-nonneg |
                                    is_nonneg(c)
                                    [by modus_ponens c-nonneg-if-positive c-positive]
                                  @c-less_than-one |
                                    less_than(c, ONE)
                                    [by hypothesis when-c-less_than-one]
                                  @c-is-zero |
                                    c = ZERO
                                    [using specialize nonnegLessThanOneIsZero(c) c-nonneg c-less_than-one]
                                  @zero-less_than-zero |
                                    less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                    [by rewrite c-is-zero c-positive]
                                  @zero-not-less_than-zero |
                                    not less_than(ZERO, ZERO)
                                    [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(ZERO)]
                                  @conclusion-factorization |
                                    exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                                      is_nonneg(k)
                                      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                                      (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                                      and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                                    [by absurd zero-less_than-zero zero-not-less_than-zero]
                                }
                            }
                        }
                      @conclusion-with-prime |
                        exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                          is_nonneg(k)
                          and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                          (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                          and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                        [by exists_elim with-cofactor-c]
                    }
                  @conclusion-factorization |
                    exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                      is_nonneg(k)
                      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                      (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                      and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                    [by exists_elim with-prime-factor-p]
                }
              @above-one-implies-factorization |
                less_than(ONE, n) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
                  is_nonneg(k)
                  and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
                  (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
                  and n = productUpTo(s, k))
                [by implies_intro given-n-above-one]
            }
          @ih-implies-factorization |
            (forall m: Int; is_nonneg(m) -> less_than(m, n) ->
              (less_than(ONE, m) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
              is_nonneg(k)
              and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
              (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
              and m = productUpTo(s, k))))
              -> (less_than(ONE, n) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
              is_nonneg(k)
              and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
              (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
              and n = productUpTo(s, k)))
            [by implies_intro given-inductive-hypothesis]
        }
      @strong-step-at-n |
        is_nonneg(n) ->
          (forall m: Int; is_nonneg(m) -> less_than(m, n) ->
          (less_than(ONE, m) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
          is_nonneg(k)
          and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
          (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
          and m = productUpTo(s, k))))
          -> (less_than(ONE, n) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
          is_nonneg(k)
          and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
          (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
          and n = productUpTo(s, k)))
        [by implies_intro given-n-nonneg]
    }
  @strong-step-for-all-n |
    forall n: Int; is_nonneg(n) ->
      (forall m: Int; is_nonneg(m) -> less_than(m, n) ->
      (less_than(ONE, m) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
      is_nonneg(k)
      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
      (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
      and m = productUpTo(s, k))))
      -> (less_than(ONE, n) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
      is_nonneg(k)
      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
      (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
      and n = productUpTo(s, k)))
    [by forall_intro strong-step]
  @factorization-for-nonneg |
    forall n: Int; is_nonneg(n) ->
      (less_than(ONE, n) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
      is_nonneg(k)
      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
      (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
      and n = productUpTo(s, k)))
    [using instantiation nonnegStrongInduction((fun n: Int => less_than(ONE, n) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int; is_nonneg(k) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) and n = productUpTo(s, k)))) strong-step-for-all-n]
  @discharge-less_than-one |
    fix n: Int {
      @given-n-above-one |
        assume less_than(ONE, n) {
          @n-above-one |
            less_than(ONE, n)
            [by hypothesis given-n-above-one]
          @one-less_than-n-transitive |
            less_than(ZERO, ONE) -> less_than(ONE, n) -> less_than(ZERO, n)
            [using specialize lessThanTransitive(ZERO, ONE, n)]
          @n-positive |
            less_than(ZERO, n)
            [using tautology one-less_than-n-transitive zero-less_than-one n-above-one]
          @n-nonneg |
            is_nonneg(n)
            [using specialize positiveIsNonneg(n) n-positive]
          @factorization-at-n |
            is_nonneg(n) ->
              (less_than(ONE, n) -> exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
              is_nonneg(k)
              and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
              (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
              and n = productUpTo(s, k)))
            [by forall_elim(n) factorization-for-nonneg]
          @conclusion-factorization |
            exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
              is_nonneg(k)
              and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
              (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
              and n = productUpTo(s, k))
            [using tautology factorization-at-n n-nonneg n-above-one]
        }
      @above-one-gives-factorization |
        less_than(ONE, n) ->
          exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
          is_nonneg(k)
          and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
          (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
          and n = productUpTo(s, k))
        [by implies_intro given-n-above-one]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall n: Int; less_than(ONE, n) ->
      exists s: Seq; exists k: Int;
      is_nonneg(k)
      and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) ->
      (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
      and n = productUpTo(s, k))
    [by forall_intro discharge-less_than-one]
qed
```

### Factorizations of the same product have equal length

Before the divides-based uniqueness above, we record the sharper statement that
underlies the Fundamental Theorem: two prime factorizations of the *same* product
have **the same length**. The argument is an induction on the length `k` of the
first factorization. A supporting fact we isolate first: no prime divides `1` (a
prime `p > 1` dividing `1` would force `p ≤ 1` by `divisorAtMost`, contradicting
`p > 1`) — the same reasoning already used in the base cases of
`primeDividesProductImpliesMember` and `infinitudeOfPrimes`, packaged as a lemma.

We also need that `less_than` is preserved by `succ` on both sides — if `a < b`
then `succ(a) < succ(b)` — since the induction shifts indices past a removed entry.
Read off the gap `d` witnessing `a < b` (`add(a, succ(d)) = b`), push a `succ`
through the left summand (`addSuccLeft`), and re-read it with `lessThanIntro`.

```bpa
theorem succMonotone: forall a, b: Int; less_than(a, b) -> less_than(succ(a), succ(b))
proof
  @generalize-a |
    fix a: Int {
      @generalize-b |
        fix b: Int {
          @given-a-less_than-b |
            assume less_than(a, b) {
              @a-less_than-b |
                less_than(a, b)
                [by hypothesis given-a-less_than-b]
              @gap-exists |
                exists d: Int; is_nonneg(d) and add(a, succ(d)) = b
                [using specialize lessThanElim(a, b) a-less_than-b]
              @with-gap-d |
                unpack d: Int from gap-exists {
                  @gap-nonneg-and-sums |
                    is_nonneg(d) and add(a, succ(d)) = b
                    [by hypothesis with-gap-d]
                  @gap-nonneg |
                    is_nonneg(d)
                    [by and_elim_left gap-nonneg-and-sums]
                  @gap-sums-to-b |
                    add(a, succ(d)) = b
                    [by and_elim_right gap-nonneg-and-sums]
                  @add-succ-left |
                    forall a2, b2: Int; add(succ(a2), b2) = succ(add(a2, b2))
                    [by cite addSuccLeft]
                  @succ-a-plus-gap |
                    add(succ(a), succ(d)) = succ(add(a, succ(d)))
                    [by forall_elim(a, succ(d)) add-succ-left]
                  @succ-a-plus-gap-is-succ-b |
                    add(succ(a), succ(d)) = succ(b)
                    [by rewrite gap-sums-to-b succ-a-plus-gap]
                  @less_than-intro |
                    forall a2, d2, b2: Int; is_nonneg(d2) -> add(a2, succ(d2)) = b2 -> less_than(a2, b2)
                    [by cite lessThanIntro]
                  @succ-a-less_than-succ-b-conditions |
                    is_nonneg(d) -> add(succ(a), succ(d)) = succ(b) -> less_than(succ(a), succ(b))
```
