```bpa
                    [by forall_elim(succ(a), d, succ(b)) less_than-intro]
                  @conclusion-succ-a-less_than-succ-b |
                    less_than(succ(a), succ(b))
                    [using tautology succ-a-less_than-succ-b-conditions gap-nonneg succ-a-plus-gap-is-succ-b]
                }
              @succ-a-less_than-succ-b |
                less_than(succ(a), succ(b))
                [by exists_elim with-gap-d]
            }
          @a-less_than-b-gives |
            less_than(a, b) -> less_than(succ(a), succ(b))
            [by implies_intro given-a-less_than-b]
        }
      @discharge-b |
        forall b: Int; less_than(a, b) -> less_than(succ(a), succ(b))
        [by forall_intro generalize-b]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall a, b: Int; less_than(a, b) -> less_than(succ(a), succ(b))
    [by forall_intro generalize-a]
qed
```

```bpa
theorem primeDoesNotDivideOne: forall p: Int;
  (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
  -> (not does_divide(p, ONE))
proof
  @generalize-p |
    fix p: Int {
      @given-p-prime |
        assume less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)) {
          @p-prime |
            less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))
            [by hypothesis given-p-prime]
          @one-less_than-p |
            less_than(ONE, p)
            [using tautology p-prime]
          @assume-p-divides-one |
            assume does_divide(p, ONE) {
              @p-divides-one |
                does_divide(p, ONE)
                [by hypothesis assume-p-divides-one]
              @zero-less_than-one |
                less_than(ZERO, ONE)
                [by cite zeroLessThanOne]
              @zero-less_than-one-less_than-p |
                less_than(ZERO, ONE) -> less_than(ONE, p) -> less_than(ZERO, p)
                [using specialize lessThanTransitive(ZERO, ONE, p)]
              @zero-less_than-p |
                less_than(ZERO, p)
                [using tautology zero-less_than-one-less_than-p zero-less_than-one one-less_than-p]
              @p-at-most-one-conditions |
                less_than(ZERO, p) -> less_than(ZERO, ONE) -> does_divide(p, ONE) -> at_most(p, ONE)
                [using specialize divisorAtMost(p, ONE)]
              @p-at-most-one |
                at_most(p, ONE)
                [using tautology p-at-most-one-conditions zero-less_than-p zero-less_than-one p-divides-one]
              @p-less_than-one-or-equals-one-conditions |
                at_most(p, ONE) -> (less_than(p, ONE) or p = ONE)
                [using specialize lessOrEqualSplit(p, ONE)]
              @p-less_than-one-or-equals-one |
                less_than(p, ONE) or p = ONE
                [by modus_ponens p-less_than-one-or-equals-one-conditions p-at-most-one]
              @one-not-less_than-one |
                not less_than(ONE, ONE)
                [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(ONE)]
              // both branches contradict less_than(ONE, p).
              @when-p-less_than-one |
                assume less_than(p, ONE) {
                  @p-less_than-one |
                    less_than(p, ONE)
                    [by hypothesis when-p-less_than-one]
                  @one-less_than-p-less_than-one |
                    less_than(ONE, p) -> less_than(p, ONE) -> less_than(ONE, ONE)
                    [using specialize lessThanTransitive(ONE, p, ONE)]
                  @one-less_than-one |
                    less_than(ONE, ONE)
                    [using tautology one-less_than-p-less_than-one one-less_than-p p-less_than-one]
                  @contradiction-p-below-one |
                    less_than(ONE, ONE)
                    [using tautology one-less_than-one]
                }
              @when-p-equals-one |
                assume p = ONE {
                  @p-equals-one |
                    p = ONE
                    [by hypothesis when-p-equals-one]
                  @one-less_than-one-from-p |
                    less_than(ONE, ONE)
                    [by rewrite p-equals-one one-less_than-p]
                  @contradiction-p-equals-one |
                    less_than(ONE, ONE)
                    [using tautology one-less_than-one-from-p]
                }
              @one-less_than-one-either-way |
                less_than(ONE, ONE)
                [by or_elim p-less_than-one-or-equals-one when-p-less_than-one when-p-equals-one]
              @contradiction |
                not does_divide(p, ONE)
                [by absurd one-less_than-one-either-way one-not-less_than-one]
            }
          @p-does-not-divide-one |
            does_divide(p, ONE) -> (not does_divide(p, ONE))
            [by implies_intro assume-p-divides-one]
          @not-p-divides-one |
            not does_divide(p, ONE)
            [using tautology p-does-not-divide-one]
        }
      @prime-implies-not-divides-one |
        (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
          -> (not does_divide(p, ONE))
        [by implies_intro given-p-prime]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall p: Int;
      (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p)))
      -> (not does_divide(p, ONE))
    [by forall_intro generalize-p]
qed
```

The **peel step** shared by both uniqueness inductions. Given two prime
factorizations of the same product — `s` of length `succ(k)` and `t` of length
`l` — it peels the last factor `at(s, k)`: that factor is prime and divides
`productUpTo(s, succ k) = productUpTo(t, l)`, so by
`primeDividesProductImpliesMember` it divides some prime `at(t, m)`, forcing
`at(s, k) = at(t, m)`. Then `l = succ(l')` (it is positive, as it contains the
index `m`), and removing entry `m` (`seqRemoveExists`) yields `t'` of length `l'`
with `productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t', l')` (cancel `at(s, k)` by
`mulCancelRight`) whose entries below `l'` are still prime, and whose entries
agree with `t` (copied below `m`, shifted above). Both `factorizationLengthUnique`
and `factorizationPermutationExists` open their inductive step by citing this.

```bpa
theorem factorizationPeelStep: forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) ->
  forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int;
  is_nonneg(l)
  -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
  -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))))
  -> is_nonneg(succ(k))
  -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l)
  -> (exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq;
  is_nonneg(lp)
  and (l = succ(lp)
  and (is_nonneg(mm)
  and (less_than(mm, l)
  and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm)
  and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp)
  and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j)))))
  and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i))
  and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
proof
  // peel the last factor and match it to at(t,m); remove m from t to get t2.
  @peel-step-proof |
    fix k: Int {
      @given-k-nonneg |
        assume is_nonneg(k) {
          @k-nonneg |
            is_nonneg(k)
            [by hypothesis given-k-nonneg]
          @successor-case-proof |
            fix s: Seq {
              @generalize-t-step |
                fix t: Seq {
                  @generalize-l-step |
                    fix l: Int {
                      @given-l-nonneg-step |
                        assume is_nonneg(l) {
                          @given-s-primes-step |
                            assume forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))) {
                              @given-t-primes-step |
                                assume forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))) {
                                  @given-succ-k-nonneg-step |
                                    assume is_nonneg(succ(k)) {
                                      @given-equal-products-step |
                                        assume productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) {
                                          @l-nonneg |
                                            is_nonneg(l)
                                            [by hypothesis given-l-nonneg-step]
                                          @s-primes-below-succ-k |
                                            forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                            [by hypothesis given-s-primes-step]
                                          @t-primes-below-l |
                                            forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))
                                            [by hypothesis given-t-primes-step]
                                          @equal-products |
                                            productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l)
                                            [by hypothesis given-equal-products-step]

                                          // shared: k < succ k, transitivity, positivity of at(s,k).
                                          @k-below-succ-k |
                                            less_than(k, succ(k))
                                            [using specialize lessThanSuccSelf(k)]
                                          @less_than-is-transitive |
                                            forall a, b, c: Int; less_than(a, b) -> less_than(b, c) -> less_than(a, c)
                                            [by cite lessThanTransitive]

                                          // the last factor at(s,k) is prime.
                                          @sk-prime-conditions |
                                            is_nonneg(k) -> less_than(k, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, k)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, k)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, k))))
                                            [by forall_elim(k) s-primes-below-succ-k]
                                          @sk-prime |
                                            less_than(ONE, at(s, k)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, k)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, k)))
                                            [using tautology sk-prime-conditions k-nonneg k-below-succ-k]
                                          @sk-above-one |
                                            less_than(ONE, at(s, k))
                                            [by and_elim_left sk-prime]
                                          @zero-less_than-one |
                                            less_than(ZERO, ONE)
                                            [by cite zeroLessThanOne]
                                          @zero-below-sk-conditions |
                                            less_than(ZERO, ONE) -> less_than(ONE, at(s, k)) -> less_than(ZERO, at(s, k))
                                            [by forall_elim(ZERO, ONE, at(s, k)) less_than-is-transitive]
                                          @sk-above-zero |
                                            less_than(ZERO, at(s, k))
                                            [using tautology zero-below-sk-conditions zero-less_than-one sk-above-one]

                                          // at(s,k) is the last factor, so it divides productUpTo(s, succ k) = productUpTo(t, l).
                                          @sk-divides-succ-product |
                                            does_divide(at(s, k), productUpTo(s, succ(k)))
                                            [using specialize lastEntryDividesProduct(s, k) k-nonneg]
                                          @sk-divides-prod-t |
                                            does_divide(at(s, k), productUpTo(t, l))
                                            [by rewrite equal-products sk-divides-succ-product]

                                          // crux: at(s,k) divides some entry at(t,m).
                                          @prime-divides-member-l |
                                            forall ss: Seq; forall p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(ss, l)) -> (exists i2: Int; is_nonneg(i2) and (less_than(i2, l) and does_divide(p, at(ss, i2))))
                                            [using specialize primeDividesProductImpliesMember(l) l-nonneg]
                                          @prime-divides-member-t-sk |
                                            (less_than(ONE, at(s, k)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, k)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, k)))) -> does_divide(at(s, k), productUpTo(t, l)) -> (exists i2: Int; is_nonneg(i2) and (less_than(i2, l) and does_divide(at(s, k), at(t, i2))))
                                            [by forall_elim(t, at(s, k)) prime-divides-member-l]
                                          @member-exists |
                                            exists i2: Int; is_nonneg(i2) and (less_than(i2, l) and does_divide(at(s, k), at(t, i2)))
                                            [using tautology prime-divides-member-t-sk sk-prime sk-divides-prod-t]

                                          @with-member-m |
                                            unpack m: Int from member-exists {
                                              @m-props |
                                                is_nonneg(m) and (less_than(m, l) and does_divide(at(s, k), at(t, m)))
                                                [by hypothesis with-member-m]
                                              @m-nonneg |
                                                is_nonneg(m)
                                                [using tautology m-props]
                                              @m-below-l |
                                                less_than(m, l)
                                                [using tautology m-props]
                                              @sk-divides-tm |
                                                does_divide(at(s, k), at(t, m))
                                                [using tautology m-props]

                                              // at(t,m) is prime; at(s,k) | at(t,m) with at(s,k) > 1 forces at(s,k) = at(t,m).
                                              @tm-prime-conditions |
                                                is_nonneg(m) -> less_than(m, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, m)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, m)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, m))))
                                                [by forall_elim(m) t-primes-below-l]
                                              @tm-prime |
                                                less_than(ONE, at(t, m)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, m)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, m)))
                                                [using tautology tm-prime-conditions m-nonneg m-below-l]
                                              @tm-divisor-clause |
                                                forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, m)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, m))
                                                [by and_elim_right tm-prime]
                                              @clause-at-sk |
                                                less_than(ZERO, at(s, k)) -> does_divide(at(s, k), at(t, m)) -> (at(s, k) = ONE or at(s, k) = at(t, m))
                                                [by forall_elim(at(s, k)) tm-divisor-clause]
                                              @sk-one-or-eq |
                                                at(s, k) = ONE or at(s, k) = at(t, m)
                                                [using tautology clause-at-sk sk-above-zero sk-divides-tm]
                                              @one-not-below-one |
                                                not less_than(ONE, ONE)
                                                [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(ONE)]
                                              @sk-equals-tm |
                                                at(s, k) = at(t, m)
                                                case sk-one-or-eq {
                                                  @when-sk-one |
                                                    assume at(s, k) = ONE {
                                                      @sk-is-one |
                                                        at(s, k) = ONE
                                                        [by hypothesis when-sk-one]
                                                      @one-below-one |
                                                        less_than(ONE, ONE)
                                                        [by rewrite sk-is-one sk-above-one]
                                                      @sk-equals-tm-absurd |
                                                        at(s, k) = at(t, m)
                                                        [by absurd one-below-one one-not-below-one]
                                                    }
                                                  @when-sk-eq |
                                                    assume at(s, k) = at(t, m) {
                                                      @sk-equals-tm-direct |
                                                        at(s, k) = at(t, m)
                                                        [by hypothesis when-sk-eq]
                                                    }
                                                }
                                              @tm-equals-sk |
                                                at(t, m) = at(s, k)
                                                [by symmetry sk-equals-tm]

                                              // l > 0, so l = succ(lprev) for nonneg lprev; and m < succ(lprev).
                                              @l-cases-if-nonneg |
                                                is_nonneg(l) -> (l = ZERO or (exists mm: Int; is_nonneg(mm) and l = succ(mm)))
                                                [using specialize nonnegCases(l)]
                                              @l-zero-or-successor |
                                                l = ZERO or (exists mm: Int; is_nonneg(mm) and l = succ(mm))
                                                [by modus_ponens l-cases-if-nonneg l-nonneg]
                                              @m-not-below-zero |
                                                is_nonneg(m) -> (not less_than(m, ZERO))
                                                [using specialize nonnegNotLessThanZero(m)]
                                              @l-is-successor-exists |
                                                exists mm: Int; is_nonneg(mm) and l = succ(mm)
                                                case l-zero-or-successor {
                                                  @when-l-zero |
                                                    assume l = ZERO {
                                                      @l-is-zero |
                                                        l = ZERO
                                                        [by hypothesis when-l-zero]
                                                      @m-below-zero |
                                                        less_than(m, ZERO)
                                                        [by rewrite l-is-zero m-below-l]
                                                      @m-not-below-zero-derived |
                                                        not less_than(m, ZERO)
                                                        [by modus_ponens m-not-below-zero m-nonneg]
                                                      @l-successor-absurd |
                                                        exists mm: Int; is_nonneg(mm) and l = succ(mm)
                                                        [by absurd m-below-zero m-not-below-zero-derived]
                                                    }
                                                  @when-l-successor |
                                                    assume exists mm: Int; is_nonneg(mm) and l = succ(mm) {
                                                      @l-successor-direct |
                                                        exists mm: Int; is_nonneg(mm) and l = succ(mm)
                                                        [by hypothesis when-l-successor]
                                                    }
                                                }

                                              @with-predecessor-lprev |
                                                unpack lprev: Int from l-is-successor-exists {
                                                  @lprev-nonneg-and-l-successor |
                                                    is_nonneg(lprev) and l = succ(lprev)
                                                    [by hypothesis with-predecessor-lprev]
                                                  @lprev-nonneg |
                                                    is_nonneg(lprev)
                                                    [by and_elim_left lprev-nonneg-and-l-successor]
                                                  @l-is-succ-lprev |
                                                    l = succ(lprev)
                                                    [by and_elim_right lprev-nonneg-and-l-successor]
                                                  @succ-lprev-is-l |
                                                    succ(lprev) = l
                                                    [using arithmetic l-is-succ-lprev]
                                                  @m-below-succ-lprev |
                                                    less_than(m, succ(lprev))
                                                    [by rewrite l-is-succ-lprev m-below-l]
                                                  @succ-lprev-nonneg |
                                                    is_nonneg(succ(lprev))
                                                    [using specialize nonnegSucc(lprev) lprev-nonneg]

                                                  // remove entry m from t: get t2 of length lprev.
                                                  @removed-exists |
                                                    exists r: Seq; mul(productUpTo(r, lprev), at(t, m)) = productUpTo(t, succ(lprev)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, m) -> at(r, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(m, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(r, i) = at(t, succ(i)))
                                                    [using specialize seqRemoveExists(t, lprev, m) lprev-nonneg m-nonneg succ-lprev-nonneg m-below-succ-lprev]

                                                  @with-removed-t2 |
                                                    unpack t2: Seq from removed-exists {
                                                      @t2-clauses |
                                                        mul(productUpTo(t2, lprev), at(t, m)) = productUpTo(t, succ(lprev)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, m) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(m, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, succ(i)))
                                                        [by hypothesis with-removed-t2]
                                                      @t2-product-times-tm |
                                                        mul(productUpTo(t2, lprev), at(t, m)) = productUpTo(t, succ(lprev))
                                                        [using tautology t2-clauses]
                                                      @t2-agrees-below-m |
                                                        forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, m) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, i)
                                                        [using tautology t2-clauses]
                                                      @t2-agrees-above-m |
                                                        forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(m, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, succ(i))
                                                        [using tautology t2-clauses]

                                                      // productUpTo(t, succ lprev) = productUpTo(t, l) = productUpTo(s, succ k)
                                                      //   = mul(productUpTo(s,k), at(s,k)); rewrite at(t,m) = at(s,k), cancel at(s,k).
                                                      @t2-product-times-tm-is-l-product |
                                                        mul(productUpTo(t2, lprev), at(t, m)) = productUpTo(t, l)
                                                        [by rewrite succ-lprev-is-l t2-product-times-tm]
                                                      @s-succ-product-splits |
                                                        productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = mul(productUpTo(s, k), at(s, k))
                                                        [using specialize productUpToSucc(s, k) k-nonneg]
                                                      @s-product-equals-t2-product-times-sk |
                                                        mul(productUpTo(s, k), at(s, k)) = mul(productUpTo(t2, lprev), at(s, k))
                                                        [using chain s-succ-product-splits equal-products t2-product-times-tm-is-l-product tm-equals-sk]

                                                      // cancel the positive factor at(s,k).
                                                      @cancel-right |
                                                        forall a, x, y: Int; less_than(ZERO, a) -> mul(x, a) = mul(y, a) -> x = y
                                                        [by cite mulCancelRight]
                                                      @cancel-at-sk |
                                                        less_than(ZERO, at(s, k)) -> mul(productUpTo(s, k), at(s, k)) = mul(productUpTo(t2, lprev), at(s, k)) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t2, lprev)
                                                        [by forall_elim(at(s, k), productUpTo(s, k), productUpTo(t2, lprev)) cancel-right]
                                                      @s-product-equals-t2-product |
                                                        productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t2, lprev)
                                                        [using tautology cancel-at-sk sk-above-zero s-product-equals-t2-product-times-sk]

                                                      // t2's entries below lprev are prime (agreement clauses transfer t's primality).
                                                      @lprev-below-succ-lprev |
                                                        less_than(lprev, succ(lprev))
                                                        [using specialize lessThanSuccSelf(lprev)]
                                                      @lprev-below-l |
                                                        less_than(lprev, l)
                                                        [by rewrite succ-lprev-is-l lprev-below-succ-lprev]
                                                      @t2-entries-prime-proof |
                                                        fix j: Int {
                                                          @given-j-nonneg |
                                                            assume is_nonneg(j) {
                                                              @given-j-below-lprev |
                                                                assume less_than(j, lprev) {
                                                                  @j-nonneg |
                                                                    is_nonneg(j)
                                                                    [by hypothesis given-j-nonneg]
                                                                  @j-below-lprev |
                                                                    less_than(j, lprev)
                                                                    [by hypothesis given-j-below-lprev]
                                                                  @j-below-l |
                                                                    less_than(j, l)
                                                                    [using specialize lessThanTransitive(j, lprev, l) j-below-lprev lprev-below-l]
                                                                  @j-vs-m |
                                                                    less_than(j, m) or j = m or less_than(m, j)
                                                                    [using specialize trichotomy(j, m)]
                                                                  // collapse the trichotomy to a binary split: below m, or m-at-most-j.
                                                                  @j-below-m-or-m-at-most-j |
                                                                    less_than(j, m) or at_most(m, j)
                                                                    case j-vs-m {
                                                                      @when-j-strictly-below-m |
                                                                        assume less_than(j, m) {
                                                                          @j-strictly-below-m |
                                                                            less_than(j, m)
                                                                            [by hypothesis when-j-strictly-below-m]
                                                                          @below-m-left |
                                                                            less_than(j, m) or at_most(m, j)
                                                                            [by or_intro_left j-strictly-below-m]
                                                                        }
                                                                      @when-j-equals-m |
                                                                        assume j = m {
                                                                          @j-equals-m |
                                                                            j = m
                                                                            [by hypothesis when-j-equals-m]
                                                                          @m-at-most-m |
                                                                            at_most(m, m)
                                                                            [using specialize lessOrEqualRefl(m)]
                                                                          @m-equals-j |
                                                                            m = j
                                                                            [using arithmetic j-equals-m]
                                                                          @m-at-most-j-from-equal |
                                                                            at_most(m, j)
                                                                            [by rewrite m-equals-j m-at-most-m]
                                                                          @at-most-right-from-equal |
                                                                            less_than(j, m) or at_most(m, j)
                                                                            [by or_intro_right m-at-most-j-from-equal]
                                                                        }
                                                                      @when-m-below-j |
                                                                        assume less_than(m, j) {
                                                                          @m-below-j |
                                                                            less_than(m, j)
                                                                            [by hypothesis when-m-below-j]
                                                                          @m-at-most-j-from-strict |
                                                                            at_most(m, j)
                                                                            [using specialize lessThanToLessOrEqual(m, j) m-below-j]
                                                                          @at-most-right-from-strict |
                                                                            less_than(j, m) or at_most(m, j)
                                                                            [by or_intro_right m-at-most-j-from-strict]
                                                                        }
                                                                    }
                                                                  @t2-entry-prime |
                                                                    less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j)))
                                                                    case j-below-m-or-m-at-most-j {
                                                                      @when-j-below-m |
                                                                        assume less_than(j, m) {
                                                                          @j-below-m |
                                                                            less_than(j, m)
                                                                            [by hypothesis when-j-below-m]
                                                                          @t2-agrees-at-j-conditions |
                                                                            is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, m) -> at(t2, j) = at(t, j)
                                                                            [by forall_elim(j) t2-agrees-below-m]
                                                                          @t2-agrees-at-j |
                                                                            at(t2, j) = at(t, j)
                                                                            [using tautology t2-agrees-at-j-conditions j-nonneg j-below-m]
                                                                          @tj-prime-conditions |
                                                                            is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))
                                                                            [by forall_elim(j) t-primes-below-l]
                                                                          @tj-prime |
                                                                            less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))
                                                                            [using tautology tj-prime-conditions j-nonneg j-below-l]
                                                                          @t2-entry-prime-below-m |
                                                                            less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j)))
                                                                            [by rewrite t2-agrees-at-j tj-prime]
                                                                        }
                                                                      @when-m-at-most-j |
                                                                        assume at_most(m, j) {
                                                                          @m-at-most-j |
                                                                            at_most(m, j)
                                                                            [by hypothesis when-m-at-most-j]
                                                                          @succ-j-nonneg |
                                                                            is_nonneg(succ(j))
                                                                            [using specialize nonnegSucc(j) j-nonneg]
                                                                          @t2-agrees-at-shifted-conditions |
                                                                            is_nonneg(j) -> is_nonneg(succ(j)) -> at_most(m, j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> at(t2, j) = at(t, succ(j))
                                                                            [by forall_elim(j) t2-agrees-above-m]
                                                                          @t2-agrees-at-shifted-j |
                                                                            at(t2, j) = at(t, succ(j))
                                                                            [using tautology t2-agrees-at-shifted-conditions j-nonneg succ-j-nonneg m-at-most-j j-below-lprev]
                                                                          @succ-j-below-succ-lprev |
                                                                            less_than(succ(j), succ(lprev))
                                                                            [using specialize succMonotone(j, lprev) j-below-lprev]
                                                                          @succ-j-below-l |
                                                                            less_than(succ(j), l)
                                                                            [by rewrite succ-lprev-is-l succ-j-below-succ-lprev]
                                                                          @t-shifted-prime-conditions |
                                                                            is_nonneg(succ(j)) -> less_than(succ(j), l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, succ(j))) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, succ(j))) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, succ(j)))))
                                                                            [by forall_elim(succ(j)) t-primes-below-l]
                                                                          @t-shifted-prime |
                                                                            less_than(ONE, at(t, succ(j))) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, succ(j))) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, succ(j))))
                                                                            [using tautology t-shifted-prime-conditions succ-j-nonneg succ-j-below-l]
                                                                          @t2-entry-prime-above-m |
                                                                            less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j)))
                                                                            [by rewrite t2-agrees-at-shifted-j t-shifted-prime]
                                                                        }
                                                                    }
                                                                }
                                                              @j-below-lprev-gives-prime |
                                                                less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j))))
                                                                [by implies_intro given-j-below-lprev]
                                                            }
                                                          @j-nonneg-gives-prime |
                                                            is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j))))
                                                            [by implies_intro given-j-nonneg]
                                                        }
                                                      @t2-primes-below-lprev |
                                                        forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j))))
                                                        [by forall_intro t2-entries-prime-proof]

                                                      // package the peeled data as the existential.
                                                      @peel-conjunction |
                                                        is_nonneg(lprev) and (l = succ(lprev) and (is_nonneg(m) and (less_than(m, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, m) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t2, lprev) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, m) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(m, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, succ(i))))))))))
                                                        [using tautology lprev-nonneg l-is-succ-lprev m-nonneg m-below-l sk-equals-tm s-product-equals-t2-product t2-primes-below-lprev t2-agrees-below-m t2-agrees-above-m]
                                                      @peel-exists-t2 |
                                                        exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lprev) and (l = succ(lprev) and (is_nonneg(m) and (less_than(m, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, m) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lprev) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, m) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(m, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i))))))))))
                                                        [by exists_intro(t2) peel-conjunction]
                                                      @peel-exists-m-t2 |
                                                        exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lprev) and (l = succ(lprev) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lprev) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i))))))))))
                                                        [by exists_intro(m) peel-exists-t2]
                                                      @peel-exists-all |
                                                        exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i))))))))))
                                                        [by exists_intro(lprev) peel-exists-m-t2]
                                                    }
                                                  @peel-exists-from-removal |
                                                    exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i))))))))))
                                                    [by exists_elim with-removed-t2]
                                                }
                                              @peel-exists-from-predecessor |
                                                exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i))))))))))
                                                [by exists_elim with-predecessor-lprev]
                                            }
                                          @conclusion-peel-exists |
                                            exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i))))))))))
                                            [by exists_elim with-member-m]
                                        }
                                      @equal-products-give-peel |
                                        productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                                        [by implies_intro given-equal-products-step]
                                    }
                                  @succ-k-nonneg-gives-peel |
                                    is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                                    [by implies_intro given-succ-k-nonneg-step]
                                }
                              @t-primes-give-peel |
                                (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                                [by implies_intro given-t-primes-step]
                            }
                          @s-primes-give-peel |
                            (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                            [by implies_intro given-s-primes-step]
                        }
                      @l-nonneg-gives-peel |
                        is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                        [by implies_intro given-l-nonneg-step]
                    }
                  @discharge-l-step |
                    forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                    [by forall_intro generalize-l-step]
                }
              @discharge-t-step |
                forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                [by forall_intro generalize-t-step]
            }
          @peel-step-for-s |
            forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
            [by forall_intro successor-case-proof]
        }
      @peel-step-if-k-nonneg |
        is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i))))))))))))
        [by implies_intro given-k-nonneg]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i))))))))))))
    [by forall_intro peel-step-proof]
qed
```

The length statement itself. We induct on the length `k` of the first
factorization `s`. **Base (`k = 0`):** the product is `1`; if `t` had positive
length `l = succ(l')`, its last entry `at(t, l')` is a prime dividing the product
`1` (`lastEntryDividesProduct`), impossible by `primeDoesNotDivideOne`; hence
`l = 0`. **Step (`P(k) ⇒ P(succ k)`):** the last factor `at(s, k)` is prime and
divides `productUpTo(s, succ k) = productUpTo(t, l)`, so by
`primeDividesProductImpliesMember` it divides some prime `at(t, m)`, forcing
`at(s, k) = at(t, m)`. Remove that entry from `t` (`seqRemoveExists`) to get `t'`
of length `l' = l − 1` whose product is `productUpTo(t, l)` with one factor of
`at(t, m)` peeled off; cancelling `at(s, k) = at(t, m)` (`mulCancelRight`) equates
`productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t', l')`. The removed sequence's entries below
`l'` are still prime, so the inductive hypothesis gives `k = l'`, whence
`succ k = succ l' = l`.

```bpa
theorem factorizationLengthUnique: forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) ->
  forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int;
  is_nonneg(l)
  -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
  -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))))
  -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l)
  -> k = l
proof
  // base case: k = 0. The product is ONE. If t had a last entry at(t, l'), it
  // would be a prime dividing ONE — impossible — so l = 0.
  @base-case-proof |
    assume is_nonneg(ZERO) {
      @generalize-s-base |
        fix s: Seq {
          @generalize-t-base |
            fix t: Seq {
              @generalize-l-base |
                fix l: Int {
                  @given-l-nonneg-base |
                    assume is_nonneg(l) {
                      @given-s-primes-base |
                        assume forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))) {
                          @given-t-primes-base |
                            assume forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))) {
                              @given-equal-products-base |
                                assume productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) {
                                  @l-nonneg |
                                    is_nonneg(l)
                                    [by hypothesis given-l-nonneg-base]
                                  @t-primes |
                                    forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))
                                    [by hypothesis given-t-primes-base]
                                  @equal-products |
                                    productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l)
                                    [by hypothesis given-equal-products-base]
                                  @empty-product-is-one |
                                    productUpTo(s, ZERO) = ONE
                                    [using specialize productUpToZero(s)]
                                  @t-product-is-one |
                                    productUpTo(t, l) = ONE
                                    [by rewrite equal-products empty-product-is-one]

                                  // suppose l = succ(l'); derive a contradiction.
                                  @l-cases-if-nonneg |
                                    is_nonneg(l) -> (l = ZERO or (exists m: Int; is_nonneg(m) and l = succ(m)))
                                    [using specialize nonnegCases(l)]
                                  @l-zero-or-successor |
                                    l = ZERO or (exists m: Int; is_nonneg(m) and l = succ(m))
                                    [by modus_ponens l-cases-if-nonneg l-nonneg]
                                  @l-is-zero |
                                    l = ZERO
                                    case l-zero-or-successor {
                                      @when-l-is-zero |
                                        assume l = ZERO {
                                          @conclusion-l-is-zero |
                                            l = ZERO
                                            [by hypothesis when-l-is-zero]
                                        }
                                      @when-l-is-successor |
                                        assume exists m: Int; is_nonneg(m) and l = succ(m) {
                                          @successor-exists |
                                            exists m: Int; is_nonneg(m) and l = succ(m)
                                            [by hypothesis when-l-is-successor]
                                          @with-predecessor-l-prime |
                                            unpack l_prime: Int from successor-exists {
                                              @predecessor-nonneg-and-l-successor |
                                                is_nonneg(l_prime) and l = succ(l_prime)
                                                [by hypothesis with-predecessor-l-prime]
                                              @predecessor-nonneg |
                                                is_nonneg(l_prime)
                                                [by and_elim_left predecessor-nonneg-and-l-successor]
                                              @l-is-successor |
                                                l = succ(l_prime)
                                                [by and_elim_right predecessor-nonneg-and-l-successor]
                                              @predecessor-less_than-its-successor |
                                                less_than(l_prime, succ(l_prime))
                                                [using specialize lessThanSuccSelf(l_prime)]
                                              @successor-is-l |
                                                succ(l_prime) = l
                                                [using arithmetic l-is-successor]
                                              @predecessor-less_than-l |
                                                less_than(l_prime, l)
                                                [by rewrite successor-is-l predecessor-less_than-its-successor]
                                              @last-entry-prime-conditions |
                                                is_nonneg(l_prime) -> less_than(l_prime, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, l_prime)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, l_prime)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, l_prime))))
                                                [by forall_elim(l_prime) t-primes]
                                              @last-entry-prime |
                                                less_than(ONE, at(t, l_prime)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, l_prime)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, l_prime)))
                                                [using tautology last-entry-prime-conditions predecessor-nonneg predecessor-less_than-l]
                                              // at(t, l') divides productUpTo(t, succ l') = productUpTo(t, l) = ONE.
                                              @succ-l-prime-nonneg |
                                                is_nonneg(succ(l_prime))
                                                [using specialize nonnegSucc(l_prime) predecessor-nonneg]
                                              @last-entry-divides-succ-product |
                                                does_divide(at(t, l_prime), productUpTo(t, succ(l_prime)))
                                                [using specialize lastEntryDividesProduct(t, l_prime) predecessor-nonneg]
                                              @last-entry-divides-t-product |
                                                does_divide(at(t, l_prime), productUpTo(t, l))
                                                [by rewrite successor-is-l last-entry-divides-succ-product]
                                              @last-entry-divides-one |
                                                does_divide(at(t, l_prime), ONE)
                                                [by rewrite t-product-is-one last-entry-divides-t-product]
                                              @prime-does-not-divide-one |
                                                forall p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) -> (not does_divide(p, ONE))
                                                [by cite primeDoesNotDivideOne]
                                              @last-entry-does-not-divide-one |
                                                (less_than(ONE, at(t, l_prime)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, l_prime)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, l_prime)))) -> (not does_divide(at(t, l_prime), ONE))
                                                [by forall_elim(at(t, l_prime)) prime-does-not-divide-one]
                                              @not-last-entry-divides-one |
                                                not does_divide(at(t, l_prime), ONE)
                                                [by modus_ponens last-entry-does-not-divide-one last-entry-prime]
                                              @conclusion-l-is-zero |
                                                l = ZERO
                                                [by absurd last-entry-divides-one not-last-entry-divides-one]
                                            }
                                          @conclusion-l-is-zero |
                                            l = ZERO
                                            [by exists_elim with-predecessor-l-prime]
                                        }
                                    }
                                  @zero-equals-l |
                                    ZERO = l
                                    [using arithmetic l-is-zero]
                                }
                              @equal-products-give-base |
                                productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> ZERO = l
                                [by implies_intro given-equal-products-base]
                            }
                          @t-primes-give-base |
                            (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> ZERO = l
                            [by implies_intro given-t-primes-base]
                        }
                      @s-primes-give-base |
                        (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> ZERO = l
                        [by implies_intro given-s-primes-base]
                    }
                  @l-nonneg-gives-base |
                    is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> ZERO = l
                    [by implies_intro given-l-nonneg-base]
                }
              @discharge-l-base |
                forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> ZERO = l
                [by forall_intro generalize-l-base]
            }
          @discharge-t-base |
            forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> ZERO = l
            [by forall_intro generalize-t-base]
        }
      @discharge-s-base |
        forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> ZERO = l
        [by forall_intro generalize-s-base]
    }
  @base-case |
    is_nonneg(ZERO) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> ZERO = l)
    [by implies_intro base-case-proof]

  // inductive step: from P(k) derive P(succ k).
  @induction-step |
    fix k: Int {
      @given-k-nonneg |
        assume is_nonneg(k) {
          @k-nonneg |
            is_nonneg(k)
            [by hypothesis given-k-nonneg]
          @succ-k-nonneg |
            is_nonneg(succ(k))
            [using specialize nonnegSucc(k) k-nonneg]
          @given-inductive-hypothesis |
            assume is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> k = l) {
              @guarded-inductive-hypothesis |
                is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> k = l)
                [by hypothesis given-inductive-hypothesis]
              @inductive-hypothesis |
                forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> k = l
                [by modus_ponens guarded-inductive-hypothesis k-nonneg]

              // prove P(succ k): fix s, t, l with the primality/product hypotheses at length succ k.
              @given-succ-k-nonneg |
                assume is_nonneg(succ(k)) {
                  @successor-case-proof |
                    fix s: Seq {
                      @generalize-t-step |
                        fix t: Seq {
                          @generalize-l-step |
                            fix l: Int {
                              @given-l-nonneg-step |
                                assume is_nonneg(l) {
                                  @given-s-primes-step |
                                    assume forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))) {
                                      @given-t-primes-step |
                                        assume forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))) {
                                          @given-equal-products-step |
                                            assume productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) {
                                              @l-nonneg |
                                                is_nonneg(l)
                                                [by hypothesis given-l-nonneg-step]
                                              @s-primes-below-succ-k |
                                                forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                                [by hypothesis given-s-primes-step]
                                              @t-primes-below-l |
                                                forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))
                                                [by hypothesis given-t-primes-step]
                                              @equal-products |
                                                productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l)
                                                [by hypothesis given-equal-products-step]

                                              // peel the last factor at(s,k): match it to at(t,m), remove m from t to get t2.
                                              // apply the shared peel step at all four binders in one
                                              // interleaved specialize (k↦∀k, k-nonneg↦guard, then s,t,l
                                              // ↦∀s,t,l and the four antecedents).
                                              @peeled-exists |
                                                exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i))))))))))
```
