```bpa
                                                [using specialize factorizationPeelStep(k, s, t, l) k-nonneg l-nonneg s-primes-below-succ-k t-primes-below-l succ-k-nonneg equal-products]

                                              @with-predecessor-lprev |
                                                unpack lprev: Int from peeled-exists {
                                                  @inner-exists-m-t2 |
                                                    exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lprev) and (l = succ(lprev) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lprev) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                                                    [by hypothesis with-predecessor-lprev]
                                                  @with-matched-m |
                                                    unpack m: Int from inner-exists-m-t2 {
                                                      @inner-exists-t2 |
                                                        exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lprev) and (l = succ(lprev) and (is_nonneg(m) and (less_than(m, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, m) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lprev) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, m) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(m, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                                                        [by hypothesis with-matched-m]
                                                      @with-removed-t2 |
                                                        unpack t2: Seq from inner-exists-t2 {
                                                          @peel-facts |
                                                            is_nonneg(lprev) and (l = succ(lprev) and (is_nonneg(m) and (less_than(m, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, m) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t2, lprev) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, m) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, i)) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(m, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                                                            [by hypothesis with-removed-t2]
                                                          @lprev-nonneg |
                                                            is_nonneg(lprev)
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @l-is-succ-lprev |
                                                            l = succ(lprev)
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @s-product-equals-t2-product |
                                                            productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t2, lprev)
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @t2-primes-below-lprev |
                                                            forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j))))
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @succ-lprev-is-l |
                                                            succ(lprev) = l
                                                            [using arithmetic l-is-succ-lprev]

                                                          // s's entries below k are prime (restrict the below-succ-k hypothesis).
                                                          @k-below-succ-k |
                                                            less_than(k, succ(k))
                                                            [using specialize lessThanSuccSelf(k)]
                                                          @less_than-is-transitive |
                                                            forall a, b, c: Int; less_than(a, b) -> less_than(b, c) -> less_than(a, c)
                                                            [by cite lessThanTransitive]
                                                          @s-primes-below-k-proof |
                                                            fix i: Int {
                                                              @given-i-nonneg |
                                                                assume is_nonneg(i) {
                                                                  @given-i-below-k |
                                                                    assume less_than(i, k) {
                                                                      @i-nonneg |
                                                                        is_nonneg(i)
                                                                        [by hypothesis given-i-nonneg]
                                                                      @i-below-k |
                                                                        less_than(i, k)
                                                                        [by hypothesis given-i-below-k]
                                                                      @i-below-succ-k-conditions |
                                                                        less_than(i, k) -> less_than(k, succ(k)) -> less_than(i, succ(k))
                                                                        [by forall_elim(i, k, succ(k)) less_than-is-transitive]
                                                                      @i-below-succ-k |
                                                                        less_than(i, succ(k))
                                                                        [using tautology i-below-succ-k-conditions i-below-k k-below-succ-k]
                                                                      @si-prime-conditions |
                                                                        is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                                                        [by forall_elim(i) s-primes-below-succ-k]
                                                                      @si-prime-below-k |
                                                                        less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))
                                                                        [using tautology si-prime-conditions i-nonneg i-below-succ-k]
                                                                    }
                                                                  @i-below-k-gives-prime |
                                                                    less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                                                    [by implies_intro given-i-below-k]
                                                                }
                                                              @i-nonneg-gives-prime |
                                                                is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                                                [by implies_intro given-i-nonneg]
                                                            }
                                                          @s-primes-below-k |
                                                            forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                                            [by forall_intro s-primes-below-k-proof]

                                                          // apply the inductive hypothesis to s, t2, lprev: k = lprev.
                                                          @ih-at-s |
                                                            forall t3: Seq; forall l2: Int; is_nonneg(l2) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l2) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t3, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t3, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t3, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t3, l2) -> k = l2
                                                            [by forall_elim(s) inductive-hypothesis]
                                                          @ih-at-s-t2-lprev |
                                                            is_nonneg(lprev) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t2, lprev) -> k = lprev
                                                            [by forall_elim(t2, lprev) ih-at-s]
                                                          @k-equals-lprev |
                                                            k = lprev
                                                            [using tautology ih-at-s-t2-lprev lprev-nonneg s-primes-below-k t2-primes-below-lprev s-product-equals-t2-product]

                                                          // succ(k) = succ(lprev) = l.
                                                          @succ-k-equals-l |
                                                            succ(k) = l
                                                            [using arithmetic k-equals-lprev succ-lprev-is-l]
                                                        }
                                                      @conclusion-succ-k-equals-l-from-removal |
                                                        succ(k) = l
                                                        [by exists_elim with-removed-t2]
                                                    }
                                                  @conclusion-succ-k-equals-l-from-matched |
                                                    succ(k) = l
                                                    [by exists_elim with-matched-m]
                                                }
                                              @conclusion-succ-k-equals-l |
                                                succ(k) = l
                                                [by exists_elim with-predecessor-lprev]
                                            }
                                          @equal-products-give-step |
                                            productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> succ(k) = l
                                            [by implies_intro given-equal-products-step]
                                        }
                                      @t-primes-give-step |
                                        (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> succ(k) = l
                                        [by implies_intro given-t-primes-step]
                                    }
                                  @s-primes-give-step |
                                    (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> succ(k) = l
                                    [by implies_intro given-s-primes-step]
                                }
                              @l-nonneg-gives-step |
                                is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> succ(k) = l
                                [by implies_intro given-l-nonneg-step]
                            }
                          @discharge-l-step |
                            forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> succ(k) = l
                            [by forall_intro generalize-l-step]
                        }
                      @discharge-t-step |
                        forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> succ(k) = l
                        [by forall_intro generalize-t-step]
                    }
                  @p-succ-k |
                    forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> succ(k) = l
                    [by forall_intro successor-case-proof]
                }
              @guarded-p-succ-k |
                is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> succ(k) = l)
                [by implies_intro given-succ-k-nonneg]
            }
          @inductive-hypothesis-gives-p-succ-k |
            (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> k = l)) -> (is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> succ(k) = l))
            [by implies_intro given-inductive-hypothesis]
        }
      @induction-step-at-k |
        is_nonneg(k) -> (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> k = l)) -> (is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> succ(k) = l))
        [by implies_intro given-k-nonneg]
    }
  @induction-step-for-all-k |
    forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) -> (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> k = l)) -> (is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> succ(k) = l))
    [by forall_intro induction-step]

  // nonnegInduction on the guard-inclusive predicate gives, for nonneg k, the
  // guarded predicate is_nonneg(k) -> BODY[k]; a nonneg k discharges the guard.
  @guarded-claim-on-nonnegs |
    forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) -> (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> k = l))
    [using instantiation nonnegInduction((fun k: Int => is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> k = l))) base-case induction-step-for-all-k]

  @collapse-double-guard |
    fix k: Int {
      @given-k-nonneg-final |
        assume is_nonneg(k) {
          @k-nonneg-final |
            is_nonneg(k)
            [by hypothesis given-k-nonneg-final]
          @claim-at-k |
            forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> k = l
            [using specialize guarded-claim-on-nonnegs(k) k-nonneg-final k-nonneg-final]
        }
      @k-nonneg-gives-claim |
        is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> k = l)
        [by implies_intro given-k-nonneg-final]
    }

  @conclusion |
    forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) ->
      forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int;
      is_nonneg(l)
      -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
      -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))))
      -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l)
      -> k = l
    [by forall_intro collapse-double-guard]
qed
```

The injectivity of the index map needs one small arithmetic fact: `succ` is
injective — `succ(a) = succ(b)` forces `a = b` — since `prev` inverts `succ`
(`prevSucc`). Apply `prev` to both sides and collapse each `prev(succ(·))`.

```bpa
theorem succInjective: forall a, b: Int; succ(a) = succ(b) -> a = b
proof
  @generalize-a |
    fix a: Int {
      @generalize-b |
        fix b: Int {
          @given-succ-a-equals-succ-b |
            assume succ(a) = succ(b) {
              @succ-a-equals-succ-b |
                succ(a) = succ(b)
                [by hypothesis given-succ-a-equals-succ-b]
              @prev-succ-a-is-a |
                prev(succ(a)) = a
                [using specialize prevSucc(a)]
              @prev-succ-b-is-b |
                prev(succ(b)) = b
                [using specialize prevSucc(b)]
              // a = prev(succ a) = prev(succ b) = b.
              @conclusion-a-equals-b |
                a = b
                [using chain prev-succ-a-is-a succ-a-equals-succ-b prev-succ-b-is-b]
            }
          @succ-equal-implies-equal |
            succ(a) = succ(b) -> a = b
            [by implies_intro given-succ-a-equals-succ-b]
        }
      @discharge-b |
        forall b: Int; succ(a) = succ(b) -> a = b
        [by forall_intro generalize-b]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall a, b: Int; succ(a) = succ(b) -> a = b
    [by forall_intro generalize-a]
qed
```

Two pure-integer facts about the `unremove` reindex `c ↦ (c < m ? c : succ c)`
used throughout the permutation induction below, extracted so the three
σ-clauses (range, injectivity, match) cite them instead of re-deriving. First,
the trichotomy on a value versus the removal threshold `m` only ever needs its
two collapsed branches `v < m` or `m ≤ v`:

```bpa
theorem lessThanOrAtMost: forall a, b: Int; less_than(a, b) or at_most(b, a)
proof
  @generalize-a |
    fix a: Int {
      @generalize-b |
        fix b: Int {
          @a-vs-b |
            less_than(a, b) or a = b or less_than(b, a)
            [using specialize trichotomy(a, b)]
          @a-below-b-or-b-at-most-a |
            less_than(a, b) or at_most(b, a)
            case a-vs-b {
              @when-a-below-b |
                assume less_than(a, b) {
                  @a-below-b-hypothesis |
                    less_than(a, b)
                    [by hypothesis when-a-below-b]
                  @a-below-b-on-the-left |
                    less_than(a, b) or at_most(b, a)
                    [by or_intro_left a-below-b-hypothesis]
                }
              @when-a-equals-b |
                assume a = b {
                  @a-equals-b-hypothesis |
                    a = b
                    [by hypothesis when-a-equals-b]
                  @b-at-most-b |
                    at_most(b, b)
                    [using specialize lessOrEqualRefl(b)]
                  @b-is-a |
                    b = a
                    [using arithmetic a-equals-b-hypothesis]
                  @b-at-most-a |
                    at_most(b, a)
                    [by rewrite b-is-a b-at-most-b]
                  @equal-case-on-the-right |
                    less_than(a, b) or at_most(b, a)
                    [by or_intro_right b-at-most-a]
                }
              @when-b-below-a |
                assume less_than(b, a) {
                  @b-below-a-hypothesis |
                    less_than(b, a)
                    [by hypothesis when-b-below-a]
                  @b-at-most-a-from-strict |
                    at_most(b, a)
                    [using specialize lessThanToLessOrEqual(b, a) b-below-a-hypothesis]
                  @b-below-a-on-the-right |
                    less_than(a, b) or at_most(b, a)
                    [by or_intro_right b-at-most-a-from-strict]
                }
            }
        }
      @discharge-b |
        forall b: Int; less_than(a, b) or at_most(b, a)
        [by forall_intro generalize-b]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall a, b: Int; less_than(a, b) or at_most(b, a)
    [by forall_intro generalize-a]
qed
```

Second, when a value `b` lies at or above the threshold `a`, its `unremove`
image `succ(b)` overshoots `a` strictly (`a ≤ b < succ b`) — this is why a
shifted tail entry can never equal, and never falls below, the threshold `m`:

```bpa
theorem atMostThenBelowSucc: forall a, b: Int; at_most(a, b) -> less_than(a, succ(b))
proof
  @at-most-then-below |
    forall x, y, z: Int; at_most(x, y) -> less_than(y, z) -> less_than(x, z)
    [by cite atMostThenLessThan]
  @generalize-a |
    fix a: Int {
      @generalize-b |
        fix b: Int {
          @given-a-at-most-b |
            assume at_most(a, b) {
              @a-at-most-b |
                at_most(a, b)
                [by hypothesis given-a-at-most-b]
              @b-below-its-successor |
                less_than(b, succ(b))
                [using specialize lessThanSuccSelf(b)]
              @a-below-succ-b-conditions |
                at_most(a, b) -> less_than(b, succ(b)) -> less_than(a, succ(b))
                [by forall_elim(a, b, succ(b)) at-most-then-below]
              @a-below-succ-b |
                less_than(a, succ(b))
                [using tautology a-below-succ-b-conditions a-at-most-b b-below-its-successor]
            }
          @a-at-most-b-implies-below-succ |
            at_most(a, b) -> less_than(a, succ(b))
            [by implies_intro given-a-at-most-b]
        }
      @discharge-b |
        forall b: Int; at_most(a, b) -> less_than(a, succ(b))
        [by forall_intro generalize-b]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall a, b: Int; at_most(a, b) -> less_than(a, succ(b))
    [by forall_intro generalize-a]
qed
```

The permutation itself. We induct on the length `k` of `s`, building the index
map σ explicitly. **Base (`k = 0`):** all three σ-clauses (range, injectivity,
match) quantify over `i < 0`, so they hold vacuously for any witness sequence.
**Step (`P(k) ⇒ P(succ k)`):** exactly as in `factorizationLengthUnique`, the
last factor `at(s, k)` matches some prime `at(t, m)`; remove `m` from `t` to get
`t2` of length `lprev = l − 1` with `productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t2, lprev)`,
whose entries stay prime. The inductive hypothesis yields an index map `sigma2`
matching `s`'s first `k` entries into `t2`. To lift `sigma2` from `t2`'s index
space back to `t`'s, we compose it with the *inverse* of the removal — the
`unremove` map `c ↦ (c < m ? c : succ c)` — realized as a Seq `r` by
`seqShiftPastExists` (shift every value `≥ m` up by one). Then σ is `r` with `m`
appended at index `k` (`seqConcatExists` of `r` with the singleton `[m]`):
index `i < k ↦ unremove(at(sigma2, i))` (matching `at(s, i)` through the
removal-agreement clauses), and index `k ↦ m` (matching `at(s, k) = at(t, m)`).
An index `i < succ k` splits (`lessThanSuccSplit`) into `i < k` (the reindexed
`sigma2` part) or `i = k` (the appended `m`). Range holds because `unremove`
maps `{0..lprev−1}` into `{0..l−1}\{m}` and `m < l`; injectivity because
`unremove` is injective, never hits `m`, and `sigma2` is injective.

```bpa
theorem factorizationPermutationExists: forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) ->
  forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int;
  is_nonneg(l)
  -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
  -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))))
  -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l)
  -> (exists sigma: Seq;
  (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l)))
  and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2)
  and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
proof
  // base case: k = 0. All three sigma-clauses are vacuous (nothing nonneg is
  // less_than ZERO), so ANY sequence works as the witness sigma.
  @base-case-proof |
    assume is_nonneg(ZERO) {
      @generalize-s-base |
        fix s: Seq {
          @generalize-t-base |
            fix t: Seq {
              @generalize-l-base |
                fix l: Int {
                  @given-l-nonneg-base |
                    assume is_nonneg(l) {
                      @given-s-primes-base |
                        assume forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))) {
                          @given-t-primes-base |
                            assume forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))) {
                              @given-equal-products-base |
                                assume productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) {
                                  // the singleton's length ONE is a valid (nonneg) bound.
                                  @one-nonneg |
                                    is_nonneg(ONE)
                                    [using import(sequence) nonnegOne]
                                  // a witness sequence exists (the singleton [ZERO]).
                                  @some-singleton-exists |
                                    exists sing: Seq; at(sing, ZERO) = ZERO and productUpTo(sing, ONE) = ZERO
                                    [using specialize seqSingletonExists(ZERO)]
                                  @with-witness-sigma |
                                    unpack sing: Seq from some-singleton-exists {
                                      // RANGE: vacuous — no nonneg i is less_than ZERO.
                                      @range-proof |
                                        fix i: Int {
                                          @given-i-nonneg-range |
                                            assume is_nonneg(i) {
                                              @given-i-below-zero-range |
                                                assume less_than(i, ZERO) {
                                                  @i-nonneg-range |
                                                    is_nonneg(i)
                                                    [by hypothesis given-i-nonneg-range]
                                                  @i-below-zero-range |
                                                    less_than(i, ZERO)
                                                    [by hypothesis given-i-below-zero-range]
                                                  @i-not-below-zero-range |
                                                    not less_than(i, ZERO)
                                                    [using specialize nonnegNotLessThanZero(i) i-nonneg-range]
                                                  @range-vacuous |
                                                    is_nonneg(at(sing, i)) and less_than(at(sing, i), l)
                                                    [by absurd i-below-zero-range i-not-below-zero-range]
                                                }
                                              @i-below-zero-gives-range |
                                                less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sing, i)) and less_than(at(sing, i), l))
                                                [by implies_intro given-i-below-zero-range]
                                            }
                                          @i-nonneg-gives-range |
                                            is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sing, i)) and less_than(at(sing, i), l))
                                            [by implies_intro given-i-nonneg-range]
                                        }
                                      @sigma-range |
                                        forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sing, i)) and less_than(at(sing, i), l))
                                        [by forall_intro range-proof]

                                      // INJECTIVE: vacuous — no nonneg i is less_than ZERO.
                                      @injective-proof |
                                        fix i: Int {
                                          @generalize-i2-inj |
                                            fix i2: Int {
                                              @given-i-nonneg-inj |
                                                assume is_nonneg(i) {
                                                  @given-i-below-zero-inj |
                                                    assume less_than(i, ZERO) {
                                                      @i-nonneg-inj |
                                                        is_nonneg(i)
                                                        [by hypothesis given-i-nonneg-inj]
                                                      @i-below-zero-inj |
                                                        less_than(i, ZERO)
                                                        [by hypothesis given-i-below-zero-inj]
                                                      @i-not-below-zero-inj |
                                                        not less_than(i, ZERO)
                                                        [using specialize nonnegNotLessThanZero(i) i-nonneg-inj]
                                                      @injective-vacuous |
                                                        is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sing, i) = at(sing, i2) -> i = i2
                                                        [by absurd i-below-zero-inj i-not-below-zero-inj]
                                                    }
                                                  @i-below-zero-gives-inj |
                                                    less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sing, i) = at(sing, i2) -> i = i2)
                                                    [by implies_intro given-i-below-zero-inj]
                                                }
                                              @i-nonneg-gives-inj |
                                                is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sing, i) = at(sing, i2) -> i = i2
                                                [by implies_intro given-i-nonneg-inj]
                                            }
                                          @discharge-i2-inj |
                                            forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sing, i) = at(sing, i2) -> i = i2
                                            [by forall_intro generalize-i2-inj]
                                        }
                                      @sigma-injective |
                                        forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sing, i) = at(sing, i2) -> i = i2
                                        [by forall_intro injective-proof]

                                      // MATCH: vacuous — no nonneg i is less_than ZERO.
                                      @match-proof |
                                        fix i: Int {
                                          @given-i-nonneg-match |
                                            assume is_nonneg(i) {
                                              @given-i-below-zero-match |
                                                assume less_than(i, ZERO) {
                                                  @i-nonneg-match |
                                                    is_nonneg(i)
                                                    [by hypothesis given-i-nonneg-match]
                                                  @i-below-zero-match |
                                                    less_than(i, ZERO)
                                                    [by hypothesis given-i-below-zero-match]
                                                  @i-not-below-zero-match |
                                                    not less_than(i, ZERO)
                                                    [using specialize nonnegNotLessThanZero(i) i-nonneg-match]
                                                  @match-vacuous |
                                                    is_nonneg(at(sing, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sing, i))
                                                    [by absurd i-below-zero-match i-not-below-zero-match]
                                                }
                                              @i-below-zero-gives-match |
                                                less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sing, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sing, i))
                                                [by implies_intro given-i-below-zero-match]
                                            }
                                          @i-nonneg-gives-match |
                                            is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sing, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sing, i))
                                            [by implies_intro given-i-nonneg-match]
                                        }
                                      @sigma-match |
                                        forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sing, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sing, i))
                                        [by forall_intro match-proof]

                                      @sigma-range-and-injective |
                                        (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sing, i)) and less_than(at(sing, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sing, i) = at(sing, i2) -> i = i2)
                                        [by and_intro sigma-range sigma-injective]
                                      @sigma-clauses |
                                        (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sing, i)) and less_than(at(sing, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sing, i) = at(sing, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sing, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sing, i)))
                                        [by and_intro sigma-range-and-injective sigma-match]
                                      @sigma-witnesses-base |
                                        exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))
                                        [by exists_intro(sing) sigma-clauses]
                                    }
                                  @sigma-exists-base |
                                    exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))
                                    [by exists_elim with-witness-sigma]
                                }
                              @equal-products-give-base |
                                productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                [by implies_intro given-equal-products-base]
                            }
                          @t-primes-give-base |
                            (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                            [by implies_intro given-t-primes-base]
                        }
                      @s-primes-give-base |
                        (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                        [by implies_intro given-s-primes-base]
                    }
                  @l-nonneg-gives-base |
                    is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                    [by implies_intro given-l-nonneg-base]
                }
              @discharge-l-base |
                forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                [by forall_intro generalize-l-base]
            }
          @discharge-t-base |
            forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
            [by forall_intro generalize-t-base]
        }
      @discharge-s-base |
        forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
        [by forall_intro generalize-s-base]
    }
  @base-case |
    is_nonneg(ZERO) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, ZERO) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, ZERO) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, ZERO) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))
    [by implies_intro base-case-proof]

  @induction-step |
    fix k: Int {
      @given-k-nonneg |
        assume is_nonneg(k) {
          @k-nonneg |
            is_nonneg(k)
            [by hypothesis given-k-nonneg]
          @succ-k-nonneg |
            is_nonneg(succ(k))
            [using specialize nonnegSucc(k) k-nonneg]
          @given-inductive-hypothesis |
            assume is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))) {
              @guarded-inductive-hypothesis |
                is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))
                [by hypothesis given-inductive-hypothesis]
              @inductive-hypothesis |
                forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                [by modus_ponens guarded-inductive-hypothesis k-nonneg]

              // prove P(succ k): fix s, t, l with the primality/product hypotheses at length succ k.
              @given-succ-k-nonneg |
                assume is_nonneg(succ(k)) {
                  @successor-case-proof |
                    fix s: Seq {
                      @generalize-t-step |
                        fix t: Seq {
                          @generalize-l-step |
                            fix l: Int {
                              @given-l-nonneg-step |
                                assume is_nonneg(l) {
                                  @given-s-primes-step |
                                    assume forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))) {
                                      @given-t-primes-step |
                                        assume forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))) {
                                          @given-equal-products-step |
                                            assume productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) {
                                              @l-nonneg |
                                                is_nonneg(l)
                                                [by hypothesis given-l-nonneg-step]
                                              @s-primes-below-succ-k |
                                                forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                                [by hypothesis given-s-primes-step]
                                              @t-primes-below-l |
                                                forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))
                                                [by hypothesis given-t-primes-step]
                                              @equal-products |
                                                productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l)
                                                [by hypothesis given-equal-products-step]

                                              // peel the last factor at(s,k): match it to at(t,m), remove m from t to get t2.
                                              // apply the shared peel step at all four binders in one
                                              // interleaved specialize (k↦∀k, k-nonneg↦guard, then s,t,l
                                              // ↦∀s,t,l and the four antecedents).
                                              @peeled-exists |
                                                exists lp: Int; exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lp) and (l = succ(lp) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lp) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lp) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lp) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                                                [using specialize factorizationPeelStep(k, s, t, l) k-nonneg l-nonneg s-primes-below-succ-k t-primes-below-l succ-k-nonneg equal-products]

                                              @with-predecessor-lprev |
                                                unpack lprev: Int from peeled-exists {
                                                  @inner-exists-m-t2 |
                                                    exists mm: Int; exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lprev) and (l = succ(lprev) and (is_nonneg(mm) and (less_than(mm, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, mm) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lprev) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, mm) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(mm, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                                                    [by hypothesis with-predecessor-lprev]
                                                  @with-matched-m |
                                                    unpack m: Int from inner-exists-m-t2 {
                                                      @inner-exists-t2 |
                                                        exists tt: Seq; is_nonneg(lprev) and (l = succ(lprev) and (is_nonneg(m) and (less_than(m, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, m) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(tt, lprev) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(tt, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(tt, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(tt, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, m) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, i)) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(m, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(tt, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                                                        [by hypothesis with-matched-m]
                                                      @with-removed-t2 |
                                                        unpack t2: Seq from inner-exists-t2 {
                                                          @peel-facts |
                                                            is_nonneg(lprev) and (l = succ(lprev) and (is_nonneg(m) and (less_than(m, l) and (at(s, k) = at(t, m) and (productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t2, lprev) and ((forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j))))) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, m) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, i)) and ((forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(m, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, succ(i)))))))))))
                                                            [by hypothesis with-removed-t2]
                                                          @lprev-nonneg |
                                                            is_nonneg(lprev)
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @l-is-succ-lprev |
                                                            l = succ(lprev)
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @m-nonneg |
                                                            is_nonneg(m)
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @m-below-l |
                                                            less_than(m, l)
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @sk-equals-tm |
                                                            at(s, k) = at(t, m)
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @s-product-equals-t2-product |
                                                            productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t2, lprev)
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @t2-primes-below-lprev |
                                                            forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j))))
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @t2-agrees-below-m |
                                                            forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, m) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, i)
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @t2-agrees-above-m |
                                                            forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(succ(i)) -> at_most(m, i) -> less_than(i, lprev) -> at(t2, i) = at(t, succ(i))
                                                            [using tautology peel-facts]
                                                          @succ-lprev-is-l |
                                                            succ(lprev) = l
                                                            [using arithmetic l-is-succ-lprev]

                                                          // facts for restricting s's primes below k and reindexing sigma.
                                                          @k-below-succ-k |
                                                            less_than(k, succ(k))
                                                            [using specialize lessThanSuccSelf(k)]
                                                          @less_than-is-transitive |
                                                            forall a, b, c: Int; less_than(a, b) -> less_than(b, c) -> less_than(a, c)
                                                            [by cite lessThanTransitive]
                                                          // s's entries below k are prime (restrict the below-succ-k hypothesis).
                                                          @s-primes-below-k-proof |
                                                            fix i: Int {
                                                              @given-i-nonneg |
                                                                assume is_nonneg(i) {
                                                                  @given-i-below-k |
                                                                    assume less_than(i, k) {
                                                                      @i-nonneg |
                                                                        is_nonneg(i)
                                                                        [by hypothesis given-i-nonneg]
                                                                      @i-below-k |
                                                                        less_than(i, k)
                                                                        [by hypothesis given-i-below-k]
                                                                      @i-below-succ-k-conditions |
                                                                        less_than(i, k) -> less_than(k, succ(k)) -> less_than(i, succ(k))
                                                                        [by forall_elim(i, k, succ(k)) less_than-is-transitive]
                                                                      @i-below-succ-k |
                                                                        less_than(i, succ(k))
                                                                        [using tautology i-below-succ-k-conditions i-below-k k-below-succ-k]
                                                                      @si-prime-conditions |
                                                                        is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                                                        [by forall_elim(i) s-primes-below-succ-k]
                                                                      @si-prime-below-k |
                                                                        less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))
                                                                        [using tautology si-prime-conditions i-nonneg i-below-succ-k]
                                                                    }
                                                                  @i-below-k-gives-prime |
                                                                    less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                                                    [by implies_intro given-i-below-k]
                                                                }
                                                              @i-nonneg-gives-prime |
                                                                is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                                                [by implies_intro given-i-nonneg]
                                                            }
                                                          @s-primes-below-k |
                                                            forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                                            [by forall_intro s-primes-below-k-proof]

                                                          // apply the inductive hypothesis to s, t2, lprev: an index map sigma2
                                                          // matching s's first k entries into t2 (length lprev).
                                                          @ih-at-s |
                                                            forall t3: Seq; forall l2: Int; is_nonneg(l2) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l2) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t3, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t3, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t3, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t3, l2) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l2))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t3, at(sigma, i))))
                                                            [by forall_elim(s) inductive-hypothesis]
                                                          @ih-at-s-t2-lprev |
                                                            is_nonneg(lprev) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, lprev) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t2, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t2, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t2, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t2, lprev) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), lprev))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t2, at(sigma, i))))
                                                            [by forall_elim(t2, lprev) ih-at-s]
                                                          @sigma2-exists |
                                                            exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), lprev))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t2, at(sigma, i)))
                                                            [using tautology ih-at-s-t2-lprev lprev-nonneg s-primes-below-k t2-primes-below-lprev s-product-equals-t2-product]

                                                          @with-index-map-sigma2 |
                                                            unpack sigma2: Seq from sigma2-exists {
                                                              @sigma2-clauses |
                                                                (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) and less_than(at(sigma2, i), lprev))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma2, i) = at(sigma2, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t2, at(sigma2, i)))
                                                                [by hypothesis with-index-map-sigma2]
                                                              @sigma2-range |
                                                                forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) and less_than(at(sigma2, i), lprev))
                                                                [using tautology sigma2-clauses]
                                                              @sigma2-injective |
                                                                forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma2, i) = at(sigma2, i2) -> i = i2
                                                                [using tautology sigma2-clauses]
                                                              @sigma2-match |
                                                                forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t2, at(sigma2, i))
                                                                [using tautology sigma2-clauses]

                                                              // reindex sigma2 through the inverse of the removal: r bumps every value
                                                              // >= m up by one (values < m are copied). r = unremove . sigma2.
                                                              @reindexed-exists |
                                                                exists r: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> less_than(at(sigma2, i), m) -> at(r, i) = at(sigma2, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> at_most(m, at(sigma2, i)) -> at(r, i) = succ(at(sigma2, i)))
                                                                [using specialize seqShiftPastExists(sigma2, k, m) k-nonneg m-nonneg]

                                                              @with-reindexed-r |
                                                                unpack r: Seq from reindexed-exists {
                                                                  @r-clauses |
                                                                    (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> less_than(at(sigma2, i), m) -> at(r, i) = at(sigma2, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> at_most(m, at(sigma2, i)) -> at(r, i) = succ(at(sigma2, i)))
                                                                    [by hypothesis with-reindexed-r]
                                                                  @r-copies-below-m |
                                                                    forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> less_than(at(sigma2, i), m) -> at(r, i) = at(sigma2, i)
                                                                    [using tautology r-clauses]
                                                                  @r-shifts-above-m |
                                                                    forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> at_most(m, at(sigma2, i)) -> at(r, i) = succ(at(sigma2, i))
                                                                    [using tautology r-clauses]

                                                                  // sigma = r ++ [m]: append the matched index m at position k
                                                                  // (the singleton's index ZERO and length ONE are valid bounds).
                                                                  @zero-is-nonneg-index |
                                                                    is_nonneg(ZERO)
                                                                    [by cite nonnegZero]
                                                                  @one-is-nonneg-length |
                                                                    is_nonneg(ONE)
                                                                    [using import(sequence) nonnegOne]
                                                                  @singleton-m-at-m |
                                                                    exists sm: Seq; at(sm, ZERO) = m and productUpTo(sm, ONE) = m
                                                                    [using specialize seqSingletonExists(m)]
                                                                  @with-singleton-m |
                                                                    unpack sm: Seq from singleton-m-at-m {
                                                                      @singleton-m-clauses |
                                                                        at(sm, ZERO) = m and productUpTo(sm, ONE) = m
                                                                        [by hypothesis with-singleton-m]
                                                                      @singleton-head-is-m |
                                                                        at(sm, ZERO) = m
                                                                        [by and_elim_left singleton-m-clauses]
                                                                      @one-is-succ-zero |
                                                                        ONE = succ(ZERO)
                                                                        [by cite oneIsSuccZero]
                                                                      @zero-nonneg |
                                                                        is_nonneg(ZERO)
                                                                        [by cite nonnegZero]
                                                                      @succ-zero-nonneg |
                                                                        is_nonneg(succ(ZERO))
                                                                        [using specialize nonnegSucc(ZERO) zero-nonneg]
                                                                      @succ-zero-is-one |
                                                                        succ(ZERO) = ONE
                                                                        [by symmetry one-is-succ-zero]
                                                                      @one-nonneg |
                                                                        is_nonneg(ONE)
                                                                        [by rewrite succ-zero-is-one succ-zero-nonneg]
                                                                      @add-k-one-nonneg |
                                                                        is_nonneg(add(k, ONE))
                                                                        [using specialize nonnegAdd(k, ONE) k-nonneg one-nonneg]
                                                                      @appended-exists |
                                                                        exists u: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> at(u, i) = at(r, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(add(k, i)) -> less_than(i, ONE) -> at(u, add(k, i)) = at(sm, i)) and productUpTo(u, add(k, ONE)) = mul(productUpTo(r, k), productUpTo(sm, ONE))
                                                                        [using specialize seqConcatExists(r, sm, k, ONE) k-nonneg one-nonneg add-k-one-nonneg]

                                                                      @with-permutation-sigma |
                                                                        unpack sigmaf: Seq from appended-exists {
                                                                          @sigma-concat-clauses |
```
