                                                                            (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> at(sigmaf, i) = at(r, i)) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(add(k, i)) -> less_than(i, ONE) -> at(sigmaf, add(k, i)) = at(sm, i)) and productUpTo(sigmaf, add(k, ONE)) = mul(productUpTo(r, k), productUpTo(sm, ONE))
                                                                            [by hypothesis with-permutation-sigma]
                                                                          @sigma-copies-r-below-k |
                                                                            forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> at(sigmaf, i) = at(r, i)
                                                                            [using tautology sigma-concat-clauses]
                                                                          @sigma-append-clause |
                                                                            forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> is_nonneg(add(k, i)) -> less_than(i, ONE) -> at(sigmaf, add(k, i)) = at(sm, i)
                                                                            [using tautology sigma-concat-clauses]
                                                                          // pin at(sigmaf, k) = m.
                                                                          @zero-below-one-append |
                                                                            less_than(ZERO, ONE)
                                                                            [by cite zeroLessThanOne]
                                                                          @add-k-zero-nonneg |
                                                                            is_nonneg(add(k, ZERO))
                                                                            [using specialize nonnegAdd(k, ZERO) k-nonneg zero-nonneg]
                                                                          @sigma-at-k-zero-conditions |
                                                                            is_nonneg(ZERO) -> is_nonneg(add(k, ZERO)) -> less_than(ZERO, ONE) -> at(sigmaf, add(k, ZERO)) = at(sm, ZERO)
                                                                            [by forall_elim(ZERO) sigma-append-clause]
                                                                          @sigma-at-k-plus-zero-is-singleton |
                                                                            at(sigmaf, add(k, ZERO)) = at(sm, ZERO)
                                                                            [using tautology sigma-at-k-zero-conditions zero-nonneg add-k-zero-nonneg zero-below-one-append]
                                                                          @add-k-zero-is-k |
                                                                            add(k, ZERO) = k
                                                                            [using specialize addZeroRight(k)]
                                                                          // σ(k) = σ(k+0) = sm(0) = m.
                                                                          @sigma-at-k-is-m |
                                                                            at(sigmaf, k) = m
                                                                            [using chain add-k-zero-is-k sigma-at-k-plus-zero-is-singleton singleton-head-is-m]

                                                                          // shared facts for verifying sigma's three clauses over indices < succ(k).
                                                                          @succ-preserves-order-sigma |
                                                                            forall a, b: Int; less_than(a, b) -> less_than(succ(a), succ(b))
                                                                            [by cite succMonotone]
                                                                          @successor-of-nonneg-sigma |
                                                                            forall n: Int; is_nonneg(n) -> is_nonneg(succ(n))
                                                                            [by cite nonnegSucc]
                                                                          @less_than-is-transitive-sigma |
                                                                            forall a, b, c: Int; less_than(a, b) -> less_than(b, c) -> less_than(a, c)
                                                                            [by cite lessThanTransitive]
                                                                          @less_than-is-irreflexive-sigma |
                                                                            forall a: Int; not less_than(a, a)
                                                                            [by cite lessThanIrreflexive]
                                                                          @less-than-or-at-most |
                                                                            forall a, b: Int; less_than(a, b) or at_most(b, a)
                                                                            [by cite lessThanOrAtMost]
                                                                          @at-most-then-below-succ |
                                                                            forall a, b: Int; at_most(a, b) -> less_than(a, succ(b))
                                                                            [by cite atMostThenBelowSucc]
                                                                          @m-below-l-copy |
                                                                            less_than(m, l)
                                                                            [using tautology m-below-l]

                                                                          // MATCH: at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigmaf, i)) for i < succ(k).
                                                                          @sigma-match-succ-proof |
                                                                            fix i: Int {
                                                                              @given-i-nonneg-match |
                                                                                assume is_nonneg(i) {
                                                                                  @given-i-below-succ-k-match |
                                                                                    assume less_than(i, succ(k)) {
                                                                                      @i-nonneg-match |
                                                                                        is_nonneg(i)
                                                                                        [by hypothesis given-i-nonneg-match]
                                                                                      @i-below-succ-k-match |
                                                                                        less_than(i, succ(k))
                                                                                        [by hypothesis given-i-below-succ-k-match]
                                                                                      @i-below-k-or-equal-match |
                                                                                        less_than(i, k) or i = k
                                                                                        [using specialize lessThanSuccSplit(i, k) i-nonneg-match k-nonneg i-below-succ-k-match]
                                                                                      @given-sigmaf-at-i-nonneg-match |
                                                                                        assume is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) {
                                                                                          @s-at-i-matches |
                                                                                            at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigmaf, i))
                                                                                            case i-below-k-or-equal-match {
                                                                                              @when-i-below-k-match |
                                                                                                assume less_than(i, k) {
                                                                                                  @i-below-k-match |
                                                                                                    less_than(i, k)
                                                                                                    [by hypothesis when-i-below-k-match]
                                                                                                  @sigma-at-i-is-r-conditions-match |
                                                                                                    is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> at(sigmaf, i) = at(r, i)
                                                                                                    [by forall_elim(i) sigma-copies-r-below-k]
                                                                                                  @sigma-at-i-is-r-match |
                                                                                                    at(sigmaf, i) = at(r, i)
                                                                                                    [using tautology sigma-at-i-is-r-conditions-match i-nonneg-match i-below-k-match]
                                                                                                  @sigmaf-at-i-nonneg-below-k |
                                                                                                    is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i))
                                                                                                    [by hypothesis given-sigmaf-at-i-nonneg-match]
                                                                                                  @r-at-i-nonneg-match |
                                                                                                    is_nonneg(at(r, i))
                                                                                                    [by rewrite sigma-at-i-is-r-match sigmaf-at-i-nonneg-below-k]
                                                                                                  @sigma2-at-i-range-conditions-match |
                                                                                                    is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) and less_than(at(sigma2, i), lprev))
                                                                                                    [by forall_elim(i) sigma2-range]
                                                                                                  @sigma2-at-i-in-range-match |
                                                                                                    is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) and less_than(at(sigma2, i), lprev)
                                                                                                    [using tautology sigma2-at-i-range-conditions-match i-nonneg-match i-below-k-match]
                                                                                                  @sigma2-at-i-nonneg-match |
                                                                                                    is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                    [by and_elim_left sigma2-at-i-in-range-match]
                                                                                                  @sigma2-at-i-below-lprev-match |
                                                                                                    less_than(at(sigma2, i), lprev)
                                                                                                    [by and_elim_right sigma2-at-i-in-range-match]
                                                                                                  @s-at-i-is-t2-conditions-match |
                                                                                                    is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t2, at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                    [by forall_elim(i) sigma2-match]
                                                                                                  @s-at-i-is-t2-match |
                                                                                                    at(s, i) = at(t2, at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                    [using tautology s-at-i-is-t2-conditions-match i-nonneg-match i-below-k-match sigma2-at-i-nonneg-match]
                                                                                                  // split on sigma2(i) vs m to relate at(t2, sigma2(i)) to at(t, at(r,i)).
                                                                                                  @sigma2-i-below-m-or-at-most-match |
                                                                                                    less_than(at(sigma2, i), m) or at_most(m, at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                    [by forall_elim(at(sigma2, i), m) less-than-or-at-most]
                                                                                                  @s-at-i-is-t-at-r |
                                                                                                    at(s, i) = at(t, at(r, i))
                                                                                                    case sigma2-i-below-m-or-at-most-match {
                                                                                                      @when-sigma2-below-m-match |
                                                                                                        assume less_than(at(sigma2, i), m) {
                                                                                                          @sigma2-below-m-match |
                                                                                                            less_than(at(sigma2, i), m)
                                                                                                            [by hypothesis when-sigma2-below-m-match]
                                                                                                          @t2-agrees-below-m-conditions-match |
                                                                                                            is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) -> less_than(at(sigma2, i), m) -> at(t2, at(sigma2, i)) = at(t, at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                            [by forall_elim(at(sigma2, i)) t2-agrees-below-m]
                                                                                                          @t2-at-sigma2-is-t-at-sigma2 |
                                                                                                            at(t2, at(sigma2, i)) = at(t, at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                            [using tautology t2-agrees-below-m-conditions-match sigma2-at-i-nonneg-match sigma2-below-m-match]
                                                                                                          @s-at-i-is-t-at-sigma2-below |
                                                                                                            at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                            [by rewrite t2-at-sigma2-is-t-at-sigma2 s-at-i-is-t2-match]
                                                                                                          @r-at-i-conditions-match |
                                                                                                            is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> less_than(at(sigma2, i), m) -> at(r, i) = at(sigma2, i)
                                                                                                            [by forall_elim(i) r-copies-below-m]
                                                                                                          @r-at-i-is-sigma2-match |
                                                                                                            at(r, i) = at(sigma2, i)
                                                                                                            [using tautology r-at-i-conditions-match i-nonneg-match i-below-k-match sigma2-below-m-match]
                                                                                                          @conclusion-s-t-r-below-m |
                                                                                                            at(s, i) = at(t, at(r, i))
                                                                                                            [using chain s-at-i-is-t-at-sigma2-below r-at-i-is-sigma2-match]
                                                                                                        }
                                                                                                      @when-sigma2-at-most-m-match |
                                                                                                        assume at_most(m, at(sigma2, i)) {
                                                                                                          @sigma2-at-most-m-match |
                                                                                                            at_most(m, at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                            [by hypothesis when-sigma2-at-most-m-match]
                                                                                                          @succ-sigma2-at-i-nonneg-match |
                                                                                                            is_nonneg(succ(at(sigma2, i)))
                                                                                                            [using specialize nonnegSucc(at(sigma2, i)) sigma2-at-i-nonneg-match]
                                                                                                          @t2-agrees-above-m-conditions-match |
                                                                                                            is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) -> is_nonneg(succ(at(sigma2, i))) -> at_most(m, at(sigma2, i)) -> less_than(at(sigma2, i), lprev) -> at(t2, at(sigma2, i)) = at(t, succ(at(sigma2, i)))
                                                                                                            [by forall_elim(at(sigma2, i)) t2-agrees-above-m]
                                                                                                          @t2-at-sigma2-is-t-at-succ |
                                                                                                            at(t2, at(sigma2, i)) = at(t, succ(at(sigma2, i)))
                                                                                                            [using tautology t2-agrees-above-m-conditions-match sigma2-at-i-nonneg-match succ-sigma2-at-i-nonneg-match sigma2-at-most-m-match sigma2-at-i-below-lprev-match]
                                                                                                          @s-at-i-is-t-at-succ-above |
                                                                                                            at(s, i) = at(t, succ(at(sigma2, i)))
                                                                                                            [by rewrite t2-at-sigma2-is-t-at-succ s-at-i-is-t2-match]
                                                                                                          @r-at-i-shift-conditions-match |
                                                                                                            is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> at_most(m, at(sigma2, i)) -> at(r, i) = succ(at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                            [by forall_elim(i) r-shifts-above-m]
                                                                                                          @r-at-i-is-succ-sigma2-match |
                                                                                                            at(r, i) = succ(at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                            [using tautology r-at-i-shift-conditions-match i-nonneg-match i-below-k-match sigma2-at-most-m-match]
                                                                                                          @conclusion-s-t-r-above-m |
                                                                                                            at(s, i) = at(t, at(r, i))
                                                                                                            [using chain s-at-i-is-t-at-succ-above r-at-i-is-succ-sigma2-match]
                                                                                                        }
                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                  @conclusion-match-below-k |
                                                                                                    at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigmaf, i))
                                                                                                    [using chain s-at-i-is-t-at-r sigma-at-i-is-r-match]
                                                                                                }
                                                                                              @when-i-equals-k-match |
                                                                                                assume i = k {
                                                                                                  @i-is-k-match |
                                                                                                    i = k
                                                                                                    [by hypothesis when-i-equals-k-match]
                                                                                                  @sigma-at-k-is-m-copy |
                                                                                                    at(sigmaf, k) = m
                                                                                                    [using tautology sigma-at-k-is-m]
                                                                                                  @sigma-at-k-nonneg-match |
                                                                                                    is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, k))
                                                                                                    [by rewrite sigma-at-k-is-m-copy m-nonneg]
                                                                                                  @sk-is-t-at-m |
                                                                                                    at(s, k) = at(t, m)
                                                                                                    [using tautology sk-equals-tm]
                                                                                                  @sk-is-t-at-sigma-k |
                                                                                                    at(s, k) = at(t, at(sigmaf, k))
                                                                                                    [using chain sk-is-t-at-m sigma-at-k-is-m-copy]
                                                                                                  @conclusion-match-at-k |
                                                                                                    at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigmaf, i))
                                                                                                    [using chain i-is-k-match sk-is-t-at-sigma-k]
                                                                                                }
                                                                                            }
                                                                                        }
                                                                                      @sigma-nonneg-gives-match |
                                                                                        is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigmaf, i))
                                                                                        [by implies_intro given-sigmaf-at-i-nonneg-match]
                                                                                    }
                                                                                  @i-below-succ-k-gives-match |
                                                                                    less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigmaf, i))
                                                                                    [by implies_intro given-i-below-succ-k-match]
                                                                                }
                                                                              @i-nonneg-gives-match |
                                                                                is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigmaf, i))
                                                                                [by implies_intro given-i-nonneg-match]
                                                                            }
                                                                          @sigma-match-succ |
                                                                            forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigmaf, i))
                                                                            [by forall_intro sigma-match-succ-proof]

                                                                          // RANGE: at(sigmaf, i) is nonneg and < l for i < succ(k).
                                                                          @sigma-range-succ-proof |
                                                                            fix i: Int {
                                                                              @given-i-nonneg-range |
                                                                                assume is_nonneg(i) {
                                                                                  @given-i-below-succ-k-range |
                                                                                    assume less_than(i, succ(k)) {
                                                                                      @i-nonneg-range |
                                                                                        is_nonneg(i)
                                                                                        [by hypothesis given-i-nonneg-range]
                                                                                      @i-below-succ-k-range |
                                                                                        less_than(i, succ(k))
                                                                                        [by hypothesis given-i-below-succ-k-range]
                                                                                      @i-below-k-or-equal-range |
                                                                                        less_than(i, k) or i = k
                                                                                        [using specialize lessThanSuccSplit(i, k) i-nonneg-range k-nonneg i-below-succ-k-range]
                                                                                      @sigma-at-i-in-range |
                                                                                        is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, i), l)
                                                                                        case i-below-k-or-equal-range {
                                                                                          @when-i-below-k-range |
                                                                                            assume less_than(i, k) {
                                                                                              @i-below-k-range |
                                                                                                less_than(i, k)
                                                                                                [by hypothesis when-i-below-k-range]
                                                                                              @sigma-at-i-is-r-conditions-range |
                                                                                                is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> at(sigmaf, i) = at(r, i)
                                                                                                [by forall_elim(i) sigma-copies-r-below-k]
                                                                                              @sigma-at-i-is-r-range |
                                                                                                at(sigmaf, i) = at(r, i)
                                                                                                [using tautology sigma-at-i-is-r-conditions-range i-nonneg-range i-below-k-range]
                                                                                              @sigma2-at-i-range-conditions-range |
                                                                                                is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) and less_than(at(sigma2, i), lprev))
                                                                                                [by forall_elim(i) sigma2-range]
                                                                                              @sigma2-at-i-in-range-range |
                                                                                                is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) and less_than(at(sigma2, i), lprev)
                                                                                                [using tautology sigma2-at-i-range-conditions-range i-nonneg-range i-below-k-range]
                                                                                              @sigma2-at-i-nonneg-range |
                                                                                                is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                [by and_elim_left sigma2-at-i-in-range-range]
                                                                                              @sigma2-at-i-below-lprev-range |
                                                                                                less_than(at(sigma2, i), lprev)
                                                                                                [by and_elim_right sigma2-at-i-in-range-range]
                                                                                              @sigma2-i-below-m-or-at-most-range |
                                                                                                less_than(at(sigma2, i), m) or at_most(m, at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                [by forall_elim(at(sigma2, i), m) less-than-or-at-most]
                                                                                              @sigma-at-i-in-range-below-k |
                                                                                                is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, i), l)
                                                                                                case sigma2-i-below-m-or-at-most-range {
                                                                                                  @when-sigma2-below-m-range |
                                                                                                    assume less_than(at(sigma2, i), m) {
                                                                                                      @sigma2-below-m-range |
                                                                                                        less_than(at(sigma2, i), m)
                                                                                                        [by hypothesis when-sigma2-below-m-range]
                                                                                                      @r-at-i-conditions-range |
                                                                                                        is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> less_than(at(sigma2, i), m) -> at(r, i) = at(sigma2, i)
                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(i) r-copies-below-m]
                                                                                                      @r-at-i-is-sigma2-range |
                                                                                                        at(r, i) = at(sigma2, i)
                                                                                                        [using tautology r-at-i-conditions-range i-nonneg-range i-below-k-range sigma2-below-m-range]
                                                                                                      @sigma-at-i-is-sigma2-range |
                                                                                                        at(sigmaf, i) = at(sigma2, i)
                                                                                                        [by rewrite r-at-i-is-sigma2-range sigma-at-i-is-r-range]
                                                                                                      @sigma2-below-l-transitivity-range |
                                                                                                        less_than(at(sigma2, i), m) -> less_than(m, l) -> less_than(at(sigma2, i), l)
                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(at(sigma2, i), m, l) less_than-is-transitive-sigma]
                                                                                                      @sigma2-below-l-range |
                                                                                                        less_than(at(sigma2, i), l)
                                                                                                        [using tautology sigma2-below-l-transitivity-range sigma2-below-m-range m-below-l-copy]
                                                                                                      @sigma2-in-l-range |
                                                                                                        is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) and less_than(at(sigma2, i), l)
                                                                                                        [by and_intro sigma2-at-i-nonneg-range sigma2-below-l-range]
                                                                                                      @conclusion-range-below-m |
                                                                                                        is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, i), l)
                                                                                                        [by rewrite sigma-at-i-is-sigma2-range sigma2-in-l-range]
                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                  @when-sigma2-at-most-m-range |
                                                                                                    assume at_most(m, at(sigma2, i)) {
                                                                                                      @sigma2-at-most-m-range |
                                                                                                        at_most(m, at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                        [by hypothesis when-sigma2-at-most-m-range]
                                                                                                      @r-at-i-shift-conditions-range |
                                                                                                        is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> at_most(m, at(sigma2, i)) -> at(r, i) = succ(at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(i) r-shifts-above-m]
                                                                                                      @r-at-i-is-succ-sigma2-range |
                                                                                                        at(r, i) = succ(at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                        [using tautology r-at-i-shift-conditions-range i-nonneg-range i-below-k-range sigma2-at-most-m-range]
                                                                                                      @sigma-at-i-is-succ-sigma2-range |
                                                                                                        at(sigmaf, i) = succ(at(sigma2, i))
                                                                                                        [by rewrite r-at-i-is-succ-sigma2-range sigma-at-i-is-r-range]
                                                                                                      @succ-sigma2-nonneg-conditions-range |
                                                                                                        is_nonneg(at(sigma2, i)) -> is_nonneg(succ(at(sigma2, i)))
                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(at(sigma2, i)) successor-of-nonneg-sigma]
                                                                                                      @succ-sigma2-nonneg-range |
                                                                                                        is_nonneg(succ(at(sigma2, i)))
                                                                                                        [by modus_ponens succ-sigma2-nonneg-conditions-range sigma2-at-i-nonneg-range]
                                                                                                      @succ-sigma2-below-succ-lprev-conditions-range |
                                                                                                        less_than(at(sigma2, i), lprev) -> less_than(succ(at(sigma2, i)), succ(lprev))
                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(at(sigma2, i), lprev) succ-preserves-order-sigma]
                                                                                                      @succ-sigma2-below-succ-lprev-range |
                                                                                                        less_than(succ(at(sigma2, i)), succ(lprev))
                                                                                                        [by modus_ponens succ-sigma2-below-succ-lprev-conditions-range sigma2-at-i-below-lprev-range]
                                                                                                      @succ-sigma2-below-l-range |
                                                                                                        less_than(succ(at(sigma2, i)), l)
                                                                                                        [by rewrite succ-lprev-is-l succ-sigma2-below-succ-lprev-range]
                                                                                                      @succ-sigma2-in-l-range |
                                                                                                        is_nonneg(succ(at(sigma2, i))) and less_than(succ(at(sigma2, i)), l)
                                                                                                        [by and_intro succ-sigma2-nonneg-range succ-sigma2-below-l-range]
                                                                                                      @conclusion-range-above-m |
                                                                                                        is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, i), l)
                                                                                                        [by rewrite sigma-at-i-is-succ-sigma2-range succ-sigma2-in-l-range]
                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                }
                                                                                              @conclusion-range-below-k |
                                                                                                is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, i), l)
                                                                                                [using tautology sigma-at-i-in-range-below-k]
                                                                                            }
                                                                                          @when-i-equals-k-range |
                                                                                            assume i = k {
                                                                                              @i-is-k-range |
                                                                                                i = k
                                                                                                [by hypothesis when-i-equals-k-range]
                                                                                              @sigma-at-k-is-m-range |
                                                                                                at(sigmaf, k) = m
                                                                                                [using tautology sigma-at-k-is-m]
                                                                                              @m-in-l-range |
                                                                                                is_nonneg(m) and less_than(m, l)
                                                                                                [by and_intro m-nonneg m-below-l-copy]
                                                                                              @sigma-at-k-in-range |
                                                                                                is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, k)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, k), l)
                                                                                                [by rewrite sigma-at-k-is-m-range m-in-l-range]
                                                                                              @conclusion-range-at-k |
                                                                                                is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, i), l)
                                                                                                [by rewrite i-is-k-range sigma-at-k-in-range]
                                                                                            }
                                                                                        }
                                                                                    }
                                                                                  @i-below-succ-k-gives-range |
                                                                                    less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, i), l))
                                                                                    [by implies_intro given-i-below-succ-k-range]
                                                                                }
                                                                              @i-nonneg-gives-range |
                                                                                is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, i), l))
                                                                                [by implies_intro given-i-nonneg-range]
                                                                            }
                                                                          @sigma-range-succ |
                                                                            forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, i), l))
                                                                            [by forall_intro sigma-range-succ-proof]

                                                                          // INJECTIVE: at(sigmaf, a) = at(sigmaf, b) with a, b < succ(k) forces a = b.
                                                                          // helper: for x < k, at(sigmaf, x) equals unremove(at(sigma2, x)), which is
                                                                          // never equal to m (values < m stay < m; values >= m become succ(...) > m).
                                                                          @sigma-tail-avoids-m-proof |
                                                                            fix x: Int {
                                                                              @given-x-nonneg-avoid |
                                                                                assume is_nonneg(x) {
                                                                                  @given-x-below-k-avoid |
                                                                                    assume less_than(x, k) {
                                                                                      @x-nonneg-avoid |
                                                                                        is_nonneg(x)
                                                                                        [by hypothesis given-x-nonneg-avoid]
                                                                                      @x-below-k-avoid |
                                                                                        less_than(x, k)
                                                                                        [by hypothesis given-x-below-k-avoid]
                                                                                      @sigma-at-x-is-r-conditions-avoid |
                                                                                        is_nonneg(x) -> less_than(x, k) -> at(sigmaf, x) = at(r, x)
                                                                                        [by forall_elim(x) sigma-copies-r-below-k]
                                                                                      @sigma-at-x-is-r-avoid |
                                                                                        at(sigmaf, x) = at(r, x)
                                                                                        [using tautology sigma-at-x-is-r-conditions-avoid x-nonneg-avoid x-below-k-avoid]
                                                                                      @sigma2-x-below-m-or-at-most-avoid |
                                                                                        less_than(at(sigma2, x), m) or at_most(m, at(sigma2, x))
                                                                                        [by forall_elim(at(sigma2, x), m) less-than-or-at-most]
                                                                                      @sigma-at-x-below-m-relation |
                                                                                        less_than(at(sigmaf, x), m) or less_than(m, at(sigmaf, x))
                                                                                        case sigma2-x-below-m-or-at-most-avoid {
                                                                                          @when-sigma2-below-m-avoid |
                                                                                            assume less_than(at(sigma2, x), m) {
                                                                                              @sigma2-below-m-avoid |
                                                                                                less_than(at(sigma2, x), m)
                                                                                                [by hypothesis when-sigma2-below-m-avoid]
                                                                                              @r-at-x-conditions-avoid |
                                                                                                is_nonneg(x) -> less_than(x, k) -> less_than(at(sigma2, x), m) -> at(r, x) = at(sigma2, x)
                                                                                                [by forall_elim(x) r-copies-below-m]
                                                                                              @r-at-x-is-sigma2-avoid |
                                                                                                at(r, x) = at(sigma2, x)
                                                                                                [using tautology r-at-x-conditions-avoid x-nonneg-avoid x-below-k-avoid sigma2-below-m-avoid]
                                                                                              @sigma-at-x-is-sigma2-avoid |
                                                                                                at(sigmaf, x) = at(sigma2, x)
                                                                                                [by rewrite r-at-x-is-sigma2-avoid sigma-at-x-is-r-avoid]
                                                                                              @sigma-at-x-below-m-avoid |
                                                                                                less_than(at(sigmaf, x), m)
                                                                                                [by rewrite sigma-at-x-is-sigma2-avoid sigma2-below-m-avoid]
                                                                                              @sigma-at-x-below-m-left-avoid |
                                                                                                less_than(at(sigmaf, x), m) or less_than(m, at(sigmaf, x))
                                                                                                [by or_intro_left sigma-at-x-below-m-avoid]
                                                                                            }
                                                                                          @when-sigma2-at-most-m-avoid |
                                                                                            assume at_most(m, at(sigma2, x)) {
                                                                                              @sigma2-at-most-m-avoid |
                                                                                                at_most(m, at(sigma2, x))
                                                                                                [by hypothesis when-sigma2-at-most-m-avoid]
                                                                                              @r-at-x-shift-conditions-avoid |
                                                                                                is_nonneg(x) -> less_than(x, k) -> at_most(m, at(sigma2, x)) -> at(r, x) = succ(at(sigma2, x))
                                                                                                [by forall_elim(x) r-shifts-above-m]
                                                                                              @r-at-x-is-succ-sigma2-avoid |
                                                                                                at(r, x) = succ(at(sigma2, x))
                                                                                                [using tautology r-at-x-shift-conditions-avoid x-nonneg-avoid x-below-k-avoid sigma2-at-most-m-avoid]
                                                                                              @sigma-at-x-is-succ-sigma2-avoid |
                                                                                                at(sigmaf, x) = succ(at(sigma2, x))
                                                                                                [by rewrite r-at-x-is-succ-sigma2-avoid sigma-at-x-is-r-avoid]
                                                                                              // m <= sigma2(x) < succ(sigma2(x)) = sigma(x), so m < sigma(x).
                                                                                              @m-below-succ-sigma2-conditions-avoid |
                                                                                                at_most(m, at(sigma2, x)) -> less_than(m, succ(at(sigma2, x)))
                                                                                                [by forall_elim(m, at(sigma2, x)) at-most-then-below-succ]
                                                                                              @m-below-succ-sigma2-avoid |
                                                                                                less_than(m, succ(at(sigma2, x)))
                                                                                                [by modus_ponens m-below-succ-sigma2-conditions-avoid sigma2-at-most-m-avoid]
                                                                                              @m-below-sigma-at-x-avoid |
                                                                                                less_than(m, at(sigmaf, x))
                                                                                                [by rewrite sigma-at-x-is-succ-sigma2-avoid m-below-succ-sigma2-avoid]
                                                                                              @m-below-sigma-at-x-right-avoid |
                                                                                                less_than(at(sigmaf, x), m) or less_than(m, at(sigmaf, x))
                                                                                                [by or_intro_right m-below-sigma-at-x-avoid]
                                                                                            }
                                                                                        }
                                                                                    }
                                                                                  @x-below-k-gives-relation |
                                                                                    less_than(x, k) -> (less_than(at(sigmaf, x), m) or less_than(m, at(sigmaf, x)))
                                                                                    [by implies_intro given-x-below-k-avoid]
                                                                                }
                                                                              @x-nonneg-gives-relation |
                                                                                is_nonneg(x) -> less_than(x, k) -> (less_than(at(sigmaf, x), m) or less_than(m, at(sigmaf, x)))
                                                                                [by implies_intro given-x-nonneg-avoid]
                                                                            }
                                                                          @sigma-tail-avoids-m |
                                                                            forall x: Int; is_nonneg(x) -> less_than(x, k) -> (less_than(at(sigmaf, x), m) or less_than(m, at(sigmaf, x)))
                                                                            [by forall_intro sigma-tail-avoids-m-proof]

                                                                          // helper: for x < k, sigma(x) is the injective image of sigma2(x) under unremove.
                                                                          // We recover sigma2(x) from sigma(x) via the two shift cases (used to prove
                                                                          // injectivity below by composing with sigma2's injectivity).
                                                                          @succ-is-injective |
                                                                            forall a, b: Int; succ(a) = succ(b) -> a = b
                                                                            [by cite succInjective]

                                                                          // main injectivity argument.
                                                                          @sigma-injective-succ-proof |
                                                                            fix a: Int {
                                                                              @generalize-b-inj |
                                                                                fix b: Int {
                                                                                  @given-a-nonneg-inj |
                                                                                    assume is_nonneg(a) {
                                                                                      @given-a-below-succ-k-inj |
                                                                                        assume less_than(a, succ(k)) {
                                                                                          @given-b-nonneg-inj |
                                                                                            assume is_nonneg(b) {
                                                                                              @given-b-below-succ-k-inj |
                                                                                                assume less_than(b, succ(k)) {
                                                                                                  @given-sigma-a-equals-sigma-b |
                                                                                                    assume at(sigmaf, a) = at(sigmaf, b) {
                                                                                                      @a-nonneg-inj |
                                                                                                        is_nonneg(a)
                                                                                                        [by hypothesis given-a-nonneg-inj]
                                                                                                      @a-below-succ-k-inj |
                                                                                                        less_than(a, succ(k))
                                                                                                        [by hypothesis given-a-below-succ-k-inj]
                                                                                                      @b-nonneg-inj |
                                                                                                        is_nonneg(b)
                                                                                                        [by hypothesis given-b-nonneg-inj]
                                                                                                      @b-below-succ-k-inj |
                                                                                                        less_than(b, succ(k))
                                                                                                        [by hypothesis given-b-below-succ-k-inj]
                                                                                                      @sigma-a-equals-sigma-b |
                                                                                                        at(sigmaf, a) = at(sigmaf, b)
                                                                                                        [by hypothesis given-sigma-a-equals-sigma-b]
                                                                                                      @a-below-k-or-equal-inj |
                                                                                                        less_than(a, k) or a = k
                                                                                                        [using specialize lessThanSuccSplit(a, k) a-nonneg-inj k-nonneg a-below-succ-k-inj]
                                                                                                      @b-below-k-or-equal-inj |
                                                                                                        less_than(b, k) or b = k
                                                                                                        [using specialize lessThanSuccSplit(b, k) b-nonneg-inj k-nonneg b-below-succ-k-inj]
                                                                                                      @a-equals-b |
                                                                                                        a = b
                                                                                                        case a-below-k-or-equal-inj {
                                                                                                          @when-a-below-k-inj |
                                                                                                            assume less_than(a, k) {
                                                                                                              @a-below-k-inj |
                                                                                                                less_than(a, k)
                                                                                                                [by hypothesis when-a-below-k-inj]
                                                                                                              @a-equals-b-from-a-below |
                                                                                                                a = b
                                                                                                                case b-below-k-or-equal-inj {
                                                                                                                  @when-b-below-k-inj |
                                                                                                                    assume less_than(b, k) {
                                                                                                                      @b-below-k-inj |
                                                                                                                        less_than(b, k)
                                                                                                                        [by hypothesis when-b-below-k-inj]
                                                                                                                      // both a, b < k: recover sigma2(a) = sigma2(b) from at(sigmaf,a)=at(sigmaf,b)
                                                                                                                      // (unremove is injective), then sigma2's injectivity gives a = b.
                                                                                                                      @sigma-a-is-r-conditions-core |
                                                                                                                        is_nonneg(a) -> less_than(a, k) -> at(sigmaf, a) = at(r, a)
                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(a) sigma-copies-r-below-k]
                                                                                                                      @sigma-a-is-r-core |
                                                                                                                        at(sigmaf, a) = at(r, a)
                                                                                                                        [using tautology sigma-a-is-r-conditions-core a-nonneg-inj a-below-k-inj]
                                                                                                                      @sigma-b-is-r-conditions-core |
                                                                                                                        is_nonneg(b) -> less_than(b, k) -> at(sigmaf, b) = at(r, b)
                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(b) sigma-copies-r-below-k]
                                                                                                                      @sigma-b-is-r-core |
                                                                                                                        at(sigmaf, b) = at(r, b)
                                                                                                                        [using tautology sigma-b-is-r-conditions-core b-nonneg-inj b-below-k-inj]
                                                                                                                      // r(a) = σ(a) = σ(b) = r(b).
                                                                                                                      @r-a-equals-r-b-core |
                                                                                                                        at(r, a) = at(r, b)
                                                                                                                        [using chain sigma-a-is-r-core sigma-a-equals-sigma-b sigma-b-is-r-core]
                                                                                                                      // sigma2 range facts for a and b.
                                                                                                                      // classify sigma2(a) and sigma2(b) relative to m.
                                                                                                                      @sigma2-a-below-or-at-most-core |
                                                                                                                        less_than(at(sigma2, a), m) or at_most(m, at(sigma2, a))
                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(at(sigma2, a), m) less-than-or-at-most]
                                                                                                                      @sigma2-b-below-or-at-most-core |
                                                                                                                        less_than(at(sigma2, b), m) or at_most(m, at(sigma2, b))
                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(at(sigma2, b), m) less-than-or-at-most]
                                                                                                                      // evaluate r at a and b in each case, equate, and recover sigma2(a)=sigma2(b).
                                                                                                                      @sigma2-a-equals-sigma2-b-core |
                                                                                                                        at(sigma2, a) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                        case sigma2-a-below-or-at-most-core {
                                                                                                                          @when-a-below-m-final |
                                                                                                                            assume less_than(at(sigma2, a), m) {
                                                                                                                              @a-below-m-final |
                                                                                                                                less_than(at(sigma2, a), m)
                                                                                                                                [by hypothesis when-a-below-m-final]
                                                                                                                              @r-a-copies-conditions |
                                                                                                                                is_nonneg(a) -> less_than(a, k) -> less_than(at(sigma2, a), m) -> at(r, a) = at(sigma2, a)
                                                                                                                                [by forall_elim(a) r-copies-below-m]
                                                                                                                              @r-a-is-sigma2-a-final |
                                                                                                                                at(r, a) = at(sigma2, a)
                                                                                                                                [using tautology r-a-copies-conditions a-nonneg-inj a-below-k-inj a-below-m-final]
                                                                                                                              @sigma2-a-equals-sigma2-b-a-below |
                                                                                                                                at(sigma2, a) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                                case sigma2-b-below-or-at-most-core {
                                                                                                                                  @when-b-below-m-final-ab |
                                                                                                                                    assume less_than(at(sigma2, b), m) {
                                                                                                                                      @b-below-m-final-ab |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(at(sigma2, b), m)
                                                                                                                                        [by hypothesis when-b-below-m-final-ab]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-copies-conditions-ab |
                                                                                                                                        is_nonneg(b) -> less_than(b, k) -> less_than(at(sigma2, b), m) -> at(r, b) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(b) r-copies-below-m]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-is-sigma2-b-ab |
                                                                                                                                        at(r, b) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                                        [using tautology r-b-copies-conditions-ab b-nonneg-inj b-below-k-inj b-below-m-final-ab]
                                                                                                                                      // σ2(a) = r(a) = r(b) = σ2(b).
                                                                                                                                      @conclusion-both-below |
                                                                                                                                        at(sigma2, a) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                                        [using chain r-a-is-sigma2-a-final r-a-equals-r-b-core r-b-is-sigma2-b-ab]
                                                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                                                  @when-b-at-most-m-final-ab |
                                                                                                                                    assume at_most(m, at(sigma2, b)) {
                                                                                                                                      // a below m but b at-least m: r(a)=sigma2(a)<m, r(b)=succ(sigma2(b))>m; equal is impossible.
                                                                                                                                      @b-at-most-m-final-ab |
                                                                                                                                        at_most(m, at(sigma2, b))
                                                                                                                                        [by hypothesis when-b-at-most-m-final-ab]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-shift-conditions-ab |
                                                                                                                                        is_nonneg(b) -> less_than(b, k) -> at_most(m, at(sigma2, b)) -> at(r, b) = succ(at(sigma2, b))
                                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(b) r-shifts-above-m]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-is-succ-sigma2-b-ab |
                                                                                                                                        at(r, b) = succ(at(sigma2, b))
                                                                                                                                        [using tautology r-b-shift-conditions-ab b-nonneg-inj b-below-k-inj b-at-most-m-final-ab]
                                                                                                                                      @r-a-is-sigma2-a-below-m |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(at(r, a), m)
                                                                                                                                        [by rewrite r-a-is-sigma2-a-final a-below-m-final]
                                                                                                                                      @m-below-succ-sigma2-b-conditions |
                                                                                                                                        at_most(m, at(sigma2, b)) -> less_than(m, succ(at(sigma2, b)))
                                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(m, at(sigma2, b)) at-most-then-below-succ]
                                                                                                                                      @m-below-succ-sigma2-b-ab |
