```bpa
                                                                                                                                        less_than(m, succ(at(sigma2, b)))
                                                                                                                                        [by modus_ponens m-below-succ-sigma2-b-conditions b-at-most-m-final-ab]
                                                                                                                                      @m-below-r-b-ab |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(m, at(r, b))
                                                                                                                                        [by rewrite r-b-is-succ-sigma2-b-ab m-below-succ-sigma2-b-ab]
                                                                                                                                      @r-a-below-m-then-r-b-transitivity |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(at(r, a), m) -> less_than(m, at(r, b)) -> less_than(at(r, a), at(r, b))
                                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(at(r, a), m, at(r, b)) less_than-is-transitive-sigma]
                                                                                                                                      @r-a-below-r-b-ab |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(at(r, a), at(r, b))
                                                                                                                                        [using tautology r-a-below-m-then-r-b-transitivity r-a-is-sigma2-a-below-m m-below-r-b-ab]
                                                                                                                                      @r-a-below-r-a-ab |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(at(r, a), at(r, a))
                                                                                                                                        [by rewrite r-a-equals-r-b-core r-a-below-r-b-ab]
                                                                                                                                      @r-a-not-below-r-a-ab |
                                                                                                                                        not less_than(at(r, a), at(r, a))
                                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(at(r, a)) less_than-is-irreflexive-sigma]
                                                                                                                                      @conclusion-a-below-b-above |
                                                                                                                                        at(sigma2, a) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                                        [by absurd r-a-below-r-a-ab r-a-not-below-r-a-ab]
                                                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                                                }
                                                                                                                            }
                                                                                                                          @when-a-at-most-m-final |
                                                                                                                            assume at_most(m, at(sigma2, a)) {
                                                                                                                              @a-at-most-m-final |
                                                                                                                                at_most(m, at(sigma2, a))
                                                                                                                                [by hypothesis when-a-at-most-m-final]
                                                                                                                              @r-a-shift-conditions |
                                                                                                                                is_nonneg(a) -> less_than(a, k) -> at_most(m, at(sigma2, a)) -> at(r, a) = succ(at(sigma2, a))
                                                                                                                                [by forall_elim(a) r-shifts-above-m]
                                                                                                                              @r-a-is-succ-sigma2-a-final |
                                                                                                                                at(r, a) = succ(at(sigma2, a))
                                                                                                                                [using tautology r-a-shift-conditions a-nonneg-inj a-below-k-inj a-at-most-m-final]
                                                                                                                              @sigma2-a-equals-sigma2-b-a-above |
                                                                                                                                at(sigma2, a) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                                case sigma2-b-below-or-at-most-core {
                                                                                                                                  @when-b-below-m-final-ba |
                                                                                                                                    assume less_than(at(sigma2, b), m) {
                                                                                                                                      // a at-least m but b below m: r(a)=succ(sigma2(a))>m, r(b)=sigma2(b)<m; impossible.
                                                                                                                                      @b-below-m-final-ba |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(at(sigma2, b), m)
                                                                                                                                        [by hypothesis when-b-below-m-final-ba]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-copies-conditions-ba |
                                                                                                                                        is_nonneg(b) -> less_than(b, k) -> less_than(at(sigma2, b), m) -> at(r, b) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(b) r-copies-below-m]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-is-sigma2-b-ba |
                                                                                                                                        at(r, b) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                                        [using tautology r-b-copies-conditions-ba b-nonneg-inj b-below-k-inj b-below-m-final-ba]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-below-m-ba |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(at(r, b), m)
                                                                                                                                        [by rewrite r-b-is-sigma2-b-ba b-below-m-final-ba]
                                                                                                                                      @m-below-succ-sigma2-a-conditions |
                                                                                                                                        at_most(m, at(sigma2, a)) -> less_than(m, succ(at(sigma2, a)))
                                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(m, at(sigma2, a)) at-most-then-below-succ]
                                                                                                                                      @m-below-succ-sigma2-a-ba |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(m, succ(at(sigma2, a)))
                                                                                                                                        [by modus_ponens m-below-succ-sigma2-a-conditions a-at-most-m-final]
                                                                                                                                      @m-below-r-a-ba |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(m, at(r, a))
                                                                                                                                        [by rewrite r-a-is-succ-sigma2-a-final m-below-succ-sigma2-a-ba]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-below-m-then-r-a-transitivity |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(at(r, b), m) -> less_than(m, at(r, a)) -> less_than(at(r, b), at(r, a))
                                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(at(r, b), m, at(r, a)) less_than-is-transitive-sigma]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-below-r-a-ba |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(at(r, b), at(r, a))
                                                                                                                                        [using tautology r-b-below-m-then-r-a-transitivity r-b-below-m-ba m-below-r-a-ba]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-below-r-b-ba |
                                                                                                                                        less_than(at(r, b), at(r, b))
                                                                                                                                        [by rewrite r-a-equals-r-b-core r-b-below-r-a-ba]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-not-below-r-b-ba |
                                                                                                                                        not less_than(at(r, b), at(r, b))
                                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(at(r, b)) less_than-is-irreflexive-sigma]
                                                                                                                                      @conclusion-a-above-b-below |
                                                                                                                                        at(sigma2, a) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                                        [by absurd r-b-below-r-b-ba r-b-not-below-r-b-ba]
                                                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                                                  @when-b-at-most-m-final-ba |
                                                                                                                                    assume at_most(m, at(sigma2, b)) {
                                                                                                                                      @b-at-most-m-final-ba |
                                                                                                                                        at_most(m, at(sigma2, b))
                                                                                                                                        [by hypothesis when-b-at-most-m-final-ba]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-shift-conditions-ba |
                                                                                                                                        is_nonneg(b) -> less_than(b, k) -> at_most(m, at(sigma2, b)) -> at(r, b) = succ(at(sigma2, b))
                                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(b) r-shifts-above-m]
                                                                                                                                      @r-b-is-succ-sigma2-b-ba |
                                                                                                                                        at(r, b) = succ(at(sigma2, b))
                                                                                                                                        [using tautology r-b-shift-conditions-ba b-nonneg-inj b-below-k-inj b-at-most-m-final-ba]
                                                                                                                                      // succ(σ2 a) = r(a) = r(b) = succ(σ2 b).
                                                                                                                                      @succ-sigma2-a-equals-succ-sigma2-b-ba |
                                                                                                                                        succ(at(sigma2, a)) = succ(at(sigma2, b))
                                                                                                                                        [using chain r-a-is-succ-sigma2-a-final r-a-equals-r-b-core r-b-is-succ-sigma2-b-ba]
                                                                                                                                      @succ-inj-at-sigma2-conditions |
                                                                                                                                        succ(at(sigma2, a)) = succ(at(sigma2, b)) -> at(sigma2, a) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(at(sigma2, a), at(sigma2, b)) succ-is-injective]
                                                                                                                                      @conclusion-both-above |
                                                                                                                                        at(sigma2, a) = at(sigma2, b)
                                                                                                                                        [by modus_ponens succ-inj-at-sigma2-conditions succ-sigma2-a-equals-succ-sigma2-b-ba]
                                                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                                                }
                                                                                                                            }
                                                                                                                        }
                                                                                                                      // sigma2 is injective, so a = b.
                                                                                                                      @sigma2-injective-at-a-b |
                                                                                                                        is_nonneg(a) -> less_than(a, k) -> is_nonneg(b) -> less_than(b, k) -> at(sigma2, a) = at(sigma2, b) -> a = b
                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(a, b) sigma2-injective]
                                                                                                                      @a-equals-b-both-below |
                                                                                                                        a = b
                                                                                                                        [using tautology sigma2-injective-at-a-b a-nonneg-inj a-below-k-inj b-nonneg-inj b-below-k-inj sigma2-a-equals-sigma2-b-core]

                                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                                  @when-b-equals-k-inj |
                                                                                                                    assume b = k {
                                                                                                                      @b-is-k-inj |
                                                                                                                        b = k
                                                                                                                        [by hypothesis when-b-equals-k-inj]
                                                                                                                      // σ(a) = σ(b) = σ(k) = m.
                                                                                                                      @sigma-a-is-m-inj |
                                                                                                                        at(sigmaf, a) = m
                                                                                                                        [using chain sigma-a-equals-sigma-b b-is-k-inj sigma-at-k-is-m]
                                                                                                                      @a-relation-conditions-inj |
                                                                                                                        is_nonneg(a) -> less_than(a, k) -> (less_than(at(sigmaf, a), m) or less_than(m, at(sigmaf, a)))
                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(a) sigma-tail-avoids-m]
                                                                                                                      @a-relation-inj |
                                                                                                                        less_than(at(sigmaf, a), m) or less_than(m, at(sigmaf, a))
                                                                                                                        [using tautology a-relation-conditions-inj a-nonneg-inj a-below-k-inj]
                                                                                                                      @m-below-m-from-a |
                                                                                                                        less_than(m, m) or less_than(m, m)
                                                                                                                        [by rewrite sigma-a-is-m-inj a-relation-inj]
                                                                                                                      @m-strictly-below-m-a |
                                                                                                                        less_than(m, m)
                                                                                                                        [using tautology m-below-m-from-a]
                                                                                                                      @m-not-below-m-a |
                                                                                                                        not less_than(m, m)
                                                                                                                        [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(m)]
                                                                                                                      @a-equals-b-absurd-1 |
                                                                                                                        a = b
                                                                                                                        [by absurd m-strictly-below-m-a m-not-below-m-a]
                                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                                }
                                                                                                            }
                                                                                                          @when-a-equals-k-inj |
                                                                                                            assume a = k {
                                                                                                              @a-is-k-inj |
                                                                                                                a = k
                                                                                                                [by hypothesis when-a-equals-k-inj]
                                                                                                              @a-equals-b-from-a-equals |
                                                                                                                a = b
                                                                                                                case b-below-k-or-equal-inj {
                                                                                                                  @when-b-below-k-inj-2 |
                                                                                                                    assume less_than(b, k) {
                                                                                                                      @b-below-k-inj-2 |
                                                                                                                        less_than(b, k)
                                                                                                                        [by hypothesis when-b-below-k-inj-2]
                                                                                                                      // σ(b) = σ(a) = σ(k) = m.
                                                                                                                      @sigma-b-is-m-inj-2 |
                                                                                                                        at(sigmaf, b) = m
                                                                                                                        [using chain sigma-a-equals-sigma-b a-is-k-inj sigma-at-k-is-m]
                                                                                                                      @b-relation-conditions-inj-2 |
                                                                                                                        is_nonneg(b) -> less_than(b, k) -> (less_than(at(sigmaf, b), m) or less_than(m, at(sigmaf, b)))
                                                                                                                        [by forall_elim(b) sigma-tail-avoids-m]
                                                                                                                      @b-relation-inj-2 |
                                                                                                                        less_than(at(sigmaf, b), m) or less_than(m, at(sigmaf, b))
                                                                                                                        [using tautology b-relation-conditions-inj-2 b-nonneg-inj b-below-k-inj-2]
                                                                                                                      @m-below-m-from-b |
                                                                                                                        less_than(m, m) or less_than(m, m)
                                                                                                                        [by rewrite sigma-b-is-m-inj-2 b-relation-inj-2]
                                                                                                                      @m-strictly-below-m-b |
                                                                                                                        less_than(m, m)
                                                                                                                        [using tautology m-below-m-from-b]
                                                                                                                      @m-not-below-m-b |
                                                                                                                        not less_than(m, m)
                                                                                                                        [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(m)]
                                                                                                                      @a-equals-b-absurd-2 |
                                                                                                                        a = b
                                                                                                                        [by absurd m-strictly-below-m-b m-not-below-m-b]
                                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                                  @when-b-equals-k-inj-2 |
                                                                                                                    assume b = k {
                                                                                                                      @b-is-k-inj-2 |
                                                                                                                        b = k
                                                                                                                        [by hypothesis when-b-equals-k-inj-2]
                                                                                                                      // a = k = b.
                                                                                                                      @a-equals-b-both-k |
                                                                                                                        a = b
                                                                                                                        [using arithmetic a-is-k-inj b-is-k-inj-2]
                                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                                }
                                                                                                            }
                                                                                                        }
                                                                                                    }
                                                                                                  @sigma-equal-implies-index-equal |
                                                                                                    at(sigmaf, a) = at(sigmaf, b) -> a = b
                                                                                                    [by implies_intro given-sigma-a-equals-sigma-b]
                                                                                                }
                                                                                              @b-below-succ-k-gives-inj |
                                                                                                less_than(b, succ(k)) -> at(sigmaf, a) = at(sigmaf, b) -> a = b
                                                                                                [by implies_intro given-b-below-succ-k-inj]
                                                                                            }
                                                                                          @b-nonneg-gives-inj |
                                                                                            is_nonneg(b) -> less_than(b, succ(k)) -> at(sigmaf, a) = at(sigmaf, b) -> a = b
                                                                                            [by implies_intro given-b-nonneg-inj]
                                                                                        }
                                                                                      @a-below-succ-k-gives-inj |
                                                                                        less_than(a, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(b) -> less_than(b, succ(k)) -> at(sigmaf, a) = at(sigmaf, b) -> a = b
                                                                                        [by implies_intro given-a-below-succ-k-inj]
                                                                                    }
                                                                                  @a-nonneg-gives-inj |
                                                                                    is_nonneg(a) -> less_than(a, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(b) -> less_than(b, succ(k)) -> at(sigmaf, a) = at(sigmaf, b) -> a = b
                                                                                    [by implies_intro given-a-nonneg-inj]
                                                                                }
                                                                              @discharge-b-inj |
                                                                                forall b: Int; is_nonneg(a) -> less_than(a, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(b) -> less_than(b, succ(k)) -> at(sigmaf, a) = at(sigmaf, b) -> a = b
                                                                                [by forall_intro generalize-b-inj]
                                                                            }
                                                                          @sigma-injective-succ |
                                                                            forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigmaf, i) = at(sigmaf, i2) -> i = i2
                                                                            [by forall_intro sigma-injective-succ-proof]

                                                                          // assemble the three clauses (top-level and is left-associative: (RANGE and INJ) and MATCH).
                                                                          @sigma-range-and-injective |
                                                                            (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigmaf, i) = at(sigmaf, i2) -> i = i2)
                                                                            [by and_intro sigma-range-succ sigma-injective-succ]
                                                                          @sigma-all-clauses |
                                                                            (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) and less_than(at(sigmaf, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigmaf, i) = at(sigmaf, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigmaf, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigmaf, i)))
                                                                            [by and_intro sigma-range-and-injective sigma-match-succ]
                                                                          @sigma-witnesses-succ |
                                                                            exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))
                                                                            [by exists_intro(sigmaf) sigma-all-clauses]

                                                                        }
                                                                      @sigma-exists-from-append |
                                                                        (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                                                        [by exists_elim with-permutation-sigma]
                                                                    }
                                                                  @sigma-exists-from-singleton |
                                                                    (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                                                    [by exists_elim with-singleton-m]
                                                                }
                                                              @sigma-exists-from-reindex |
                                                                (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                                                [by exists_elim with-reindexed-r]
                                                            }
                                                          @sigma-exists-from-index-map |
                                                            (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                                            [by exists_elim with-index-map-sigma2]

                                                        }
                                                      @conclusion-sigma-exists-from-removal |
                                                        (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                                        [by exists_elim with-removed-t2]
                                                    }
                                                  @conclusion-sigma-exists-from-predecessor |
                                                    (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                                    [by exists_elim with-matched-m]
                                                }
                                              @conclusion-sigma-exists |
                                                (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                                [by exists_elim with-predecessor-lprev]
                                            }
                                          @equal-products-give-step |
                                            productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                            [by implies_intro given-equal-products-step]
                                        }
                                      @t-primes-give-step |
                                        (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                        [by implies_intro given-t-primes-step]
                                    }
                                  @s-primes-give-step |
                                    (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                    [by implies_intro given-s-primes-step]
                                }
                              @l-nonneg-gives-step |
                                is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                [by implies_intro given-l-nonneg-step]
                            }
                          @discharge-l-step |
                            forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                            [by forall_intro generalize-l-step]
                        }
                      @discharge-t-step |
                        forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                        [by forall_intro generalize-t-step]
                    }
                  @p-succ-k |
                    forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                    [by forall_intro successor-case-proof]
                }
              @guarded-p-succ-k |
                is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))
                [by implies_intro given-succ-k-nonneg]
            }
          @inductive-hypothesis-gives-p-succ-k |
            (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))) -> (is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))))
            [by implies_intro given-inductive-hypothesis]
        }
      @induction-step-at-k |
        is_nonneg(k) -> (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))) -> (is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))))
        [by implies_intro given-k-nonneg]
    }
  @induction-step-for-all-k |
    forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) -> (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))) -> (is_nonneg(succ(k)) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, succ(k)) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, succ(k)) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, succ(k)) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))))
    [by forall_intro induction-step]

  // nonnegInduction on the guard-inclusive predicate gives, for nonneg k, the
  // guarded predicate is_nonneg(k) -> BODY[k]; a nonneg k discharges the guard.
  @guarded-claim-on-nonnegs |
    forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) -> (is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))))
    [using instantiation nonnegInduction((fun k: Int => is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))))) base-case induction-step-for-all-k]

  @collapse-double-guard |
    fix k: Int {
      @given-k-nonneg-final |
        assume is_nonneg(k) {
          @k-nonneg-final |
            is_nonneg(k)
            [by hypothesis given-k-nonneg-final]
          @claim-at-k |
            forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
            [using specialize guarded-claim-on-nonnegs(k) k-nonneg-final k-nonneg-final]
        }
      @k-nonneg-gives-claim |
        is_nonneg(k) -> (forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))
        [by implies_intro given-k-nonneg-final]
    }

  @conclusion |
    forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) ->
      forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int;
      is_nonneg(l)
      -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
      -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))))
      -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l)
      -> (exists sigma: Seq;
      (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l)))
      and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2)
      and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
    [by forall_intro collapse-double-guard]
qed
```


### Uniqueness

Judson's uniqueness — "if `n = q₁⋯qₗ` is a second factorization into primes, then
`k = l` and the `qᵢ` are, after reordering, the `pᵢ`" — is proved **in full**, in
three parts culminating in `ftaUniquenessFull` below.

The core is that **every prime in one factorization appears in the other**: given
two prime factorizations of the same product `s` (length `k`) and `t` (length
`l`), each entry `at(s, i)` is prime and divides `productUpTo(s, k) =
productUpTo(t, l)` (`everyEntryDividesProduct`), so by
`primeDividesProductImpliesMember` it divides some `at(t, j)`; and a prime
dividing a prime equals it (the primality clause of `at(t, j)`, since
`at(s, i) > 1`). Hence `at(s, i) = at(t, j)` — each factor of `s` is a factor of
`t` (theorem `ftaUniqueness`).

That recurrence is strengthened two ways, both by induction on `k` that peels the
last factor `at(s, k)`, matches it to some `at(t, m)` via the crux, deletes that
entry with `seqRemoveExists`, and recurses on the shortened `t`:

- **equal length** — `k = l` (theorem `factorizationLengthUnique`): the peeled
  primes cancel one-for-one (`mulCancelRight`), so the shorter products stay equal
  and the induction bottoms out only when both lengths hit zero.
- **an explicit reordering** — a bijection `σ` (a `Seq` of indices) with `at(s, i)
  = at(t, σ(i))`, injective and into `t`'s index range (theorem
  `factorizationPermutationExists`): the recursion records the matched index at
  each step, lifting the sub-factorization's map back through the deletion with
  `seqShiftPastExists` (the removal-inverse reindex) and appending the new match.

The permutation `σ` is exactly Judson's "after reordering." Together with `k = l`
it is multiset equality: the two factorizations are the same primes with the same
multiplicities, related by a concrete index bijection — no `Seq`-permutation
*predicate* is needed, since `σ` is produced as an ordinary existentially-bound
sequence.

```bpa
theorem ftaUniqueness: forall s: Seq; forall k: Int; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int;
  is_nonneg(k) -> is_nonneg(l)
  -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
  -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))))
  -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l)
  -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j: Int; is_nonneg(j) and (less_than(j, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j))))
proof
  @generalize-s |
    fix s: Seq {
      @generalize-k |
        fix k: Int {
          @generalize-t |
            fix t: Seq {
              @generalize-l |
                fix l: Int {
                  @given-k-nonneg |
                    assume is_nonneg(k) {
                      @given-l-nonneg |
                        assume is_nonneg(l) {
                          @given-s-primes |
                            assume forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))) {
                              @given-t-primes |
                                assume forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))) {
                                  @given-equal-products |
                                    assume productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) {
                                      @per-index |
                                        fix i: Int {
                                          @given-i-nonneg |
                                            assume is_nonneg(i) {
                                              @given-i-less_than-k |
                                                assume less_than(i, k) {
                                                  @k-nonneg |
                                                    is_nonneg(k)
                                                    [by hypothesis given-k-nonneg]
                                                  @l-nonneg |
                                                    is_nonneg(l)
                                                    [by hypothesis given-l-nonneg]
                                                  @i-nonneg |
                                                    is_nonneg(i)
                                                    [by hypothesis given-i-nonneg]
                                                  @i-less_than-k |
                                                    less_than(i, k)
                                                    [by hypothesis given-i-less_than-k]
                                                  @s-primes |
                                                    forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i2)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i2)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i2))))
                                                    [by hypothesis given-s-primes]
                                                  @t-primes |
                                                    forall j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) -> less_than(j2, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j2)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j2)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j2))))
                                                    [by hypothesis given-t-primes]
                                                  @equal-products |
                                                    productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l)
                                                    [by hypothesis given-equal-products]

                                                  // at(s,i) is prime
                                                  @si-prime-at-i |
                                                    is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                                    [by forall_elim(i) s-primes]
                                                  @si-prime |
                                                    less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))
                                                    [using tautology si-prime-at-i i-nonneg i-less_than-k]
                                                  @si-above-one |
                                                    less_than(ONE, at(s, i))
                                                    [by and_elim_left si-prime]

                                                  // at(s,i) divides productUpTo(s,k), hence productUpTo(t,l)
                                                  @entries-of-s-divide-2 |
                                                    forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> does_divide(at(s, i2), productUpTo(s, k))
                                                    [using specialize everyEntryDividesProduct(s, k) k-nonneg]
                                                  @si-divides-at-i |
                                                    is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> does_divide(at(s, i), productUpTo(s, k))
                                                    [by forall_elim(i) entries-of-s-divide-2]
                                                  @si-divides-prod-s |
                                                    does_divide(at(s, i), productUpTo(s, k))
                                                    [using tautology si-divides-at-i i-nonneg i-less_than-k]
                                                  @si-divides-prod-t |
                                                    does_divide(at(s, i), productUpTo(t, l))
                                                    [by rewrite equal-products si-divides-prod-s]

                                                  // crux: at(s,i) divides some entry at(t,j)
                                                  @crux |
                                                    forall kk: Int; is_nonneg(kk) -> forall ss: Seq; forall p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(ss, kk)) -> (exists i2: Int; is_nonneg(i2) and (less_than(i2, kk) and does_divide(p, at(ss, i2))))
                                                    [by cite primeDividesProductImpliesMember]
                                                  @crux-at-l |
                                                    is_nonneg(l) -> forall ss: Seq; forall p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(ss, l)) -> (exists i2: Int; is_nonneg(i2) and (less_than(i2, l) and does_divide(p, at(ss, i2))))
                                                    [by forall_elim(l) crux]
                                                  @crux-l |
                                                    forall ss: Seq; forall p: Int; (less_than(ONE, p) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, p) -> (d = ONE or d = p))) -> does_divide(p, productUpTo(ss, l)) -> (exists i2: Int; is_nonneg(i2) and (less_than(i2, l) and does_divide(p, at(ss, i2))))
                                                    [by modus_ponens crux-at-l l-nonneg]
                                                  @crux-t-si |
                                                    (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))) -> does_divide(at(s, i), productUpTo(t, l)) -> (exists i2: Int; is_nonneg(i2) and (less_than(i2, l) and does_divide(at(s, i), at(t, i2))))
                                                    [by forall_elim(t, at(s, i)) crux-l]
                                                  @member-exists |
                                                    exists i2: Int; is_nonneg(i2) and (less_than(i2, l) and does_divide(at(s, i), at(t, i2)))
                                                    [using tautology crux-t-si si-prime si-divides-prod-t]

                                                  @with-member-j |
                                                    unpack j: Int from member-exists {
                                                      @j-props |
                                                        is_nonneg(j) and (less_than(j, l) and does_divide(at(s, i), at(t, j)))
                                                        [by hypothesis with-member-j]
                                                      @j-nonneg |
                                                        is_nonneg(j)
                                                        [using tautology j-props]
                                                      @j-less_than-l |
                                                        less_than(j, l)
                                                        [using tautology j-props]
                                                      @si-divides-tj |
                                                        does_divide(at(s, i), at(t, j))
                                                        [using tautology j-props]

                                                      // at(t,j) is prime; at(s,i) | at(t,j) with at(s,i) > 1 forces at(s,i) = at(t,j)
                                                      @tj-prime-at-j |
                                                        is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))
                                                        [by forall_elim(j) t-primes]
                                                      @tj-prime |
                                                        less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))
                                                        [using tautology tj-prime-at-j j-nonneg j-less_than-l]
                                                      @tj-divisor-clause |
                                                        forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))
                                                        [by and_elim_right tj-prime]
                                                      @zero-less_than-one |
                                                        less_than(ZERO, ONE)
                                                        [by cite zeroLessThanOne]
                                                      @lt-trans |
                                                        forall a, b, c: Int; less_than(a, b) -> less_than(b, c) -> less_than(a, c)
                                                        [by cite lessThanTransitive]
                                                      @lt-trans-si |
                                                        less_than(ZERO, ONE) -> less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) -> less_than(ZERO, at(s, i))
                                                        [by forall_elim(ZERO, ONE, at(s, i)) lt-trans]
                                                      @si-above-zero |
                                                        less_than(ZERO, at(s, i))
                                                        [using tautology lt-trans-si zero-less_than-one si-above-one]
                                                      @clause-at-si |
                                                        less_than(ZERO, at(s, i)) -> does_divide(at(s, i), at(t, j)) -> (at(s, i) = ONE or at(s, i) = at(t, j))
                                                        [by forall_elim(at(s, i)) tj-divisor-clause]
                                                      @si-one-or-eq |
                                                        at(s, i) = ONE or at(s, i) = at(t, j)
                                                        [using tautology clause-at-si si-above-zero si-divides-tj]
                                                      // at(s,i) > 1 rules out at(s,i) = ONE (would give ONE < ONE, false by irreflexivity)
                                                      @not-one-less_than-one |
                                                        not less_than(ONE, ONE)
                                                        [using specialize lessThanIrreflexive(ONE)]
                                                      // case-split the disjunction: at(s,i)=ONE contradicts at(s,i)>1 (rewrite
                                                      // less_than(ONE, at(s,i)) to less_than(ONE, ONE), refuted); else at(s,i)=at(t,j).
                                                      @si-eq-tj |
                                                        at(s, i) = at(t, j)
                                                        case si-one-or-eq {
                                                          @when-si-one |
                                                            assume at(s, i) = ONE {
                                                              @si-is-one |
                                                                at(s, i) = ONE
                                                                [by hypothesis when-si-one]
                                                              @one-less_than-one |
                                                                less_than(ONE, ONE)
                                                                [by rewrite si-is-one si-above-one]
                                                              @si-eq-tj-absurd |
                                                                at(s, i) = at(t, j)
                                                                [by absurd one-less_than-one not-one-less_than-one]
                                                            }
                                                          @when-si-eq |
                                                            assume at(s, i) = at(t, j) {
                                                              @si-eq-tj-direct |
                                                                at(s, i) = at(t, j)
                                                                [by hypothesis when-si-eq]
                                                            }
                                                        }

                                                      @j-witnesses |
                                                        is_nonneg(j) and (less_than(j, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j))
                                                        [using tautology j-nonneg j-less_than-l si-eq-tj]
                                                      @found-j |
                                                        exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2))
                                                        [by exists_intro(j) j-witnesses]
                                                    }
                                                  @member-found |
                                                    exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2))
                                                    [by exists_elim with-member-j]
                                                }
                                              @less_than-k-gives |
                                                less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2)))
                                                [by implies_intro given-i-less_than-k]
                                            }
                                          @i-nonneg-gives |
                                            is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2)))
                                            [by implies_intro given-i-nonneg]
                                        }
                                      @all-i |
                                        forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2)))
                                        [by forall_intro per-index]
                                    }
                                  @products-give |
                                    productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2))))
                                    [by implies_intro given-equal-products]
                                }
                              @t-primes-give |
                                (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2))))
                                [by implies_intro given-t-primes]
                            }
                          @s-primes-give |
```
