```bpa
                            (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2))))
                            [by implies_intro given-s-primes]
                        }
                      @l-nonneg-gives |
                        is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2))))
                        [by implies_intro given-l-nonneg]
                    }
                  @k-nonneg-gives |
                    is_nonneg(k) -> is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2))))
                    [by implies_intro given-k-nonneg]
                }
              @discharge-l |
                forall l: Int; is_nonneg(k) -> is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2))))
                [by forall_intro generalize-l]
            }
          @discharge-t |
            forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(k) -> is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2))))
            [by forall_intro generalize-t]
        }
      @discharge-k |
        forall k: Int; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(k) -> is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2))))
        [by forall_intro generalize-k]
    }
  @conclusion |
    forall s: Seq; forall k: Int; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int; is_nonneg(k) -> is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (exists j2: Int; is_nonneg(j2) and (less_than(j2, l) and at(s, i) = at(t, j2))))
    [by forall_intro generalize-s]
qed
```

The capstone conjoins the two strengthenings: equal length **and** an explicit
matching bijection. Both are established theorems, cited once up front, then
specialized and applied under the shared hypotheses — the whole of Judson's
"unique: `k = l` and the `qᵢ` are, after reordering, the `pᵢ`."

```bpa
theorem ftaUniquenessFull: forall k: Int; is_nonneg(k) ->
  forall s: Seq; forall t: Seq; forall l: Int;
  is_nonneg(l)
  -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))))
  -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))))
  -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l)
  -> (k = l and (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))
proof
  @generalize-k |
    fix k: Int {
      @given-k-nonneg |
        assume is_nonneg(k) {
          @generalize-s-seq |
            fix s: Seq {
              @generalize-t-seq |
                fix t: Seq {
                  @generalize-l |
                    fix l: Int {
                      @given-l-nonneg |
                        assume is_nonneg(l) {
                          @given-s-primes |
                            assume forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i)))) {
                              @given-t-primes |
                                assume forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j)))) {
                                  @given-equal-products |
                                    assume productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) {
                                      @k-nonneg |
                                        is_nonneg(k)
                                        [by hypothesis given-k-nonneg]
                                      @l-nonneg |
                                        is_nonneg(l)
                                        [by hypothesis given-l-nonneg]
                                      @s-primes |
                                        forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))
                                        [by hypothesis given-s-primes]
                                      @t-primes |
                                        forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))
                                        [by hypothesis given-t-primes]
                                      @equal-products |
                                        productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l)
                                        [by hypothesis given-equal-products]
                                      // apply factorizationLengthUnique at all four
                                      // binders in one interleaved specialize:
                                      // k↦∀k, k-nonneg↦is_nonneg(k), then
                                      // s/t/l↦∀s,t,l and the four antecedents.
                                      @lengths-equal |
                                        k = l
                                        [using specialize factorizationLengthUnique(k, s, t, l) k-nonneg l-nonneg s-primes t-primes equal-products]
                                      @permutation-exists |
                                        (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                        [using specialize factorizationPermutationExists(k, s, t, l) k-nonneg l-nonneg s-primes t-primes equal-products]
                                      @length-and-permutation |
                                        k = l and (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i))))
                                        [by and_intro lengths-equal permutation-exists]
                                    }
                                  @products-give |
                                    productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (k = l and (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))
                                    [by implies_intro given-equal-products]
                                }
                              @t-primes-give |
                                (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (k = l and (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))
                                [by implies_intro given-t-primes]
                            }
                          @s-primes-give |
                            (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (k = l and (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))
                            [by implies_intro given-s-primes]
                        }
                      @l-nonneg-gives |
                        is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (k = l and (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))
                        [by implies_intro given-l-nonneg]
                    }
                  @discharge-l |
                    forall l: Int; is_nonneg(l) -> (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (less_than(ONE, at(s, i)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(s, i)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(s, i))))) -> (forall j: Int; is_nonneg(j) -> less_than(j, l) -> (less_than(ONE, at(t, j)) and (forall d: Int; less_than(ZERO, d) -> does_divide(d, at(t, j)) -> (d = ONE or d = at(t, j))))) -> productUpTo(s, k) = productUpTo(t, l) -> (k = l and (exists sigma: Seq; (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> (is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) and less_than(at(sigma, i), l))) and (forall i: Int; forall i2: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(i2) -> less_than(i2, k) -> at(sigma, i) = at(sigma, i2) -> i = i2) and (forall i: Int; is_nonneg(i) -> less_than(i, k) -> is_nonneg(at(sigma, i)) -> at(s, i) = at(t, at(sigma, i)))))
                    [by forall_intro generalize-l]
                }
              @discharge-t-seq |
```
